### [1] [Measures on partial orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02021)
*Andrew Snowden*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文确定了偏序集Fraïssé类上的Harman–Snowden度量空间，该空间由一条平面、八条直线和15个孤立点组成。这是首个度量空间非等维的情形，也是首个维度至少为2的原始情形。注：部分论证使用ChatGPT辅助完成，但写作完全由作者独立完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在确定偏序集Fraïssé类上的所有Harman–Snowden度量。这一问题的意义在于，它构成了首个度量空间非等维（non-equidimensional）的案例，同时也是首个度量空间维度至少为2的原始（primitive）案例，因而对理解一般Fraïssé类上的度量结构具有突破性意义。

Method: 论文通过系统分析偏序集Fraïssé类的结构，对可能的Harman–Snowden度量进行枚举和分类。得到的度量空间包含三部分：一个二维平面（连续统），八条一维直线，以及15个零维孤立点。论文还注明了部分论证过程使用了ChatGPT辅助生成。

Result: 偏序集Fraïssé类上的Harman–Snowden度量空间由一个平面、八条直线和15个孤立点的并集构成。这是首次发现度量空间不是等维的，也是首次在原始情形中观察到维度达到至少2的度量空间。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了偏序集类中度量结构的丰富性和复杂性，突破了此前已知案例中等维性的限制，为后续研究更高维度、非等维的Fraïssé类度量空间开辟了新方向。同时，作者在文中披露了使用ChatGPT辅助论证这一事实，但对写作的完全自主性作了声明。

Abstract: We determine the measures (in the sense of Harman--Snowden) on the Fraïssé class of partially ordered sets: the space of measures is a union of a plane, eight lines, and 15 isolated points. This is the first case where the space is not equidimensional, and the first primitive case in which it has dimension at least two.
  ChatGPT was used to obtain many arguments. The writing was done entirely by the author.

</details>


### [2] [A negative answer to a question on tilting objects and two-term complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02023)
*Jing He,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过一个显式的有限维例子，否定了"呈现函子将倾斜对象映为倾斜对象"的猜想。作者在路径代数Λ的导出范畴中构造了一个siltng对象M和倾斜对象T，使得呈现函子将T映为二项silting复形而非倾斜复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来有研究者提出一个开放问题：若M是幂等完备代数三角范畴中的silting对象，T是其pr(M)子范畴中的tilting对象，则与M相关的呈现函子P_M(T)是否必然是tilting的？本文旨在回答该问题（给出否定答案），并希望澄清silting/tilting理论中呈现函子的行为边界。

Method: 选取具体代数Λ = k(1→α2→β3→γ4)/(αβγ)（一条带关系0=αβγ的四顶点箭图路径代数），在其有界导出范畴K^b(proj Λ)中构造一个silting对象M，并选择T = ΣΛ作为pr(M)中的tilting对象。然后通过计算K^b(proj B)中的同态维数（其中B=End_T(M)），验证dim_k Hom(P_M(T), Σ^{-1}P_M(T)) = 1，从而证明P_M(T)不满足tilting条件。

Result: 对于所构造的例子，dim_k Hom_{K^b(proj B)}(P_M(T), Σ^{-1}P_M(T)) = 1 ≠ 0，这表明P_M(T)在K^b(proj B)中不是tilting复形（因为tilting允许的同态维数应为0），但它仍是一个两项silting复形。因此该反例否证了原问题的正向猜测。

Conclusion: 呈现函子不能保持tilting性质，即使输入T是tilting对象，输出P_M(T)也可能只是silting复形而非tilting复形。这为silting/tilting理论中呈现函子的性质划定了更精确的界限，给出对开放问题的否定解答。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a silting object in an idempotent complete algebraic triangulated category $\mathcal T$. Put $B={\rm End}_{\mathcal T}(M)$, and let $\mathbb{P}_M\colon {\rm pr}(M)\to K^{[-1,0]}({\rm proj}B)$ be the presentation functor associated with $M$. It was recently asked whether $\mathbb{P}_M(T)$ must be tilting whenever $T\in{\rm pr}(M)$ is a tilting object. We answer this question in the negative by giving an explicit finite-dimensional example. Namely, for $$ Λ=k(1\xrightarrowα2\xrightarrowβ3\xrightarrowγ4)/(αβγ),$$ we construct a silting object $M\in K^b({\rm proj}Λ)$ and a tilting object $T=ΣΛ\in{\rm pr}(M)$ for which $$ {\rm dim}_k{\rm Hom}_{K^b({\rm proj}B)}\bigl(\mathbb{P}_M(T),Σ^{-1}\mathbb{P}_M(T)\bigr)=1.$$ Thus $\mathbb{P}_M(T)$ is a two-term silting complex but not a tilting complex.

</details>


### [3] [A Serre-type presentation for $\imath$quantum supergroups of type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02176)
*Weideng Cui,Yaolong Shen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为A型量子超对称对产生的imath量子包络超代数建立了Serre型表示。先在任意类型下利用投影技巧证明量子Serre多项式作用于余理想生成元会产生严格更小权重的修正项，再对所有A型超Satake图计算这些余项，特别确定了相关局部Satake图中各向同性奇简单根对应的新的四次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: imath量子群（量子对称对的余不动点子代数）在量子群表示论中具有重要地位，但其超代数的推广——imath量子包络超代数——尚缺乏系统的Serre型表示。建立此类表示是理解其代数结构与表示理论的基础，而处理各向同性奇简单根带来的新关系是其中的关键难点。

Method: 作者采用投影技术，在任意类型中证明量子Serre多项式对余理想生成子的求值会产生一个在自然滤过下严格更小权重的修正（余项）。随后针对所有A型超Satake图系统地计算这些余项，从而推导出Serre型关系的完整形式，其中对局部Satake图中各向同性奇简单根确定了新的四次（degree-four）关系。

Result: 建立了A型imath量子包络超代数的Serre型表示；完成了所有A型超Satake图的量子Serre多项式余项计算；发现了与各向同性奇简单根相关联的新四次关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将Serre型表示推广到A型量子超对称对情形，为imath量子超代数的结构与表示理论研究提供了基础框架，并通过投影方法统一处理了任意类型中余项的结构性质。

Abstract: We establish a Serre-type presentation for $\imath$quantum enveloping superalgebras arising from quantum supersymmetric pairs of type A. We first prove, in arbitrary type, that evaluating a quantum Serre polynomial on the coideal generators produces a correction of strictly smaller weight in the natural filtration using the projection technique. We then compute these remainder terms for all type A super Satake diagrams. In particular, we determine the new degree-four relations associated with isotropic odd simple roots of the relevant local Satake diagrams.

</details>


### [4] [Algebraic study of quantum configuration spaces of decorated flags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02509)
*Tsukasa Ishibashi,Hironori Oya*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了量子化的K个装饰旗配置空间O_q(Conf_K A_G)，基于量子包络代数的表示论建立了其基本代数性质（量子规范化、量子循环移位、量子Wilson线），并利用量子Wilson线将Berenstein–Zelevinsky量子簇结构从O_q(G)传输到O_q(Conf_4 A_G)，证明了后者与相应的量子簇代数及上簇代数（在冻结变量处局部化后）一致，交换矩阵与Goncharov–Shen交换矩阵吻合。在自然猜想下，还构造了K≥5情形的量子种子，并证明相应量子簇代数包含量子配置空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置空间Conf_K A_G（K个装饰旗）是Fock–Goncharov模空间A_{G,Σ}（曲面上装饰扭曲G-局部系统）的局部基本构件，经典的量子簇代数方法在A_{G,Σ}研究中至关重要。本文的动机是将这一框架量子化：为Conf_K A_G建立量子代数体系，并构造其量子簇结构，从而为量子化A_{G,Σ}及其相关不变量理论奠定代数基础。

Method: 本文方法基于量子包络代数的表示论：首先定义量子配置空间O_q(Conf_K A_G)，建立量子规范化、量子循环移位与量子Wilson线等基本代数性质；然后通过量子Wilson线将Berenstein–Zelevinsky在O_q(G)上的量子簇结构传递到O_q(Conf_4 A_G)，得到量子种子并证明局部化后的等价性；对K≥5的情形，在自然猜想下推广构造量子种子并证明包含关系。

Result: 建立了O_q(Conf_K A_G)的基本代数性质；对O_q(Conf_4 A_G)构造了量子种子，证明其局部化后与相应量子簇代数及上簇代数一致，交换矩阵与Goncharov–Shen交换矩阵一致，且可限制到O_q(Conf_3 A_G)的量子种子；在自然猜想下对K≥5构造了量子种子，并证明相应量子簇代数包含量子配置空间。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了装饰旗量子配置空间的代数框架，并在K=4情形完整证明了其量子簇代数刻画，为量子Fock–Goncharov理论提供了代数基础。K≥5的结果依赖于一个自然猜想，表明该方向仍有待进一步突破与推广。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected, simply connected complex simple algebraic group and $\mathscr{A}_G=G/U^+$ its base affine space, whose elements are called decorated flags. We introduce the quantum configuration space of decorated flags $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G)$ and initiate its algebraic study, based on the representation theory of quantized enveloping algebras. Our algebra $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G)$ gives a quantum analogue of the configuration space $\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G$ of $K$ decorated flags, which provides local building blocks for the Fock--Goncharov moduli space $\mathscr{A}_{G,Σ}$ of decorated twisted $G$-local systems on a marked surface $Σ$.
  We establish basic algebraic properties of $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G)$ such as quantum normalization of representatives, the quantum cyclic shifts, the quantum Wilson lines, whose classical counterparts have been fundamental in the study of $\mathscr{A}_{G,Σ}$. Moreover, we construct quantum seeds for $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_4 \mathscr{A}_G)$ by transporting the Berenstein--Zelevinsky quantum cluster structure on $\mathcal{O}_q(G)$ via quantum Wilson lines, and prove that $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_4 \mathscr{A}_G)$ coincides with the corresponding quantum cluster algebra and its upper counterpart after the localization at frozen variables. The exchange matrices for our quantum seeds agree with the Goncharov--Shen exchange matrices. We also show that quantum seeds for $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_4 \mathscr{A}_G)$ restrict to those for $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_3 \mathscr{A}_G)$.
  Finally, up to a natural conjecture, we construct quantum seeds for $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G)$, $K\geq 5$, and prove that the corresponding quantum cluster algebras contain the quantum configuration space $\mathcal{O}_q(\mathrm{Conf}_K \mathscr{A}_G)$.

</details>


### [5] [On the representation type of higher preprojective algebras of type $A$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02597)
*Weikang Weng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(n+1)$-预投射代数（type $A_m$）的表示类型，建立了有限-驯顺-野的三分法，并给出了该类代数关于$τ$-倾斜有限性的完全分类；此外，对于$n\ge 2$，刻画了每个不可分解模何时为$τ$-刚性模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $(n+1)$-预投射代数是经典预投射代数的高维推广，其表示类型与$τ$-倾斜理论性质是表示论中的重要问题。对此类代数建立有限-驯顺-野三分法并分类$τ$-倾斜有限性，有助于深入理解这类代数的表示结构。

Method: 通过分析type $A_m$的$(n+1)$-预投射代数的结构，利用表示维数、$τ$-倾斜理论以及不可分解模的$τ$-刚性条件进行分类研究，分别建立表示类型的三分法以及对$τ$-倾斜有限性的完整刻画，并针对$n\ge 2$给出不可分解模为$τ$-刚性的精确判据。

Result: 建立了type $A_m$的$(n+1)$-预投射代数关于表示类型的有限-驯顺-野三分法，给出了该类代数$τ$-倾斜有限性的完整分类，并在$n\ge 2$情形下精确刻画了每个不可分解模成为$τ$-刚性模的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该文为type $A_m$的$(n+1)$-预投射代数的表示类型和$τ$-倾斜性质提供了完整的理论刻画，弥补了此前对该类代数在这些方面缺乏系统性结果的研究空白。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a finite--tame--wild trichotomy for the representation type of $(n+1)$-preprojective algebras of type $A_m$. We also provide a complete classification of such algebras with respect to $τ$-tilting finiteness. Moreover, for $n\ge 2$, we give a precise characterization of when each indecomposable module is $τ$-rigid.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum wreath products and Yang-Baxter equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02765)
*Alexandre Minets*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将Lai-Nakano-Xiang的量子花环积代数推广到对称幺半范畴和变形辫子关系，并在温和假设下大幅简化了此类代数具有PBW基的判定准则——新准则以结合方程和量子Yang-Baxter方程表述。作为应用，作者简化了文献中已有的基结果，并提出了箭图Hecke代数的一类推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子花环积代数是联结量子群表示论、Hecke代数与花环积结构的重要工具。作者希望将该构造推广到更一般的范畴设定（对称幺半范畴）及更广泛的辫子关系（变形辫子关系），同时改进原有的PBW基判定准则——原准则可能过于复杂或限制性过强，有必要寻找更简洁、更具普适性的判据。

Method: 作者在对称幺半范畴和变形辫子关系的框架下重新构建量子花环积代数，并在温和假设下给出新的PBW基存在性准则。该准则的关键创新在于将其化简为两个基础方程的验证：结合方程（associative Yang-Baxter equation）与量子Yang-Baxter方程。

Result: 得到了一个远比原有准则简化的、以结合方程和量子Yang-Baxter方程表述的PBW基判定条件；利用这一新准则清理和统一了既有文献中关于基的结果，并构造了箭图Hecke代数的一个推广框架。

Conclusion: 本文在更广的范畴与辫子关系设定下建立了量子花环积代数的统一理论，发现了PBW基与经典代数方程（结合Yang-Baxter方程）之间的深刻联系，为后续推广和应用提供了简洁而有力的工具。

Abstract: We extend the setup of Lai-Nakano-Xiang's quantum wreath product algebras to symmetric monoidal categories and deformed braid relations. Under some mild assumptions, we drastically simplify the criterion for such algebras to have PBW basis; curiously, the new criterion is phrased in terms of associative and quantum Yang-Baxter equations. As an application, we clean up some basis results in the existing literature, and propose a certain generalization of quiver Hecke algebras.

</details>

### [7] [Measuring Small-scale Turbulence in Supernova Remnants with Jitter Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01715)
*Emily Simon,Damiano Caprioli,Niccolò Bucciantini,Emanuele Greco,Marco Miceli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出仙后座A（Cas A）硬X射线发射源于抖动辐射（jitter radiation），意味着磁湍流存在于离子拉莫尔半径或更小的尺度。作者提出这些磁场由激波下游的离子尺度微观不稳定性（如mirror和firehose）产生，并通过高分辨率混合模拟证明了这种小尺度湍流的产生。在考虑数值耗散后，其谱指数与Cas A推断结果一致，表明抖动辐射是超新星遗迹中离子尺度湍流的直接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Cas A硬X射线发射的物理机制，并解释其中磁湍流在离子拉莫尔半径或更小尺度上存在的起源问题。

Method: 使用高分辨率混合模拟（hybrid simulations）研究激波下游由离子尺度微观不稳定性（mirror和firehose不稳定性）产生的磁湍流，并与Cas A观测推断的谱指数进行比较。

Result: 混合模拟成功演示了离子尺度小尺度磁湍流的产生；在考虑数值耗散后，其谱指数与Cas A所推断的抖动辐射谱指数一致。

Conclusion: Cas A硬X射线的抖动辐射可归因于激波下游离子尺度微观不稳定性产生的磁湍流，抖动辐射因此可作为超新星遗迹中离子尺度湍流的直接探测手段。

Abstract: Hard X-ray emission from Cas A has been attributed to jitter radiation, implying magnetic turbulence on scales near or below the ion Larmor radius. We propose these fields arise from ion-scale micro-instabilities (e.g., mirror and firehose) in the shock downstream, and use high-resolution hybrid simulations to demonstrate the generation of such small-scale turbulence. Its spectral index, once numerical dissipation is accounted for, is consistent with that inferred for Cas A, suggesting that jitter radiation is a direct probe of ion-scale turbulence in supernova remnants.

</details>


### [8] [The Mass-Ratio Distribution of the Low-Mass Binary Black Hole Subpopulation: A Natural Outcome of Isolated Binary Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01784)
*Monica Gallegos-Garcia,Anarya Ray,Vicky Kalogera,Max Briel,Michael Zevin,Abhishek Chattaraj,Zepei Xing,Jeff J. Andrews,Seth Gossage,Philipp M. Srivastava*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用POSYDON二元种群合成代码，研究了孤立双星演化产生的黑洞并合质量比(q)分布，发现其与引力波观测到的低质量子总体的q分布一致。模型中的q分布呈现非对称峰值(q≈0.5-0.7)和近等质量成分两个特征，分别对应于不同的物理形成亚通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的并合黑洞双星群体越来越多地被认为是由不同形成机制的亚总体混合而成，其中低质量亚总体（主黑洞质量≤15M⊙）被认为源自孤立双星演化。本研究旨在系统探讨孤立双星演化能否自然产生观测到的低质量子总体的q分布特征。

Method: 使用POSYDON二元种群合成代码模拟孤立双星演化产生的黑洞并合，系统探索超新星残骸处理方案、公共包层效率和黑洞吸积效率的变化，并对比模拟产生的q分布与引力波观测到的低质量子总体数据。

Result: 模型倾向于产生非对称的q分布，大部分在q≈0.5-0.7处有宽峰，同时存在近等质量成分且其相对强度随模型变化。该q分布与引力波观测的低质量子总体一致。非对称峰来自公共包层和稳定质量转移系统，而近等质量成分可追溯至双核公共包层和接触系统。

Conclusion: 观测到的低质量黑洞并合子总体的q分布特征可自然地从孤立双星演化中产生，且对多种模型假设具有稳健性。随着引力波目录的增长，q分布特征的相对强度将成为诊断孤立双星演化及其形成亚通道的有力工具。

Abstract: The observed merging binary black hole population is increasingly consistent with being composed of a mixture of subpopulations, each likely the result of different formation mechanisms. In particular, the low-mass subpopulation, with primary black hole masses below $\approxeq 15\,M_{\odot}$, has been attributed to mergers formed through isolated binary evolution. We use the binary population synthesis code POSYDON to study the mass ratio $(q)$ distribution of binary black hole mergers from isolated binary evolution and compare to the observed low-mass subpopulation. We explore variations in supernova remnant prescriptions, common-envelope efficiency, and black hole accretion efficiency. We find that our models have a preference for asymmetric $q$, most with a broad peak near $q\approxeq0.5-0.7$, and a near-equal-mass component whose relative strength varies across models. This resultant $q$ distribution is consistent with the $q$ distribution of the low-mass subpopulation observed with gravitational waves. Method: 结合AstroSat/LAXPC20功率密度谱分析（检测QPO）、宽带X射线光谱反射建模（约束吸积盘倾角、电离度及温�度）、MeerKAT射电监测（追踪射电变与X射线演化的关联），以及准同时射电/X射线相关分析（检验与已知银河系黑洞X射线双星的L_X-L_R关系）。

Conclusion: Swift J151857.0-572147在2024年爆发期间表现为软/中等谱态，其吸积盘高度电离且倾角适中，射电与X射线行为的紧密关联表明吸积流与喷流存在强耦合，而其在L_X-L_R平面的位置证实其确为银河系黑洞X射线双星。

Abstract: We investigate multiwavelength timing and spectral properties of the newly discovered black hole X-ray binary Swift J151857.0-572147 during its 2024 outburst using observations from AstroSat, NuSTAR, NICER, Swift, and MeerKAT. The AstroSat/LAXPC20 power density spectrum reveals a $\sim$8 Hz low-frequency QPO with a quality factor of $\sim2.8$. The broadband X-ray spectrum of the source exhibits prominent reflection features, which are modeled to constrain the geometry and physical properties of the accretion disk. The spectral analysis indicates that the source was in the soft/intermediate spectral state, characterized by a steep power law with a photon index of $Γ\approx 2.6$ and a relatively high disk temperature of $\sim0.9$ keV. The reflection modeling suggests a disk inclination of $23-31^{\circ}$ and a highly ionized accretion disk with $\logξ\sim 3.3$-4.0. The MeerKAT monitoring revealed a strong radio variability that closely tracked the hard X-ray evolution, including a bright radio flare during the hard-to-soft state transition, supporting close coupling between the accretion flow and jet activity. Using the quasi-simultaneous AstroSat, Swift/XRT and MeerKAT observations, we investigated the radio/X-ray correlation and found that Swift J151857.0-572147 follows the established $L_{\rm X}$-$L_{\rm R}$ relation for Galactic black hole X-ray binaries. Its location in the $L_{\rm X}$-$L_{\rm R}$ plane is consistent with that of Galactic black hole X-ray binaries, further supporting its black hole nature.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文对Blandford–Znajek机制进行了突破性重审：将喷流自身磁场纳入引力作用，证明在广义相对论自洽框架下稳态喷流不可能存在，并推导出喷流功率的宇宙学上限、黑洞寿命输出等精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去五十年来，Blandford–Znajek机制只是一个"在固定度规背景上施加受力自由电动力学"的近似机制，喷流自带的能量-动量（磁场）并不参与引力场方程的求解。这种"test-field"框架是自相矛盾的——喷流携带的能量实际上足够改变时空几何。作者希望建立喷流场自身参与引力作用的自洽解，从而揭示测试场近似无法捕捉的物理。

Method: 作者将分裂单极子（split monopole）磁场升格为真实的引力源。由于分裂单极子的自重等效于一个磁荷，其自引力几何就是磁Reissner–Nordström解。将两个这样的解沿赤道电流片粘合，得到一个包含黑洞、吸积盘和喷流驱动磁力线的统一时空，并利用受力自由电动力学等离子体构建其磁层。在此基础上，作者从场方程而不是人为假设出发，研究该系统的稳态性、极端条件束和演化动力学。

Result: 得出三个标志性结论：(1) 稳态喷流不可能存在——静止视界无法被加热，但滑移磁层必然加热视界，因此唯一的稳态是随黑洞一起转动的"死"黑洞，工作喷流意味着黑洞处于衰变之中（衰变速率由场方程决定）。(2) 磁通量以电荷的身份进入黑洞力学定律，与自旋共享同一极端性预算，该预算将喷流功率限制在c^5/48G，与质量无关。(3) 寿命总输出有限：最大自旋黑洞释放其质量的9.2%（1-e^{1/4}/√2），视界面积精确增长√e倍；视界自身的转动惯量以无理指数(√17-1)/2消失（由近视界喉道决定），最终留下一个自旋角动量已转移至自身场中的旋转黑洞。

Conclusion: 喷流能量动量必须参与引力动力学，这不仅改变了喷流的物理图像（从"机制"升格为"解"），还揭示出测试场近似在本质上的局限：稳态喷流、极端性上限和精确的可观测输出都是场论自洽性带来的新物理。

Abstract: For fifty years the Blandford--Znajek mechanism has been a mechanism and not an exact solution: force-free electrodynamics on a prescribed metric, with the jet's own field carrying no weight. Here that field gravitates. A split monopole weighs as much as a magnetic monopole, so its self-gravity is the magnetic Reissner--Nordström geometry; two copies glued across an equatorial current sheet put hole, disk and jet-driving flux into one spacetime, and force-free plasma makes a magnetosphere of it. Three things then follow that no test-field calculation can see. First, a steady jet is impossible. A stationary horizon cannot be heated but a slipping magnetosphere necessarily heats it, so the only stationary state is a dead one corotating with the hole, and a working jet is a black hole in decay at rates the field equations fix rather than assume. Second, flux enters the laws of black-hole mechanics as a charge, sharing one extremality budget with spin. That budget caps the jet power at $c^5/48G$ whatever the mass. Third, the lifetime output is finite: a maximally spinning hole delivers $1-e^{1/4}/\sqrt2=9.2\%$ of its mass and grows its horizon area by exactly $\sqrt e$. The horizon's own moment of inertia vanishes with the irrational exponent $(\sqrt{17}-1)/2$ set by the near-horizon throat, and what is left is a rotating hole whose angular momentum has passed to its own field.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星是极端物理的天然实验室，具有极强磁场，对其短暴进行系统、长期的监测和编目对于理解其爆发机制和物理过程至关重要。现有的GBM磁星目录（Collazzi et al. 2015）仅覆盖5年数据，需要更新以利用GBM长达17年的完整观测数据。

Method: 利用Fermi GBM长达17年的观测数据，系统整理和分析了来自17个银河系源的1254次SGR短暴，测量了爆发持续时间、多种光子模型下的谱参数、通量等物理量，并与近期其他任务的目录及先前GBM磁星目录进行了比较。

Result: 新目录包含1254次SGR短暴（覆盖17年），来自17个独特银河系源，其中主要贡献来自SGR J1935+2154和SGR J1550-5418。提供了爆发持续时间、光谱参数、通量等整体特征，并与先前目录及其他任务的目录进行了系统比较。所有目录数据、光谱和响应文件已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该目录是目前时间跨度最长、不间断、高灵敏度的全天磁星爆发监测数据，具有前所未有的光谱和时间分辨率，为社区提供了重要的公共资源，有助于深化对磁星爆发物理的理解。

Abstract: The Fermi Gamma-ray Burst Monitor (GBM) has been in operation for over 17 years, during which it has observed more than a thousand bursts from soft gamma repeaters (SGRs), also known as magnetars. Serving as a laboratory for extreme physics, magnetars are a sub-family of neutron stars characterized by extreme magnetic field strength, observed through a combination of persistent and short transient emission across the electromagnetic spectrum. We present the comprehensive GBM catalog of SGR short bursts which supersedes the 5-year catalog of Collazzi et al. 2015. The new catalog contains 1254 SGR short bursts observed over 17 years, providing the longest uninterrupted, high-sensitivity all-sky monitoring of magnetar bursts with unprecedented spectral and temporal resolution. Our catalog contains bursts from 17 unique Galactic sources, with major contributions by bursts from SGR J1935+2154 and SGR J1550-5418. We present overall characteristics of these bursts, such as the durations, spectral parameters for various photon models, fluxes, as well as their comparison with recently published catalogs of other missions and the previous GBM magnetar catalog. The machine readable catalog, as well as burst spectra and response files are made publicly available for the community.

</details>

Abstract: We report a day-scale hard lag between GeV and TeV $γ$-ray emission from the HBL 1ES~1959+650 in early 2024. Since the LHAASO-WCDA real-time monitoring system began operation in late 2023, multiple TeV flares from this source have been triggered, including the 1st trigger flare on 2024 February 9. A Bayesian-block analysis of the WCDA light curve identifies three TeV flares in 2024. For the second triggered flare, a discrete cross-correlation analysis reveals a $>3\,σ$ correlation (relative to uncorrelated red-noise simulations) at a time delay of $Δt = 5.0_{-2.1}^{+2.1}$ days, with the TeV emission lagging the GeV. Time-resolved spectroscopy shows that this flare has the softest TeV spectrum among these flares (intrinsic spectral index $Γ=3.16\pm0.18$), while the 1st trigger flare is harder ($Γ=2.48\pm0.21$). The observed five-day hard lag is difficult to reconcile with a purely cooling-driven temporal ordering and is consistent with scenarios in which particle energization and/or transport may contribute to the evolution. However, the current data do not uniquely identify the underlying mechanism.

</details>


### [19] [Vortex pinning and the elastic response of neutron-star crusts II. Non-axisymmetric loading and Magnus mountains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02863)
*Elia Giliberti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子星外壳中钉扎的超流体涡旋通过Magnus力传递到星壳上，从而支持非轴对称的持久质量四极矩（"Magnus山"）。对SLy4和BSk21状态方程的1.4太阳质量球状星体，在100 Hz下计算l=m=2响应，得到最大椭率约为2.44-2.67×10^-9。结果表明山体对剪切模量的微观物理不敏感（仅差异百分之几），而主要不确定性来自深部内壳的压缩响应，且计算值比Gangwar & Jones (2026)的圆柱模型低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中子星中钉扎超流体涡旋如何通过Magnus力支持持久的非轴对称质量四极矩（即"山脉"），因为这种结构可作为连续引力波源，是探测中子星内部物态方程和超流性质的重要途径。此前计算强烈依赖剪切模量的微观模型和状态方程选择，本文旨在系统评估这些依赖性的真实程度，并确定模型的主要不确定来源。

Method: 采用自引力、径向分层球状恒星模型，分别使用SLy4和BSk21状态方程背景，计算l=m=2的响应。使用局部微观钉扎上限和依赖于密度的Coulomb晶格剪切模量。分析了密度分辨的压缩核函数，并比较不同剪切模型（mu=0.01P与Coulomb剖面）和边界层变化的影响。固定组分计算仅作为非弛豫灵敏度诊断手段。

Result: 对于1.4太阳质量、100 Hz的恒星，平衡压缩最大值为SLy4的2.67×10^-9和BSk21的2.44×10^-9。全局四极矩对剪切处方高度不敏感（仅百分比级别差异），主要内部敏感性来自压缩效应。密度分辨压缩核函数在内壳中增大，并在壳-芯界面附近急剧变陡（特别是BSk21），表明深部内壳控制状态方程依赖性。在共同速度基准下，本模型椭率比Gangwar & Jones (2026)的互补两分量圆柱计算低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该结果是物理性而非数值性的：Magnus山的尺度对剪切微观物理具有鲁棒性，而深壳压缩响应则构成单位移模型的主要不确定性。文中指出引入相对超流体-晶格自由度作为共同球模型下一步发展的自然方向。

Abstract: Pinned superfluid vortices transmit a Magnus force to the neutron-star crust. A non-axisymmetric component of the superfluid-lattice lag can therefore support a persistent mass quadrupole. We calculate the l=m=2 response of self-gravitating, radially stratified spherical stars with SLy4 and BSk21 backgrounds, using a local microscopic pinning cap and a density-dependent Coulomb-lattice shear modulus. For a 1.4 M_sun star at 100 Hz, the equilibrium-compression maxima are epsilon=2.67x10^-9 (SLy4) and 2.44x10^-9 (BSk21). The global quadrupole is strikingly insensitive to the shear prescription: replacing mu=0.01P by the Coulomb profile and strongly varying uncertain edge layers changes the result only at the percent level. The dominant internal sensitivity is instead compressional. A density-resolved compressional kernel increases through the inner crust and steepens close to the crust-core interface, particularly for BSk21, showing that the deep inner crust controls the EoS dependence of the mountain. A fixed-composition calculation is used only as a non-relaxed sensitivity diagnostic, not as the secular prediction. At a common true local velocity benchmark, our fiducial ellipticities remain more than an order of magnitude below the complementary two-component cylindrical calculation of Gangwar & Jones (2026). We argue that the comparison points to the relative superfluid-lattice degree of freedom as the natural next ingredient for a common spherical model. The main result is therefore physical rather than numerical: the Magnus-mountain scale is robust to shear microphysics, while the deep-crust compressional response sets the leading uncertainty of the one-displacement model.

</details>


### [20] [Magnetized accretion onto rapidly spinning binary black holes: mini-disk thermodynamics, magnetic transport, and dual jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02867)
*Luciano Combi,Manuela Campanelli,Sean M. Ressler,Alexander J. Dittmann,Federico Cattorini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，研究了一个分离为30个引力半径、无因次自旋χ=0.9的等质量双黑洞系统在磁化环双星盘中的吸积过程，发现该系统能发射能量提取效率约40%的双喷流，且喷流呈on-off交替状态，并探讨了mini-disk热力学对周期性和喷流光度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大质量双黑洞嵌入富气体环境是脉冲星计时阵列和未来空间引力波干涉仪的重要多信使源。其电磁辐射受双黑洞周围非线性等离子体动力学主导，预期携带轨道运动相关的变异性。由于活动星系核本质上具有随机性，识别稳健的双黑洞特征需要能连接大尺度环双星流与黑洞的预测模型。然而以往相对论模拟多集中于较小分离和较低自旋，缺乏大分离、高自旋情况下的研究。

Method: 采用三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，针对分离为30个引力半径、无因次自旋χ=0.9的等质量双黑洞系统，模拟了弛豫的磁化环双星盘吸积过程；同时对比了腔内mini-disk的高效冷却与低效冷却两种热力学方案。

Result: 在该分离尺度下，mini-disk是持续的质量储库，但表现出由偏心环双星盘和mini-disk间的sloshing气流驱动的显著振幅调制。磁通从环双星盘输运至事件视界，发射能量提取效率高达约40%的强大双喷流。视界穿过的磁通和喷流光度在两个黑洞间交替变化，形成on-off双喷流状态。宽喷流漏斗在双黑洞上方相互作用形成持续的有利于磁重联的电流片。冷却效率较低（更热）的mini-disk质量更小，相干周期性更弱，喷流光度更低，尽管视界磁通相当。

Conclusion: 大分离双黑洞吸积系统能高效提取旋转能量并产生显著的双喷流结构，其on-off喷流交替和电流片结构可用于多信使观测识别；mini-disk热力学状态对系统周期性特征和喷流发射具有重要调控作用，为未来多信使观测提供了关键的理论预测约束。

Abstract: Supermassive binary black holes embedded in gas-rich environments are promising multi-messenger sources for pulsar timing arrays and future space-borne gravitational-wave interferometers. Their electromagnetic emission is governed by nonlinear plasma dynamics around the binary and is expected to inherit variability associated with the orbital motion thereof. Because active galactic nuclei are intrinsically stochastic, identifying robust binary signatures requires predictive models that connect large-scale circumbinary flows to the black holes. Previous relativistic simulations of accreting binaries have mostly focused on smaller separations and lower spins. We perform three-dimensional general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations of a relaxed, magnetized circumbinary disk accreting onto an equal-mass binary black hole with a separation of $30$ gravitational radii and dimensionless spin $χ=0.9$. At these separations, the mini-disks around each black hole are persistent mass reservoirs but still show pronounced amplitude modulations governed by the eccentric circumbinary disk and the sloshing gas between the mini-disks. We analyze how the magnetic flux is transported from the circumbinary disk to the horizons, launching powerful dual jets with energy extraction efficiencies reaching $\approx 40\%$. The horizon-threading flux and jet luminosity alternate between the two black holes, producing an on-off dual-jet state. The wide jet funnels interact above the binary and form a persistent current sheet favorable to reconnection. We explore the influence of mini-disk thermodynamics, comparing efficient and inefficient cooling inside the cavity. Hotter mini-disks are less massive, exhibit weaker coherent periodicity, and launch less luminous jets despite comparable horizon-threading magnetic flux.

</details>

### [21] [Not All Agreement Counts as Corroboration: Provenance-Conserving Multi-View Fusion for Typed Action Admission in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01662)
*Zekai Jin,Hanrong Zhang,Yihong Tang,Fei Hu,Zhen Dong,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT将证据可数性作为关系变量，用于保真溯源融合与类型化动作准入，区分计算性多重复制与证据性多重复制，仅在溯源允许分离累积时才能将一致性视为佐证。该方法在31,200次评估中达到0.0861的归一化风险覆盖率面积，并在离线人机协作实验中无观察到参考不一致的动作准入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具身系统而言，预测一致性本身不足以决定证据是否应触发动作，证据的来源属性至关重要。对同一观测的重复推断可以倍增一致性但不增加证据量，而源局部数值无法揭示输出是否具有可分别计数的来源。

Method: PACT将证据可数性建模为关系变量，通过提供的溯源划分（provenance partition）定义可计数的单元。它保留每个单元内坐标上的共享支持值，仅跨单元累积证据，并将未满足的释放条件映射为hold/confirm/fallback三种类型化动作。在数学上，author证明坐标方向的meet结构是满足单例保真与插入非放大的最大预算，并具有粗化单调性与固定划分稳定性。

Result: 在48个场景簇共31,200次评估中，PACT达到0.0861的常见支持归一化风险覆盖率面积（ncsAURC）。排除对抗性一致性构建臂后，溯源划分聚合相对于单例聚合将ncsAURC降低0.0557，且佐证对比效应消失。在完整来源记录上，原生分数偏好PACT，但公共后验峰值分数缩小了其与嵌套Dirichlet的差异并偏好乘积融合。在离线人机协作中，8倍相机内重复使每检查点720个类型化响应保持不变；按相机分组的PACT在47/57的Qwen3-VL-32B参考一致候选中全部被准入，60个回合中未观察到参考不一致准入。

Conclusion: PACT成功分离了计算性多重性与证据性多重性的概念：只有当溯源允许分离累积时，一致性才构成佐证。该工作表明证据的可数性不能由源局部数值识别，需要显式的溯源关系建模来确保具身系统的可靠动作准入。

Abstract: For embodied systems, predictive agreement alone does not determine whether evidence warrants action; evidential origin matters. Repeated inference over one observation can multiply agreement without adding evidence, while source-local values do not reveal whether outputs have separately countable origins. PACT treats evidence countability as a relational variable for provenance-conserving fusion and typed action admission. A supplied provenance partition defines countable units. PACT retains coordinatewise support shared within each unit, accumulates only across units, and maps unmet release conditions to hold, confirm, or fallback. Under the stated assumptions, source-local values cannot identify countability; the coordinatewise meet is the greatest budget satisfying singleton fidelity and insertion non-amplification, with coarsening monotonicity and fixed-partition stability. Across 31,200 evaluations in 48 scene clusters, PACT attains a common-support normalized risk-coverage area (ncsAURC) of 0.0861. Excluding the constructed adversarial-consensus arm, provenance-partition aggregation reduces ncsAURC by 0.0557 relative to singleton aggregation, while the corroboration contrast vanishes. On complete-source records, native scores favor PACT, but a common posterior-peak score narrows its difference from nested Dirichlet and favors product fusion. Reassigning provenance over unchanged predictions moves evidence budgets as predicted. In offline human-robot collaboration, eightfold within-camera duplication leaves 720 typed responses per checkpoint unchanged; camera-grouped PACT admits 47 of 57 Qwen3-VL-32B reference-consistent candidates with no observed reference-inconsistent admission in 60 episodes. PACT separates computational from evidential multiplicity: agreement constitutes corroboration only when provenance permits separate accumulation.

</details>


### [22] [TriSAR: Task Coordination and Collision Avoidance for Aerial Robot Teams in Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01731)
*Aditya Anil Kapile,Pedro Machado,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在基于物理的Gazebo地震灾区仿真环境中，以2x2析因设计评估五种UAV协同系统TriSAR，比较遗传算法与贪心适应度分配两种任务分配策略在启用/禁用反应式避碰下的表现。结果表明，反应式斥力无论在何种分配策略下都能显著消除碰撞阈值违规（贪心：r=0.97；GA：r=0.50），而GA任务分配仅在禁用斥力时才在步数、路径长度和能量上优于贪心分配（|g|介于0.92-1.76之间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多UAV灾难响应系统需要协调的任务分配与局部轨迹控制，但现有研究缺乏在受控实验条件下对这两层协调各自的及联合贡献（任务效率与操作安全）的充分表征。

Method: 采用TriSAR五UAV系统，在物理仿真环境Gazebo中模拟地震受损城市。使用2x2析因设计：两种任务分配策略（遗传算法GA与贪心适应度分配）×反应式避碰启用/禁用，共四个配置，每配置运行30个随机情节（5架UAV、8个目标）。使用Mann-Whitney检验和Welch t检验分别分析碰撞与效率指标。

Result: 贪心分配下启用斥力完全消除碰撞阈值违规（U=885, p=4.03x10^-12, r=0.97）；GA分配下同样显著（U=675, p=1.26x10^-5, r=0.50）。效率指标方面：启用斥力时GA相对贪心无统计显著优势，但禁用斥力时GA在步数、路径长度和能量上显著更优（|g|=0.92-1.76）。

Conclusion: 反应式斥力提供了显著且依赖分配策略的安全增益；而GA任务分配额外的计算复杂度仅在斥力禁用时才能转化为可检测的任务效率优势。

Abstract: Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) disaster-response systems require coordinated task assignment and local trajectory control, yet the individual and combined contributions of these coordination layers to mission efficiency and operational safety remain insufficiently characterised under controlled experimental conditions. TriSAR is evaluated as a five-UAV coordination system operating in a physics-based Gazebo simulation of an earthquake-damaged urban environment. A 2 x 2 factorial design compares two task-allocation strategies (Genetic Algorithm and greedy fitness-based allocation) with reactive collision avoidance enabled or disabled. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 stochastic episodes in a common scenario of five UAVs and eight targets. Under greedy allocation, enabling repulsion eliminated recorded collision-threshold violations, confirmed by a Mann-Whitney test (U = 885, p = 4.03 x 10^-12, rank-biserial r = 0.97). Under GA allocation, the same protective effect was confirmed (U = 675, p = 1.26 x 10^-5, rank-biserial r = 0.50). For mission-efficiency metrics, GA-based allocation showed no statistically detectable advantage over greedy allocation when repulsion was enabled, but a significant advantage in steps, path length, and energy when repulsion was disabled (Welch's t-tests, |g| between 0.92 and 1.76). These results show that reactive repulsion provides a substantial, allocation-dependent safety benefit, while the additional computational complexity of GA-based task allocation yields a detectable mission-efficiency benefit only when repulsion is disabled.

</details>


### [23] [Designing Versatile Samples for Learned Trajectory Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01799)
*Yaguang Li,Jiaru Zhang,Chuheng Wei,Can Cui,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种更富含信息量的训练数据集来提升端到端驾驶策略中轨迹评分器的性能。通过沿横向（朝向可行驶边界）和纵向（朝向领航车辆）两个轴扰动人类记录的轨迹，生成更有效的正负样本，使评分器在决策边界附近获得更充分的监督。将基于Transformer的评分器附着于两个冻结的生成式规划器（DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser），在NAVSIM navtrain数据集上训练，分别达到90.1和90.4的EPDMS分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端驾驶策略通过生成候选轨迹池并选出最优轨迹，评分器可以被单独重训练，但强规划器往往将候选轨迹集中在安全模式附近，导致评分器在决策边界附近的监督信息不足，限制了其判别能力的提升。

Method: 设计了两个轨迹扰动生成器：一个沿横向方向将记录的人类轨迹朝可行驶边界扰动，另一个沿纵向方向朝前方领航车辆扰动，以此构造比基础规划器候选池更具信息量的正负样本对。将基于Transformer的评分器附加到两个冻结的生成式规划器上，并在NAVSIM navtrain数据集上训练。

Result: 在DiffusionDrive上达到90.1 EPDMS，在MeanFuser上达到90.4 EPDMS（使用ResNet-34作为骨干网络），与该训练数据集相比分别带来0.4和0.3 EPDMS的提升。

Conclusion: 通过在轨迹空间两个关键位移轴上进行定向扰动构造训练数据，能够为评分器提供更丰富的决策边界监督，从而有效提升基于冻结规划器的端到端驾驶评分性能。

Abstract: Many current end-to-end driving policies emit a pool of candidate trajectories and select one, which makes selection a separable component: a scorer can be retrained while the planner, its backbone, and its trajectory generator all stay frozen. However, many strong planners concentrate their proposals around safe mode, providing limited supervision near decision boundaries. In this work, we design a training dataset that provides more informative supervision for the scorer. In particular, we construct two generators that perturb the logged human trajectory along the two axes a vehicle can be displaced: laterally toward the drivable boundary and longitudinally toward a leading vehicle. The designed dataset produces more informative positive and negative samples than the base planner's proposal pool. We attach a transformer-based scorer to two frozen generative planners, DiffusionDrive and MeanFuser, and train it on the NAVSIM navtrain dataset. The results of the experiments show that we achieve 90.1 EPDMS on DiffusionDrive and 90.4 EPDMS on MeanFuser when using ResNet-34, with 0.4 and 0.3 EPDMS respectively, from the designed training dataset.

</details>


### [24] [One Demonstration, Many Objects: Generalizing Manipulation via Local Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01938)
*Satvik Sharma,Samrat Sahoo,Huang Huang,Fei-Fei Li Jiajun Wu,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoMimic是一种基于人类演示的灵巧操作策略，通过聚焦物体接触点附近的局部几何信息，利用接触中心化的奖励机制提升精确接触与sim-to-real一致性，成功在16个物体、4个任务和2种机械手平台上实现71%的真实世界成功率，且sim-to-real性能下降最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手的灵巧操作需要大规模数据，但采集此类数据困难重重；虽然从人类演示学习中获得的先验知识已成为扩展方案，但现有的sim-to-real强化学习方法要么缺乏明确激励精确接触的奖励导致真实世界表现弱，要么对未见物体泛化能力差。

Method: 提出DemoMimic策略，其核心思想是聚焦物体与接触点局部的几何结构来操控物体。方法包含：1) 引入接触中心化的奖励函数，明确激励精确接触行为；2) 通过改善sim-to-real一致性，使单一真实世界策略能够在保持局部接触结构的前提下，跨不同形状、尺度、质量及摩擦系数的物体迁移。

Result: 真实世界消融实验表明，DemoMimic在16个物体、4个任务和2种机械手构型上实现了71%的成功率，相较于基线方法具有最小的sim-to-real性能降级。

Conclusion: DemoMimic验证了接触局部几何建模与接触中心化奖励设计的有效性，为灵巧操作提供了跨物体泛化的可行方案，显著缩小了仿真到真实世界的性能差距。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered robot hands promises human-level dexterity, but collecting large-scale dexterous robot hand data remains difficult. Learning from human demonstrations has emerged as a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation, providing strong priors on object interaction and contact strategies. Recent sim-to-real RL methods incorporate such priors, but often (i) omit rewards that explicitly incentivize precise contact, yielding weak real-world performance, and/or (ii) generalize poorly to unseen object instances. We propose DemoMimic (Dexterous Motion Mimic), a policy that manipulates objects by focusing on their geometry local to the contact points. Its contact-centric rewards encourage precise contact and improve sim-to-real consistency, yielding a single real-world policy that transfers across objects of varying shape, scale, mass, and friction wherever local contact structure is preserved. Real-world ablations show that DemoMimic achieves 71% success across 16 objects, four tasks, and two robot-hand embodiments, with the smallest sim-to-real drop compared to baselines.

</details>


### [25] [MACAW: Reliable And Efficient Surgical Debridement Using Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01961)
*Ziyang Chen,Shutong Jin,Preethi Satish,Sareena Mann,Cael Magner,Danyal Fer,Omid Mohareri,Gary Guthart,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于手术清创的增强灵巧系统，结合视觉伺服实现图像平面内的抓钳对齐，并提出名为MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意力窗口）的新型深度控制方法。在dVRK机器人上进行100次物理试验，相机框架伺服将平均抓钳位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内（平均仅需0.39秒）。MACAW显著优于过程式和学习的VLA基线，在单臂设置下达93%成功率、每块碎片11秒，吞吐量304块/小时；扩展到双臂清创后保持92%成功率、每块7秒，吞吐量提升至473块/小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强外科医生的灵巧性有望将其从繁琐的子任务中解放出来。清创手术（切除病变或坏死组织碎片）因空间感知不精确和缆线驱动的不确定性而具有挑战性，现有方法难以可靠地完成精细清创操作，因此需要开发一种能够提升清创灵巧性的自动化辅助系统。

Method: 系统分为两个核心环节：(1) 利用视觉伺服将缆线驱动的抓钳在图像平面内与目标位置对齐；(2) 提出一种新颖的深度控制方法MACAW（Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows），即基于单目视觉的自适应紧凑注意力窗口机制，用于精确控制深度方向上的接近。系统在dVRK机器人上实现，并进一步扩展至双臂清创设置。

Result: 在100次物理试验中，相机框架视觉伺服在4次优化步内将平均抓钳位置偏移从37像素降至不到5像素，平均耗时仅0.39秒。MACAW相比过程式基线和学习的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基线表现显著更优，单臂清创达到93%成功率，每块碎片11秒，吞吐量为304块/小时；双臂扩展设置保持92%成功率，每块碎片平均7秒，吞吐量提高至473块/小时。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强灵巧系统通过视觉伺服与MACAW深度控制相结合，能够高效可靠地执行手术清创，在成功率和吞吐量上均显著超越现有基线方法，且双臂扩展进一步提升了效率，展示了该系统在手术辅助中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Augmenting the dexterity of human surgeons has the potential to free them from tedious subtasks. We consider debridement (removal of diseased or dead tissue fragments), which is challenging due to imprecision in spatial perception and cable actuation. We develop an augmented dexterity system for surgical debridement that uses visual servoing to align the cable-driven gripper with the target position in the image plane, and then introduces a novel approach to depth control, MACAW: Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows. Across 100 physical trials using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) robot, camera-frame servoing reduced average gripper position offset from 37 to fewer than 5 pixels within 4 optimization steps, taking an average of only 0.39s. MACAW significantly outperforms procedural and learned VLA baselines, achieving a 93% success rate at 11 seconds per fragment, yielding a throughput of 304 fragments per hour. Extending MACAW to a bimanual debridement setup maintains a 92% success rate at an average of 7 seconds per fragment, increasing the throughput to 473 fragments per hour.

</details>


### [26] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight, Low-Profile Powered Knee Prosthesis with Quasi-Direct Drive Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02003)
*Ross J. Cortino,Ryan Posh,Emily G. Keller,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一款轻量级（2.6 kg）、低外形（24.5 cm）的准直接驱动（QDD）膝关节假肢，通过优化18:1两级传动以及热学和结构有限元分析，实现了145 Nm的峰值扭矩和1 Nm的低反向驱动扭矩，并在三名不同K级别的经股截肢参与者中验证了仿生运动学和峰值伸膝扭矩（在能体正常人参考的一个标准差内）。该设备的重量、构建高度、峰值扭矩和声学噪声达到或优于领先的商业动力膝关节，确立了QDD假肢的临床可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的被动假肢膝关节无法提供主动正功，导致用户在高能量活动中产生补偿行为。虽然QDD执行器相较于传统高传动比执行器具有更优的扭矩控制、反向驱动性和声学特性，但先前QDD原型因过于笨重和体积庞大而难以商业化，亟需轻量化和紧凑化的设计方案。

Method: 设计上通过优化两级18:1传动系统参数并结合热学与结构有限元分析来减轻质量；验证方面采用台架测试评估峰值扭矩、反向驱动扭矩和位置/扭矩控制精度，并招募三名不同K级别的经股截肢者在水平行走和坐站转换中评估仿生运动学和峰值伸膝扭矩。

Result: 设备总质量仅2.6 kg，总构建高度24.5 cm，峰值输出扭矩达145 Nm，反向驱动扭矩低至1 Nm。台架测试验证了精确的位置和扭矩控制能力；三名参与者的临床测试中膝关节运动学呈现仿生特征，峰值伸膝扭矩在能体正常人参考的一个标准差以内。设备的各项性能指标达到或优于领先的商业动力膝关节。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统的机械优化证明了QDD假肢膝关节在质量、体积、性能上已具备商业转化的临床可行性，为截肢用户提供更优的动态运动表现和更自然的行走能力。

Abstract: Fully-powered knee prostheses, unlike traditional passive knees, can perform controlled positive work, reducing the need for compensatory behaviors by users during energy-intensive activities. While quasi-direct drive (QDD) actuators provide superior torque control, backdrivability, and acoustic noise properties compared to traditional highly-geared actuators, prior QDD prototypes have been too heavy and bulky for commercial translation. In this work, we present the design and validation of a new lightweight (2.6 kg) and low-profile (24.5 cm tip-to-tip build height) QDD knee prosthesis. By optimizing an 18 to 1 two-stage transmission alongside thermal and structural finite-element analyses, we significantly reduce device mass while enabling a peak torque of 145 Nm. Through benchtop tests, we validate the device's high output torque, low backdrive torque (1 Nm), and its precision position and torque control capabilities. We also demonstrate biomimetic kinematics and peak knee extension torques (within one standard deviation of able-bodied references) during both level-ground walking and sit-stand transitions performed by three participants with transfemoral amputation and varying K-levels. By meeting or improving upon the mass, build height, peak torque, and acoustic noise of a leading commercial powered knee, this work establishes the clinical viability of emerging QDD prostheses that promise improved dynamic performance for their users.

Abstract: This study proposes a novel Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)-based task-space control framework. By leveraging MPPI, the proposed framework efficiently handles nonlinear dynamics that are difficult to solve with conventional MPC approaches in real-time. Furthermore, we develop a torque-sampling-based control architecture that enables efficient exploitation of GPU-based parallelization, resulting in effective compliant and force-aware behaviors. As a result, the proposed framework achieves a solver update rate of over 166 Hz with a 0.18 s prediction horizon, and its performance is validated through real-world experiments on a 7-DoF manipulator.

*Param Thakkar,Parsika Paresh Shah,Manisha Sushant Gote*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种显式建模"变化"的稀疏残差世界模型（每对象变化门控+残差增量头），相比稠密整体世界模型在物理预测与控制任务上更精确、参数更少、可迁移、泛化性强，并能服务于规划任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体式世界模型在每个时间步重新预测整个下一个状态，将计算和参数预算浪费在场景中静态的大多数部分，并不断向其中注入预测误差。作者质疑是否显式建模变化（先判断哪些对象发生变化，再仅对变化对象施加残差修正）是一种更有效且更具可解释性的物理预测与控制偏差。

Method: 提出稀疏/残差世界模型架构：包含一个"每对象变化门控"来标记哪些物体发生改变，以及一个"残差增量头"仅扰动被门控标记的对象的状态。在MuJoCo桌面推物基准上，在3到8个物体数量范围内与稠密多层感知机基线进行对比，并进一步测试其在自回归展开、以及基于采样的规划器中的表现。

Result: (1) 下一状态位姿预测精度提升2.5至4.6倍，参数量仅为稠密MLP的1/11.1至1/8.6；(2) 变化检测F1达0.80-0.87，稠密基线在此退化；(3) 跨物体数量零重训迁移，F1保留率99.4%；(4) 仅用1/4数据即可达到全数据约90%准确率；(5) 自回归展开误差累积显著更小，贴近"无运动"基准，而稠密模型漂移；(6) 在基于采样的规划器中，纯预测模型失败但真模拟器Oracle能成功，说明规划器本身没问题；然而经过特征化并在规划器访问状态下训练后，稀疏模型达到0.23±0.06成功率（三随机种子），稠密整体模型在所有种子下成功率均为零。

Conclusion: 建模"什么在变化"而非重新预测整个世界，是一种简单高效且面向物体中心的物理AI归纳偏差。该方法在精度、参数效率、数据效率、跨规模迁移及规划可用性等方面均优于稠密整体式世界模型。

Abstract: Monolithic world models predict the entire next state at every step, spending capacity re-predicting the static majority of a scene and injecting error into it. We ask whether explicitly modeling change (a per-object change gate plus a residual delta head that perturbs only the objects the gate flags) is a more effective and interpretable bias for physical prediction and control. On a MuJoCo tabletop pushing benchmark scaling from 3 to 8 objects, the sparse/residual model predicts next-state poses 2.5 to 4.6 times more accurately than a dense multilayer perceptron at 8.6 to 11.1 times fewer parameters, sustains change-detection F1 of 0.80 to 0.87 where the dense baseline is degenerate, transfers across object counts with zero retraining (99.4 percent F1 retention), and reaches about 90 percent of its full-data accuracy with a quarter of the data. In autoregressive rollout it compounds far less error, hugging the no-motion floor while the dense model drifts. Finally, inside a sampling-based planner, prediction-only models fail (though a true-simulator oracle solves the task with the identical planner, confirming the planner is sound), but once featurized and trained for the states a planner visits, the sparse model begins to plan (0.23 plus or minus 0.06 success over three seeds) while the dense monolith stays at zero at every seed. Modeling what changes, rather than re-predicting the whole world, is a simple, effective bias for object-centric physical AI; code, data generators, and all checkpoints will be released upon publication.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子的随机模型预测控制（MPC）框架，针对间歇性状态测量下的约束非线性系统，通过Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型提供线性潜变量预测器，并采用马尔可夫跳变误差模型与概率截断软约束机制，在可验证条件下证明了均方最终有界性与递归可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性的状态测量（如反馈中断和测量触发重置）会破坏标称状态传播，导致预测不确定性增长，从而威胁闭环稳定性和递归可行性，这是传统MPC在约束非线性系统中面临的根本性挑战。

Method: 采用Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型构建线性潜变量预测器以提升在线优化计算效率；将间歇测量过程建模为双模态离散时间马尔可夫链，统一描述开环传播与测量触发重置的误差动态；基于数值可验证的充分条件证明预测误差的均方最终有界性并给出显式二阶矩界；构造无分布依赖的概率误差半径，用于截断依赖于丢失率的约束收紧；采用精确罚软约束机制应对重置引起的跳变与长时间数据丢失。

Result: 理论方面，建立了递归可行性与闭环调节误差的均方最终有界性；仿真方面，在视觉伺服跟踪任务中验证了理论结果，展示了在随机测量不可用条件下的有效跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的Koopman随机MPC框架能够在间歇性测量条件下保证闭环稳定性与可行性约束，为实际工程中测量中断场景下的预测控制提供了理论保障与有效解决方案。

Abstract: Intermittent state measurements pose fundamental challenges to model predictive control of constrained nonlinear systems because prediction uncertainty grows during feedback outages and measurement-triggered resets disrupt nominal state propagation, potentially compromising closed-loop stability and recursive feasibility. This paper develops a Koopman-based stochastic MPC framework with probabilistically truncated soft constraints. Specifically, a Lipschitz-constrained deep Koopman model provides a linear latent predictor, enabling computationally efficient online optimization. The intermittent measurement process is modeled as a two-mode discrete-time Markov chain, yielding a unified Markov jump error model for open-loop propagation and measurement-triggered resets. Under numerically verifiable sufficient conditions, the prediction error is shown to be mean-square ultimately bounded, and an explicit uniform second-moment bound is obtained. A distribution-free probabilistic error radius is then constructed for a prescribed confidence level and used to truncate dropout-dependent constraint tightening. An exact-penalty soft-constraint mechanism accommodates reset-induced jumps and prolonged dropouts. Under the stated terminal compatibility and bounded-disturbance conditions, recursive feasibility and mean-square ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop regulation error are established. Numerical simulations on a visual-servoing tracking task corroborate these theoretical results and demonstrate effective tracking under stochastic measurement unavailability.

</details>


### [30] [Unified Motion Retargeting for Humanoids with Learned Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02134)
*Hanyang Cao,Yuetong Fang,Taesoo Kwon,Runyi Yu,Ji Ma,Jing Tan,Yangchen Zhou,Baoze Du,Yi Gu,Yukang Gao,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UMR框架，通过学习人类运动与仿人机器人之间的稠密点云对应关系，无需手工设计的人机映射，实现跨异构运动源、机器人形态和下游场景的统一运动重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法依赖于手工设计的稀疏关键点或身体部位对应关系，受限于人工语义设计，可扩展性差，且对细节姿态和交互的复现指导稀疏，难以满足大规模多样化人类运动数据向机器人参考轨迹高效转化的需求。

Method: UMR以外部点云作为人类运动与仿人机器人之间的统一接口，将重定向与源特定骨骼语义及机器人特定拓扑解耦；通过学习稠密点云对应关系，为受约束的点云匹配优化提供细粒度几何锚点，实现表面级姿态对齐和交互接触点直接迁移。

Result: 实验表明UMR能够统一跨越异构运动源、不同机器人形态及从运动到交互等多种下游场景的重定向任务，在运动保真度和合理性上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: UMR为将大规模人类运动参考高效转化为机器人训练数据提供了可扩展的基础框架，有助于推动仿人机器人学习对多样化人类运动数据的利用。

Abstract: Humanoid learning increasingly relies on transforming vast and diverse human motion data into high-quality robot reference trajectories. However, retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is challenging due to substantial differences in morphology, degrees of freedom, joint ranges, and kinematic constraints between humans and robots. Existing retargeting methods typically address these differences by defining human-robot correspondence through hand-crafted sparse keypoints or body-part pairs. As a result, retargeting quality depends heavily on manual semantic design, limiting scalability across motion sources and robot morphologies and providing only sparse guidance for reproducing detailed poses and interactions. In this paper, we present Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR), a framework that learns dense point cloud correspondence without requiring manually designed human-robot mappings. By treating exterior point clouds as a unified interface between human motion and humanoid robots, UMR decouples retargeting from source-specific skeletal semantics and robot-specific topology. The learned dense correspondence provides fine-grained geometric anchors for constrained point cloud matching optimization, enabling surface-level pose alignment and direct transfer of interaction contacts. Experiments demonstrate that UMR unifies retargeting across heterogeneous motion sources, robot embodiments, and downstream scenarios ranging from locomotion to interaction, while achieving higher motion fidelity and plausibility than state-of-the-art methods. UMR therefore provides a scalable foundation for transforming large-scale human motion references into robot-ready training data.

</details>


### [31] [Towards Effective Physical Reservoir Computing with a Pneumatic Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02157)
*Jeevan Hebbal Manjunath,Jun Wang,Suyi Li,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用带有五囊传感柱的气动软臂，系统研究了囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度和传感数量对弯曲角估计性能的影响，提出三条物理储层计算（PRC）设计准则：独立密封囊保留更丰富的可观察状态；增大传感柱基线压力会增加估计误差（在耦合拓扑中最严重）；密封拓扑中少量策略性放置的传感器即可获得绝大部分性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（PRC）利用物理动力学系统作为计算资源用于状态估计和控制，但缺乏关于如何更有效设计此类物理储层的正式设计规则研究。

Method: 采用气动软臂的五囊传感柱结构，改变囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度和传感器数量，在36组匹配试验中（覆盖波形、传感柱基线压力和驱动范围的变化），以0.2秒压力历史和固定岭估计器，在同一时间弯曲角估计基准下评估所有设计。

Result: 发现三条设计准则：1）独立密封的囊比共享歧管保留更丰富的可观察状态；2）增大传感柱基线压力使囊响应更冗余并增加估计误差，这在耦合拓扑中最显著；3）在密封拓扑中，两个策略性放置的传感器即可恢复大部分性能收益，三个可捕获几乎所有收益，额外传感器几乎没有价值。

Conclusion: 拓扑、刚度和传感数量应协同设计以实现软体机器人状态的高精度PRC；单纯增强激励无法恢复糟糕设计选择已去除的状态多样性。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) refers to the use of a physical dynamical system as a computational resource for tasks such as state estimation and control, but there has been a lack of formal study of design rules towards more effective design of such physical reservoirs. Using a pneumatic soft arm with a five-pouch sensing column, this work studies how the pouch interconnection topology, robot stiffness, and the number of instrumented sensors affect bending-angle estimation performance. Across 36 matched trials spanning waveform, baseline pressure of the sensing column, and actuation range, all designs are evaluated under the same-time bending-angle estimation benchmark using 0.2 s of pressure history and a fixed ridge estimator. Our analysis of the experimental results leads to three design guidelines. First, independently sealed pouches preserve a much richer observable state than a shared manifold. Second, increasing the baseline pressure of the sensing column makes the pouch responses more redundant and increases estimation error most strongly in the coupled topology. Third, in the sealed topology, two strategically placed sensors already recover most of the attainable benefit, three capture essentially all of it, and additional sensors provide little or no additional value. In summary, the results suggest that topology, stiffness, and number of instrumented sensors should be co-designed for accurate PRC of soft robot states; stronger excitation alone cannot recover the diversity that poor design choices have already removed.

</details>


### [32] [Hardware-Accelerated Instance Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Space Robotics with Criticality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02219)
*Siddhant Shete,Hilmi Dogu Kücüker,Udo Frese,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源受限月球机器人的端到端实例分割框架，通过AVIS标签无关校准策略恢复量化精度损失、在DPU上部署YOLO分割模型实现有界延迟推理，并引入软件级关键性分析降低辐射故障风险。实验表明该方法在月球微型漫游车上恢复了69.8%的量化精度损失，推理延迟309ms、功耗5.7W，全局关键性降低31.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主月球任务需要同时在极低光照、有限星载算力和辐射诱导硬件故障三重耦合约束下实现实时感知。现有方法未能联合解决量化校准与系统级故障暴露问题，缺乏在严格计算约束下兼顾精度、延迟与可靠性的部署方案。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS)，一种基于激活方差统计的确定性标签无关校准样本选择策略；(2) 在深度学习处理器单元(DPU)上部署YOLO实例分割模型，通过架构改造减少CPU回退路径并支持静态编译执行以获得低光照条件下的有界延迟；(3) 软件级关键性分析用于估计辐射约束下的故障暴露并指导缓解措施。

Result: 在月球微型漫游车平台上，AVIS结合偏差校正恢复了69.8%的量化诱导精度损失，实现309ms推理延迟与5.7W功耗；定向缓解使全局关键性降低31.7%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该集成方法的有效性，为空间部署约束下可靠且安全的AI感知框架提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Autonomous lunar missions require real-time per- ception under three coupled constraints: extreme low-light conditions, limited onboard compute, and radiation-induced hardware faults that can silently corrupt inference. We present a deployment-oriented instance segmentation framework for resource-constrained lunar robotics that jointly addresses quan- tization calibration and system-level fault exposure under strict compute constraints. First, we introduce Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS), a label-free calibration strategy that deterministically selects calibration samples based on activation variance statistics. Second, we deploy a YOLO-based segmentation model on a Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU) with architectural modifications that reduce CPU fallback paths and enable statically compiled execution with bounded latency in low-lighting conditions. We further introduce a software-level criticality analysis to estimate fault exposure and guide mitigation under radiation-constrained operation. On a lunar micro-rover platform, AVIS with bias correction recovers 69.8% of quantization-induced accuracy loss while achieving 309 ms inference latency and 5.7 W power consumption. Targeted mitigation reduces global criticality by 31.7%. The results demonstrate an integrated approach and a blueprint for a reliable and safe AI perception framework under space deployment constraints.

</details>


### [33] [FOCUS: Foot Observation Confidence for Robust Humanoid Proprioceptive Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02222)
*Kaixin Feng,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,You Li,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCUS方法，利用仿真训练的神经网络预测每条腿的连续正向运动学（FK）可靠性权重，替代传统的二元接触判断，用于腿式里程计中平滑地融合FK速度观测与IMU传播的速度信息，显著降低轨迹漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触辅助估计器依赖二元接触决策来判断是否信任整条腿的FK测量，然而接触并不等价于FK可靠。在动态运动中存在部分支撑、脚趾拖拽和打滑等情况，二元接触决策会在长轨迹中累积显著漂移。因此需要一种能刻画FK可靠性的连续权重预测方法。

Method: 提出FOCUS框架：1）训练神经网络预测每条腿的连续FK可靠性权重而非二元接触；2）预测的权重用于在EKF中将FK速度观测与IMU传播的身体速度进行加权融合，并自适应调整观测协方差，实现平滑的可靠性感知融合；3）网络通过自动生成的仿真信号，利用FK加权速度一致性损失和轻量仿真接触正则化进行训练，无需人工标注的连续FK可靠性标签；4）部署模型仅依赖IMU和关节运动学测量，不依赖扭矩传感。

Result: 仿真行走场景中绝对轨迹误差（ATE）降低83.7%；保持动态运动的尺度保真度和频谱能量；在19段真实行走数据上ATE降低70.8%；在四组真实动态运动例程中平均ATE降低42.7%。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过以连续FK可靠性权重替代二元接触判断，有效解决了动态运动中接触不等于FK可靠的问题，显著提升了腿式里程计在长期和动态场景下的精度，且仅依赖IMU与关节运动学，具备良好的硬件部署适用性。

Abstract: Foot forward kinematics (FK) is widely used to improve proprioceptive legged odometry by providing reliable velocity constraints during foot support. Existing contact-aided estimators generally rely on binary contact decisions to determine whether the FK measurements of an entire foot should be trusted. However, contact does not necessarily imply FK reliability. Dynamic locomotion often involves partial support, toe dragging, and foot slip, causing binary contact decisions to accumulate significant drift over long trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose FOCUS (Foot Observation Confidence from Unannotated Simulation), which predicts a continuous FK reliability weight for each foot instead of estimating binary foot contact. Rather than replacing the model-based estimator, the predicted reliability weights are used to blend FK velocity observations with IMU-propagated body velocity and to adapt the observation covariance of an extended Kalman filter (EKF), enabling smooth reliability-aware fusion without hard contact switching. The network is trained from automatically generated simulation signals using an FK-weighted velocity consistency loss with lightweight simulator-contact regularization, without manually annotated continuous FK-reliability labels. The deployed model relies only on IMU and joint kinematic measurements, making it suitable for hardware platforms with unreliable torque sensing. Experiments demonstrate that FOCUS reduces absolute trajectory error (ATE) by 83.7% on simulated walking episodes, preserves simulated dynamic-motion fidelity in motion scale and spectral energy, reduces ATE by 70.8% across 19 real walking segments, and reduces mean ATE by 42.7% across four real dynamic-motion routines.

</details>


### [34] [DiffuSearch: How Hybrid Trajectory Planning Benefits from Aligned Objectives in Diffusion and Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02252)
*Steffen Hagedorn,Aron Distelzweig,Alexandru P. Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffuSearch是一种新型混合规划器，通过统一的驾驶目标集合（碰撞避免、可行驶区域合规性、舒适性和进度）将扩散模型生成与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）细化集成在一起，在nuPlan和interPlan基准上实现了强大的闭环比基准测试性能，尤其显著减少了复杂交互场景中的碰撞并提升了舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶混合规划架构由多个目标各异的独立模块组成，缺乏统一的指导原则，导致初始轨迹与细化轨迹之间不一致，从而产生次优行为。作者旨在通过跨生成与细化阶段共享同一组驾驶目标来解决这一一致性问题。

Method: DiffuSearch采用两阶段架构：（1）引导扩散模型利用驾驶目标作为可微引导函数，隐式引导去噪过程生成场景一致的联合轨迹预测；（2）蒙特卡洛树搜索在离散动作空间中对轨迹提议进行显式局部细化，并使用相同的驾驶目标作为奖励函数。这种协同设计结合了扩散模型寻找场景一致解的能力与MCTS的可解释、约束感知的细化能力。

Result: 在nuPlan和interPlan反应式闭环基准上的实验表明，DiffuSearch实现了强健且常为最先进的性能，显著减少了碰撞并改善了舒适性，尤其在复杂交互场景中。消融研究显示MCTS细化是性能提升的主要机制，而共享生成与搜索阶段的目标能提供进一步的一致改进。

Conclusion: 在生成与细化阶段共享统一的目标集合，是构建一致且高性能自动驾驶混合规划器的有效途径。该设计在复杂交互场景中展现出显著的碰撞减少和舒适性提升优势。

Abstract: In trajectory planning for autonomous driving, hybrid planning architectures are often realized as a collection of disparate modules, each with its own objectives. This lack of a unifying principle can lead to inconsistencies between the initial and refined trajectory, resulting in suboptimal behavior. We address this by introducing DiffuSearch, a novel hybrid planner that uses a unified set of objectives across generation and refinement. Our model encourages all components to follow the same shared driving goals: collision avoidance, drivable area compliance, comfort, and progress. DiffuSearch employs a two-stage architecture. First, a guided diffusion model generates a scene-consistent, joint trajectory prediction, using our driving objectives as differentiable guidance functions to implicitly steer the denoising process. Second, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a discretized action space performs an explicit, local refinement of this proposal, leveraging the same driving objectives as its reward function. This synergistic design leverages the diffusion model's strength in finding scene-consistent solutions combined with the explainable, constraint-aware refinement of MCTS. Experiments on nuPlan and interPlan reactive closed-loop benchmarks demonstrate that DiffuSearch achieves strong and often state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing collisions and improving comfort, particularly in complex, interactive scenarios. Our ablation studies indicate that MCTS refinement is the main mechanism behind the gains, while sharing objectives between implicit guidance and explicit search provides further consistent improvements.

</details>


### [35] [CrashDiffuser: VLM-Guided Collision Intent Reasoning for Fine-Grained Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02270)
*Shucheng Zhang,Yuang Zhang,Bingzhang Wang,Muhammad Monjurul Karim,Kehua Chen,Yinhai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CrashDiffuser，一种闭环VLM引导的扩散框架，用于精细粒度安全关键场景生成，不仅要求目标碰撞发生，还要求碰撞发生在指定接触区域（车头、车尾或侧面）。在WOMD闭环场景中，单次尝试目标碰撞率为50.33%，三次尝试为67.98%，接触区域控制成功率为40.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成器主要聚焦于诱导碰撞，缺乏对碰撞发生在目标车辆具体位置（车头、车尾或侧面）的精细控制能力，难以满足对自动驾驶系统进行更细粒度安全评估的需求。

Method: 提出CrashDiffuser框架：通过层次化碰撞意图接口解耦语义碰撞推理与连续轨迹合成。初始化阶段VLM提取可复用的场景级上下文；每个重规划步骤中VLM预测结构化动作元组（速度变化、转向行为、碰撞阶段），该意图条件化扩散模型生成可执行的对抗性轨迹；结合碰撞引导采样、候选选择和短时域重规划适应目标车辆动态行为。

Result: 在WOMD派生的闭环场景上，单次尝试目标碰撞率为50.33%，三次尝试为67.98%；接触区域控制成功率为40.05%，轨迹自然度具有竞争力。组件消融实验支持了所提设计的有效性。

Conclusion: CrashDiffuser成功实现了具有碰撞接触区域精细控制能力的对抗性场景生成，验证了VLM语义推理与扩散轨迹合成的解耦设计的有效性，为安全关键场景生成提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing generators primarily focus on inducing collisions and offer limited control over where contact occurs on the target vehicle. In this paper, we study fine-grained safety-critical scenario generation, where success requires both a target collision and a specified head, rear, or side contact region. We propose CrashDiffuser, a closed-loop VLM-guided diffusion framework that decouples semantic collision reasoning from continuous trajectory synthesis through a hierarchical collision-intent interface derived from the requested target contact region. At initialization, the VLM extracts reusable scene-level context; at each replanning step, it predicts a structured action tuple describing speed change, turning behavior, and collision stage. This intent conditions a diffusion model to generate executable adversarial trajectories, while collision-guided sampling, candidate selection, and short-horizon replanning adapt generation to the target vehicle's evolving behavior. On WOMD-derived closed-loop scenarios, CrashDiffuser achieves a target-collision rate of 50.33% in a single attempt and 67.98% after three attempts, together with a contact-region control success rate of 40.05% and competitive trajectory naturalness. Component ablations further support the proposed design.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖关节编码器和骨盆IMU、在静态双足支撑接触下进行人形机器人下肢关节编码器偏置自校准的框架。通过最小化双脚固定时前向运动学求得的脚间变换的姿态相关离散度，构建12维偏置向量的非线性最小二乘问题，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证了校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人下肢的关节编码器偏置准确性对运动学一致性至关重要，但现有标定方法通常依赖外部动作捕捉系统或靶标，限制了实际应用中的便捷性和自包含性。

Method: 提出自包含校准框架：利用静态双足支撑接触条件下，双脚固定时由前向运动学得到的脚间变换必须保持不变的物理约束，最小化其姿态相关离散度，构建关于12维偏置向量的非线性最小二乘优化问题。通过Hessian特征结构分析揭示平行俯仰轴导致的旋转耦合；针对A3机器人pitch-to-roll-to-yaw的关节顺序，利用髋部横滚和髋部偏航激励降低髋部俯仰耦合；引入站立姿态膝关节先验锚定弱俯仰链分解。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人注入测试均显示偏置可一致恢复；在留存录音数据上，A3机器人足高RMS残差从4.26mm降至2.20mm，A2机器人从8.03mm降至1.43mm。通过与LiDAR-惯性参考对比检验俯仰耦合通道，去除注入的俯仰偏置后腿部里程计垂直漂移回归LiDAR轨迹。

Conclusion: 少量静态双足支撑站位即可为充分激励的方向提供与接触一致的偏置校正，但弱俯仰链中的个别偏置仍依赖先验信息，呈现先验依赖性。

Abstract: Accurate joint encoder offsets are essential for kinematic consistency in humanoid lower limbs, yet existing calibration methods typically require external motion-capture systems or fiducial targets. We present a self-contained calibration framework exploiting only onboard joint encoders and a pelvis-mounted IMU during static double-support contact. The inter-foot transform from forward kinematics must stay constant when both feet are fixed; minimizing its posture-dependent dispersion yields a nonlinear least-squares problem over the 12-dimensional offset vector. A Hessian eigenstructure analysis shows that parallel pitch axes induce a rotational coupling. Orientation residuals then observe only the pitch-offset sum, while translation and posture diversity set the remaining numerical observability. For the A3 pitch-to-roll-to-yaw ordering, hip-roll and hip-yaw excitation reduce hip-pitch coupling. A standing-posture knee prior then anchors the remaining weak pitch-chain decomposition. Simulation and real-machine injection tests show consistent recovery, and on held-out recordings calibration reduces foot-height RMS residuals from 4.26 to 2.20 mm on A3 and from 8.03 to 1.43 mm on A2. An independent LiDAR-inertial reference checks the pitch-coupled channel. Removing an injected pitch offset moves the leg-odometry vertical drift back toward the LiDAR trajectory. A few static double-support stances thus provide contact-consistent corrections for well-excited directions. Individual offsets in the weak pitch chain remain prior-dependent.

</details>


### [37] [From Multi-Fisheye Sensing to Panoramic Perception: A Parallax-Aware Onboard Platform for Ultra-Low-Altitude UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02319)
*Dun Dai,Ze Lu,Cheng He,Yaowen Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向超低空无人机(UAV)的视差感知全景视觉平台，该平台将四个同步鱼眼相机流融合为开放的1280x640等距柱状投影(ERP)全景。平台采用碳纤维机框架集成相机、NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX、飞控与GNSS接收器，形成流水线包含按重叠区域的投影深度选择、受控接缝与光度融合。提供"高精度配置"(内容自适应接缝搜索+验证门控残差网格)与"部署配置"(保留边距门控逐接缝更新以支持传感器速率运行)双模式。基于50,000+四视图组(18个野外序列)评估：高精度配置使远场P90特征错位较固定深度降低41.6%，部署配置在保留场地取得最低聚合几何误差；20Hz重放下部署配置维持19.99帧/s、平均模块输入功耗13.29W；八扇区ERP采样在日间视觉位置识别层面达到90.8%平均Recall@5。项目已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低空UAV在建筑物、植被和障碍物附近飞行时需要环绕视野，但缺乏集成的机载全景感知方案，特别是考虑到视差(parallax)问题的影响——不同深度物体在全景拼接中会产生几何错位，影响下游视觉任务。

Method: 构建专用碳纤维机架集成四路同步鱼眼相机、NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX、飞控与GNSS接收器；设计全景拼接流水线，核心创新包括：(1)按重叠区域自适应选择投影深度；(2)受控接缝搜索与光度融合；(3)提供双配置——精度配置采用内容自适应接缝搜索与验证门控残差网格，(4)部署配置采用边距门控逐接缝更新以适配传感器速率机载实时执行。

Result: 在超过50,000个四视图样本(来自18个野外序列)上进行评估：高精度配置相对Fixed Depth方法将远场P90特征错位降低41.6%；部署配置在保留测试场地取得最低聚合几何误差；20Hz播放下部署配置达到19.99帧/s吞吐，平均功耗13.29W；八扇区ERP采样用于下游视觉位置识别任务达到90.8%的平均Recall@5。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了该集成式机载全景感知架构的有效性，统一了视差感知生成、嵌入式传感器速率执行与可复用的下游视觉接口，为超低空UAV提供实用且开源的全景视觉感知方案。

Abstract: Ultra-low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) require surround vision near buildings, vegetation, and other obstacles. We present a parallax-aware onboard platform that converts four synchronized fisheye streams into an open 1280x640 equirectangular panorama (ERP) interface. A purpose-built carbon-fiber airframe integrates the cameras, NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, a flight controller, and a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) receiver. The formation pipeline selects projection depth per overlap and combines controlled seams and photometric fusion. Its accuracy profile adds content-adaptive seam search and a validation-gated residual mesh, whereas its deployed profile retains margin-gated Per-seam updates for sensor-rate operation. Evaluation uses more than 50,000 four-view groups from 18 field sequences. Relative to Fixed Depth, the accuracy profile reduces far-field P90 feature misalignment by 41.6%; the deployed Per-seam profile achieves the lowest aggregate geometric errors across held-out sites. Under a paced 20 Hz replay, the deployed profile sustains 19.99 frames/s at 13.29W mean module-input power. Eight-sector ERP sampling reaches 90.8% mean daytime visual-place-recognition Recall@5. Together, these results validate an integrated onboard panoramic-perception architecture that unifies parallax-aware formation, sensor-rate embedded execution, and reusable downstream vision interfaces for ultra-low-altitude UAVs. The project has been open-sourced at https://github.com/DUNDAI1998/parallax-aware-uav-panorama.

</details>


### [38] [Humanoid Safe Stop via Learned Stoppability Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02358)
*Junfeng Long,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath,Roberto Horowitz,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Safe-Stop 是一种任务无关的人形机器人紧急停止框架，通过将紧急停止问题建模为可达-规避（reach-avoid）问题，结合学习式停止策略与两类互补的可停止性估计器（停止概率估计器与可达-规避估计器），在部署时仅当两个估计器均判断停止可行时才执行停止，否则切换至阻尼跌落回退策略，从而在不牺牲反应速度的前提下实现鲁棒的紧急停止决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人对紧急停止命令的响应通常执行固定的预定动作，而不推断从当前状态出发安全停止是否实际可行。这种缺乏推理的固定策略可能导致在某些状态下停止本身就不安全，需要一种能评估停止可行性的框架。

Method: 将紧急停止建模为可达-规避问题；提出 Safe-Stop 框架，包含：1) 学习式停止策略；2) 两个互补的估计器——停止概率估计器（由固定停止策略的实际结果监督）和可达-规避估计器（由物理状态上的 Hamilton-Jacobi 备份监督）；3) 部署时两个估计作一致性检查，仅在两个估计器都表明停止可行时执行停止，否则移交至阻尼跌落回退策略。由于停止策略与估计器不依赖停止命令前的行为策略，因此可跨多样上游任务迁移而无需重训练。

Result: Safe-Stop 的一致性检查使决策既鲁棒又不牺牲反应速度；框架是任务无关的，可跨不同上游任务直接迁移，停止概率估计器捕获学习控制器的涌现停止行为，可达-规避估计器提供互补的可恢复性信号。

Conclusion: Safe-Stop 通过将停止可行性的双重估计融合为一致性判定，为类人机器人紧急停止提供了一种任务无关、无需重训练即可跨任务迁移的鲁棒框架，为安全关键场景下的停止决策提供了新思路。

Abstract: Humanoid robots responding to emergency stop commands typically execute a fixed maneuver, without reasoning about whether a safe stop is actually feasible from the current state. We cast emergency stopping as a reach-avoid problem and propose Safe-Stop, a task-agnostic framework that pairs a learned stop policy with learned stoppability estimators. The estimators are complementary: a stop-probability estimator supervised by the actual outcomes of the fixed stop policy, and a reach-avoidance estimator supervised by a Hamilton-Jacobi backup over physical state. The first captures emergent stopping behavior of the learned controller; the second provides a complementary recoverability signal. Because the stop policy and estimators do not depend on the behavior policy that preceded the stop command, they transfer across diverse upstream tasks without retraining. At deployment, the two estimates are combined: Safe-Stop commits to the stop only when both estimators indicate that stopping remains feasible, otherwise it hands off to a fall policy, instantiated as a damping fallback. This agreement check yields decisions that are robust without sacrificing reactivity.

</details>


### [39] [A Physics-Consistent Benchmark for Contact-Rich Human-Robot Interaction in Assistive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02402)
*Chengxiao He,Shanghai Yuan,Liuqun Fan,Shenzhen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个物理一致的接触丰富人机交互基准，实例化为机器人辅助沐浴任务。该基准结合可变形被动响应的人体模型、物理意识评分与任务级成功率，以及冻结的纯视觉/纯评分评估协议。实验表明任务完成率并不保证物理有效的接触，呼吁在部署前进行物理意识筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的任务级评估仅检查机器人策略是否完成指定动作，但可能遗漏仅在物理人体接触中出现的失败。这在接触丰富型辅助任务中尤为关键，因为此类任务需要物理响应的人体、超越任务成功的交互质量评估，以及无泄漏的观察者-评分者协议。

Method: 构建机器人辅助沐浴的物理一致基准：(1)可变形且被动响应的人体模型；(2)结合物理意识评分与任务级成功率的多维评估框架；(3)采用冻结的仅视觉/仅评分评估协议（T1-T7，每方法140次运行），避免信息泄漏；区域级模拟响应通过Franka阻抗推压医疗护理人体模型获得的力-压痕数据进行校准。

Result: LLM增强状态机方法任务成功率达72.9%，但经正确区域和力的安全筛选后降至56.4%；VoxPoser产生更轻更稳定的接触但仅完成任务27.9%的试验；零样本pi0.5任务成功率仅0.7%，且无正确区域或安全门控的成功。

Conclusion: 任务完成本身并不蕴含物理有效的接触行为。接触丰富型辅助机器人策略在部署前需要经过物理意识的安全筛选与验证，以保障人机交互中的物理合理性与安全性。

Abstract: Conventional task-level evaluation asks whether a robot policy completes a specified action, but can miss failures that emerge only during physical human contact. The benchmark combines a deformable, passively responding human, physics-aware scores alongside task-level success, and a frozen vision-only / scorer-only evaluation protocol. To establish physical validity, region-wise simulated responses are calibrated against force-indentation measurements from Franka impedance pushes on a medical-care manikin. Under a frozen T1-T7 protocol with 140 runs per method, an LLM-augmented state machine (State Machine) achieves 72.9% task success but drops to 56.4% after correct-region and force-safety screening; VoxPoser produces lighter and more stable contact but completes only 27.9% of trials; and zero-shot pi0.5 achieves 0.7% task success with no correct-region or safety-gated successes. These results show that task completion alone does not imply physically valid contact and motivate physics-aware screening before deployment of contact-rich assistive robot policies.

Abstract: The ability to move stably over terrain with varying slopes and textures is essential for mobile agricultural robots operating in complex and dynamic environments such as greenhouses, where small terrain irregularities can lead to significant navigation errors. Based on this information, a cascade trajectory-tracking scheme has been designed, consisting of a model-based predictive controller (MPC) in the outer loop and a PI controller in the inner loop. The system incorporates an adaptive feedforward control through gain scheduling approach, capable of adjusting to disturbances caused by variations in slope and terrain type. Simulation results demonstrate that the differential-drive robot achieves a significant improvement both in error indices and in control signal efficiency, highlighting the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

*Yitian Shi,Jesper Mücke,Nils Dengler,Sicong Pan,Rania Rayyes,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MS-MEM是一个面向受限制杂乱场景（如货架）的多技能操作增强建图框架，将主动视角选择、物体推动与抓取整合进一个统一的证据推断框架中，通过不确定性感知的度量-语义建图来提升场景理解精度，同时利用碰撞扰动约束（CDC）最小化对场景的过度干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在拥挤受限环境（如货架）中需要准确理解场景以可靠地定位和抓取物体，但严重遮挡、可及性受限以及需避免过度场景改变使得这一任务极具挑战。现有方法未能有效协同多种操作技能并控制操作带来的场景扰动。

Result: 实验表明，与忽略场景扰动的单技能及无约束基线相比，MS-MEM在实现更高建图精度的同时大幅降低了场景扰动，验证了主动视角选择、推动与抓取动作之间的协同增效作用。

Conclusion: MS-MEM证明了将多种操作技能与不确定性感知地图表征统一在一个信息最大化框架中的有效性，实现了在复杂受限空间中的高精度、低干预建图，为服务机器人在真实场景中的可靠操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate scene understanding in confined, cluttered spaces such as shelves is essential for service robots, as many everyday tasks require them to locate and retrieve objects reliably. Yet, it remains challenging due to severe occlusions, restricted accessibility, and the need to avoid excessive scene changes. In this paper, we propose Multi-Skill Manipulation-Enhanced Mapping (MS-MEM), an evidential framework for uncertainty-aware mapping that integrates active viewpoint selection, object pushing, and grasping. MS-MEM combines scene-level metric-semantic evidential belief estimators with an uncertainty-aware grasp representation. This representation is learned using a novel full-evidential grasp estimator that models both grasp affordance and orientation uncertainty. In our framework, candidate perception and manipulation actions are evaluated within a unified action selection pipeline using a common information gain criterion. For manipulation actions, we further introduce a collateral disturbance constraint (CDC) that discourages excessive changes to confident regions of the scene belief. This enables MS-MEM to select actions that effectively reduce map uncertainty while limiting collateral scene changes. Experimental results show that, compared with single-skill and unconstrained baselines that ignore scene disturbance, MS-MEM achieves higher mapping accuracy while substantially reducing scene disturbance, highlighting the synergistic effects of active viewpoint selection, push, and grasp actions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出WM-LOCO方法，通过联合训练循环世界模型与PPO策略，为足式机器人在立足点受限地形（踏脚石、间隙、窄台阶）上的步态规划提供预测性信息。该方法仅需本体感觉和单目深度图，无需显式立足点标签，仿真中在间隙和踏脚石上显著优于基线，并在实体Unitree G1人形机器人上实现93.3%的平均成功率。

Motivation: 立足点受限地形（踏脚石、间隙、窄台阶）上可行落足点稀疏且几何受限，一旦失足几乎没有恢复余地。现有策略主要依赖即时可见地形进行落脚决策，缺乏对近未来状态的预判能力，在这种高风险场景下容易失败。

Method: 提出World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion (WM-LOCO)框架：(1) 联合训练一个循环世界模型（recurrent world model）和PPO策略；(2) 世界模型以本体感觉和单一机载深度图像为条件，生成预测性的循环特征（predictive recurrent feature）来引导策略；(3) 整个过程无需显式立足点标签。

Result: 仿真实验中，WM-LOCO在间隙和踏脚石地形上完全超越匹配基线（基线完全失败），在楼梯上匹配基线成功率的同时提升了步幅效率并降低了骨盆加速度。在物理Unitree G1人形机器人上，使用机载本体感觉和单深度流部署相同策略，三种地形平均成功率达93.3%。

Conclusion: 通过学习近未来观测与奖励的预测性摘要，WM-LOCO有效地为视觉运动策略提供了在立足点受限地形上所需的预期性信息，并通过零额外标注的端到端学习在仿真和实体机器人上均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Foothold-constrained terrain is characterized by sparse, discontinuous, or geometrically restricted feasible foot contacts, as encountered on stepping stones, across gaps, and on narrow stair treads. On such terrain, a single misstep often leaves little room to recover, so policies that base foot-placement decisions primarily on the immediately visible terrain are prone to failure. We ask whether a learned predictive summary of near-future observations and rewards can provide the anticipatory information required in such settings. We present World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion (WM-LOCO), which jointly trains a recurrent world model and a PPO policy. Conditioned on proprioception and a single onboard depth image, the world model produces a predictive recurrent feature that guides the policy, without explicit foothold labels. In simulation, WM-LOCO succeeds on gaps and stepping stones where a matched baseline fails completely, and matches the baseline's success rate on stairs while improving stride efficiency and reducing pelvis acceleration. We deploy the same policy onboard a physical Unitree G1 humanoid using onboard proprioception and a single depth stream; it traverses all three terrain classes with an average success rate of 93.3%.

</details>

*Mi Yan,Wenhao Zhang,Zhiqi Zhang,Yu Peng,Tangxinyu Wang,Lingfei Zhai,Jiayi Su,Shengliang Deng,Lin Peng,Yaowei Liu,Yuxing Chen,Zhiyuan Wei,Jilong Wang,Jiayi Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的跨实体零样本泛化问题，首次区分了"严格零样本迁移"与"预训练暴露零样本迁移"两种定义，并构建了包含14个保留目标实体的受控基准，系统分析了状态-动作表示、预训练实体多样性、辅助共训练目标及目标实体暴露四个因素对迁移效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人硬件不断演进且任务特定数据采集成本高昂，VLA模型对未见实体的零样本泛化至关重要，但该问题缺乏系统性的理解——现有文献没有统一的零样本迁移定义，也缺少能够将实体变化与任务、场景或协议差异分离开来的受控评估设置。

Method: 首先定义了两种零样本迁移类型：严格零样本迁移（目标实体完全不出现在训练数据中）和预训练暴露零样本迁移（目标实体仅出现在预训练阶段）。随后构建了一个覆盖14个保留目标实体的受控基准，涵盖仿真和真实世界验证。在该框架下，对四个因素进行受控分析：状态-动作表示、预训练实体多样性、辅助共训练目标和目标实体暴露程度。

Result: 局部末端执行器（EEF）状态-动作表示、源实体多样性及辅助共训练分别将跨实体迁移性能提升约15、18和7个百分点；在预训练中加入仅5%的目标实体数据可将平均目标实体进步提升13.4个百分点，证实了严格零样本与预训练暴露零样本迁移之间存在本质区别。

Conclusion: 研究为固定桌面操作场景中双指夹爪的跨实体VLA迁移评估和改进提供了实用指导，同时呼吁未来探索更广泛的设置，包括移动底座控制、灵巧手和长时程任务。

Abstract: Zero-shot generalization to unseen embodiments is important for generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models as robot hardware evolves and task-specific data collection remains costly. However, a systematic understanding of this problem remains limited, in part because the literature lacks a unified zero-shot transfer definition and controlled evaluation settings that isolate embodiment changes from differences in tasks, scenes, or protocols. To address this gap, we first distinguish strict zero-shot transfer, where the target embodiment is absent from all training data, from pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer, where it appears only during pretraining. We then introduce a controlled benchmark spanning 14 held-out target embodiments across simulation and real-world validation. Within this framework, we conduct a controlled analysis of four factors: state-action representations, pretraining embodiment diversity, auxiliary co-training objectives, and target-embodiment exposure. Experimental results show that local end-effector (EEF) state-action representations, the source embodiment diversity, and auxiliary co-training improve cross-embodiment transfer by around 15, 18, and 7 percentage points, respectively. We further find that adding only 5% target-embodiment data during pretraining improves average target-embodiment progress by 13.4 percentage points, showing that strict and pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer are distinct and should be reported separately. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for evaluating and improving cross-embodiment VLA transfer in stationary tabletop manipulation with two-finger grippers, while motivating future investigation of broader settings including mobile-base control, dexterous hands, and long-horizon tasks.

</details>
