<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变道模型通常关注横向机动执行，但对变道前纵向准备阶段（即目标间隙选择与纵向调整）缺乏清晰的建模参考点和可观测特征，而这一阶段对理解真实自动驾驶车辆的变道决策过程至关重要。

Method: 基于NC-tALC实验的150个受控强制变道样本，定义SigT为横向运动开始前的操作参考点。在该时刻提取相对位置与相对速度特征，采用Firth逻辑回归预测tAV最终是并入最近目标车道车辆前方还是后方；随后将SigT到变道开始期间的纵向推进过程分为原位（in-position）与重新定位（repositioning）两种情形分别分析。

Result: SigT时刻的交通状态包含关于最终目标间隙选择的大量信息，并提供有意义的提前量。模型预测tAV保持当前间隙或前进/后退重新定位到相邻间隙，对纵向重叠及当前间隙几何模糊的情况同样适用，平均五折交叉验证准确率为0.89。原位与重新定位情形在SigT至变道开始的纵向路径上呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究支持可观测变道过程的两阶段猜想：从SigT到变道开始的纵向准备阶段，以及随后的横向机动执行阶段。该表述适用于原位、重新定位及纵向重叠情形，可支持区分目标间隙选择与横向起始时机、并刻画横向运动前纵向准备过程的变道模型。

Abstract: This paper investigates how a production transitional autonomous vehicle (tAV) develops and executes mandatory lane-change decisions. Using 150 controlled mandatory lane changes from the NC-tALC experiments, the study examines whether the eventual target gap is observable before lateral movement begins and how the tAV progresses longitudinally from that pre-lane-change state to lane-change start.
  Signal time (SigT) is defined as an operational pre-lane-change-start reference point. A Firth logistic regression predicts whether the tAV eventually merges in front of or behind its nearest target-lane vehicle using relative position and relative speed at SigT. Longitudinal progression from SigT to lane-change start is then examined separately for in-position and repositioning cases.
  The traffic state at SigT contains substantial information about eventual target-gap choice and provides meaningful lead time before lateral movement begins. The proposed formulation predicts whether the tAV remains with its current gap or repositions to a neighboring gap by moving forward or dropping back, including cases with longitudinal overlap and ambiguous current-gap geometry. The model achieves an average five-fold cross-validated accuracy of 0.89. Results also provide preliminary evidence that in-position and repositioning cases follow different longitudinal pathways from SigT to lane-change start.
  These findings support a two-stage conjecture of the observable lane-change process: longitudinal preparation from SigT to lane-change start, followed by lateral maneuver execution. The formulation applies to in-position, repositioning, and longitudinally overlapping cases, and can support lane-change models that distinguish target-gap choice from lateral-onset timing while representing longitudinal preparation before lateral movement begins.

</details>

Conclusion: Mini-Girona展示了低成本水下自主干预平台的可行性，可作为水下机器人研究与教育的有效工具，有助于推动真实水下应用的创新。

Abstract: The Mini-Girona Intervention Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (I-AUV) represents an advancement in accessible underwater robotics, designed to bridge the gap between costly, specialized research AUVs and basic Remotely Operated Vehicles (ROVs). Developed with a focus on affordability and usability, the Mini-Girona, priced at approximately $50,000, integrates advanced components such as a 5-DOF manipulator arm, stereo vision, and AI-driven processing for autonomous navigation and intervention tasks. This paper presents the design and development of the Mini-Girona, detailing its performance during the RAMI 2025 student competition. Despite challenges such as thermal management issues and restricted team access, the Mini-Girona achieved second place overall, excelling in vision-based perception and intervention tasks. This work highlights the platform's potential as a tool for underwater robotics research and education, fostering innovation in real-world underwater applications.

### [46] [Latent Cluster Analysis for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02634)

TL;DR: 本文提出LAVLA框架，对视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型进行逐层潜在簇分析，并针对GR00T N1.5模型的动作解码器展开研究，通过跨注意力嵌入加权方法提升聚类性能并提取可解释概念，揭示了潜在表征在中间层逐渐解耦时空与运动学特征、输出端趋于稳定的规律。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人领域应用日益广泛，但其内部表征如何驱动行为仍缺乏理解，模型的潜在空间可解释性不足。

Method: 提出LAVLA框架进行VLA模型的潜在簇分析；引入基于跨注意力的嵌入加权方法，放大相关特征并抑制非信息特征；采用定量聚类评估并提取人类可理解的概念来描述各簇。

Result: 加权聚类性能始终优于基线；潜在簇在中间层渐进式解耦时空与运动学特征，表征在中间层更加精细并朝输出端稳定化。

Conclusion: LAVLA推进了语言驱动机器人系统的可解释性研究，为理解VLA模型内部表征演化提供了系统性分析框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models are increasingly used in robotics for their ability to ground language and perception into action, yet the internal representations driving their behaviour remain poorly understood. We propose LAVLA, a framework for latent cluster analysis of VLA models, and conduct a layer-wise study of the state-of-the-art GR00T N1.5 model, with particular focus on its action decoder. To better characterise the latent space during action diffusion, we introduce a cross-attention-based embedding-weighting method that amplifies relevant features while suppressing less informative ones. Quantitative evaluation shows that weighted clustering consistently outperforms the baseline. To improve interpretability, we extract human-interpretable concepts for each cluster, linking latent representations to semantic descriptions. Our analysis shows that latent clusters progressively disentangle spatiotemporal and kinematic features, with representations becoming more refined in the middle layers and stabilising toward the output. As such, LAVLA advances the interpretability of language-driven robotic systems.

</details>


### [47] [HINT: Human-Intent Inception for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02653)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Shihao Jin,Zibo Dai,Qihao Cheng,Zhengrui Lv,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Guang Chen,Xinyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HINT是一个受人类操作原理启发的具身智能体框架，仅在操作模式转换时触发语义推理以解析当前子任务和目标，并通过多视角grounding和视觉跟踪维持该意图，最后通过图像空间语义高亮和注意力先验注入两种视觉接口将跟踪到的意图传递给动作策略，无需在基础动作模型上增加可训练参数。在长时程任务和分布外变体上显著提升了意图理解、任务进度和端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和其他动作策略在密集演化的视觉输入和稀疏语言引导下难以实现高层智能操作行为，因为视觉相关性会主导语义意图，导致动作追随视觉捷径而非人类目标。核心问题在于如何在高频视觉变化与低频语义意图之间取得平衡。

Method: HINT框架的核心设计包括：(1) 仅在操作模式转换处触发语义推理，以解析当前子任务和目标（对应人类语义意图稀疏变化的特性）；(2) 通过多视角grounding和视觉跟踪维持已确立的意图承诺（对应连续控制主要依赖物体-手关系的演化）；(3) 提出两种视觉接口——图像空间语义高亮和注意力先验注入——在不引入额外可训练参数的情况下将跟踪的意图传递给基础动作策略。

Result: 在三个长时程任务及其分布外变体上的实验表明，HINT在两种基础策略上显著提升了意图理解能力、任务进展和端到端成功率，同时保持了低延迟控制。

Conclusion: 通过在语义意图变化的稀疏时间尺度与连续视觉控制的密集时间尺度之间解耦推理，HINT验证了模仿人类操作原理（稀疏语义推理+连续的物手关系跟踪）可以有效缓解视觉短路问题，提升长时程操作中的意图一致性。

Abstract: Humans can perform complex manipulations given a simple intent through an overall instruction, while continuously adapting to evolving visual observations. However, current vision-language action (VLA) models and other action policies struggle to realize this high-level intelligent behavior under dense, evolving visual inputs and sparse language guidance. Visual correlations can then dominate semantic intent, leading actions to follow visual shortcuts rather than human goals. We present HINT (Human-INTent INcepTion), an agentic framework inspired by the human manipulation principles: semantic intent changes sparsely at manipulation-pattern transitions, whereas continuous control primarily depends on the evolving object-hand relationship. HINT invokes semantic reasoning only at pattern transitions to resolve the current subtask and target, then maintains this commitment through multi-view grounding and visual tracking. We explore two visual interfaces-image-space semantic highlighting and attention-prior injection-to communicate the tracked intent to the action policy without introducing additional trainable parameters into the foundation action model. Experiments across three long-horizon tasks and out-of-distribution variants show that HINT substantially improves intent understanding, task progress, and end-to-end success across two foundation policies while preserving low-latency control.

</details>


### [48] [From Proxy Learning to Driving Decisions: A Transfer-Based Framework for Evaluating Future-Aware Autonomous Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02688)
*Yikai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出代理到决策迁移（PDT）框架，用于评估基于未来感知/世界模型的自动驾驶规划器在代理指标上的改进是否真正转化为驾驶性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于未来感知表示的自动驾驶规划器在代理目标或受限子集上的改进常常被直接解读为规划收益，而未验证提议排序、选中的轨迹、全尺度效用及关键驾驶组件，导致性能改进声明缺乏可靠性。

Method: 提出PDT框架，包含两个模块：1）决策迁移分解模块，通过得分边际、开关条件效用和支持-选择遗憾定位价值损失；2）可靠性约束验证模块，要求精确配对、最小有意义效应、尺度扩展确认、安全不可补偿、顺序可比性和族级稳健性。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1上评估代表性未来感知规划器：组件BCE从0.705降至0.530，但被选中的PDM从0.963降至0.961。另一候选在512条前缀上提升0.00909（场景自助95%区间[0.000744, 0.0177]），但2048条与全量数据区间包含零。提议级回放确认了开关效用分解，但432个配置无一通过两半、两种子稳健性门控。

Conclusion: PDT框架能够识别决策迁移在代理、子集、聚合和选择证据层面失败或不确定的具体位置，提醒研究者不能将代理或子集级别的改进直接视为端到端驾驶性能的提升。

Abstract: Future-aware representations and world models are increasingly used in proposal-based autonomous-driving planners to improve trajectory selection. However, improvements in proxy objectives or restricted subsets are often interpreted as planning gains without verifying proposal ordering, selected trajectories, full-scale utility, and critical driving components. We propose the Proxy-to-Decision Transfer (PDT) Framework, an analysis framework that evaluates when learned future information supports a reliable driving-performance improvement claim. Its Decision-Transfer Decomposition Module localizes value loss through score margins, switch-conditioned utility, and support-versus-selection regret. Its Reliability-Constrained Validation Module requires exact pairing, a minimum meaningful effect, scale-expanded confirmation, safety non-compensation, sequential comparability, and family-level robustness. On a representative future-aware planner evaluated with NAVSIM-v1, component BCE decreases from 0.705 to 0.530 while held selected PDM decreases from 0.963 to 0.961. A separate candidate improves a 512-record prefix by 0.00909, with a scene-bootstrap 95% interval of [0.000744, 0.0177], but its 2048-record and complete-support intervals include zero. A proposal-level replay further confirms the switch-utility decomposition, yet none of 432 screened configurations passes the two-half, two-seed robustness gate. PDT therefore identifies where decision transfer fails or remains indeterminate across proxy, subset, aggregate, and selection evidence.

</details>


### [49] [Do Better Imagined Rollouts Mean Better Robot Control? A Controlled Study of World-Model Evaluation Under Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02811)
*Dharini Raghavan,Amritpal Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人在闭环操作中预测模型评估与开环预测评估之间的差异。在差速驱动路径跟踪任务中，对比了三种评估方法（轨迹重放、无测量滚动预测、闭环跟踪）下六种状态估计器的表现。结果表明轨迹重放的错误率与闭环性能的相关性更强（Spearman rho=0.923 vs 0.774），且在选择最优估计器时与闭环结果的一致性更高（错选率5/24 vs 18/24）。进一步研究发现，预测评估的有用性高度依赖于预测时域和测量更新间隔的组合——只有保留定期测量校正时，长预测时域才保持信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人预测模型通常在固定时域的开环预测精度上被评估，但实际应用中机器人在闭环中反复行动、接收新测量、更新状态并进行重计算。这种评估方式与实际使用场景的失配可能导致选错模型，因此需要系统研究评估协议与闭环行为之间的关系。

Method: 实验包含：(1) 在差速驱动路径跟踪任务中，用6种状态估计器在24种传感条件下进行对比；(2) 三种评估协议：轨迹重放、H=20的无测量滚动预测、真实闭环跟踪；(3) 系统变化滚动时域和测量更新间隔，构建时域-更新网格；(4) 训练在更长传感中断下的循环估计器（GRU-EKF等）并比较性能。

Result: 轨迹重放的错误(位置RMSE)与闭环横跨界RMSE的相关性(p=0.923)强于无测量滚动预测(p=0.774)；轨迹重放在24种条件下仅5次选错估计器，而滚动预测错选18次。在H=20时，相关性从每步测量的0.916降到无测量的0.774。训练更长传感中断使GRU-EKF的跨界RMSE从1.72m降至1.06m，但在孤立中断或不同架构下增益不一致。

Conclusion: 机器人预测模型评估必须同时指定预测时域和测量更新调度。对于用于反馈控制的模型，离线滚动预测在感知与校正模式反映闭环操作时最具信息量。评估协议与真实闭环运行方式匹配是选择正确模型的关键。

Abstract: Predictive models are increasingly used in robotics for state estimation, planning, control, and policy evaluation, yet they are often judged by open-loop prediction accuracy over a fixed horizon. In closed-loop operation, a robot repeatedly acts, receives new measurements, updates its state estimate, and recomputes control. We study this difference in a differential-drive path-tracking task with biased odometry and intermittent landmark sensing. Six state estimators are evaluated across 24 sensing conditions using trajectory replay, a 20-step measurement-free rollout, and closed-loop tracking. Replay position RMSE correlates more strongly with closed-loop cross-track RMSE than rollout error (Spearman rho = 0.923 vs. 0.774) and selects a different estimator from the closed-loop optimum in 5/24 conditions, compared with 18/24 for the rollout metric. We then vary rollout horizon and measurement-update interval. With H=20, rank agreement decreases from rho = 0.916 with measurements at every step to rho = 0.774 with no measurements. A horizon-update grid shows that long prediction horizons remain informative when regular corrections are retained, whereas long rollouts without correction can produce rankings that differ substantially from closed-loop behavior. We also test recurrent estimators trained on longer sensing outages. This improves the EKF-anchored models under combined sensing degradation, reducing GRU-EKF cross-track RMSE from 1.72 m to 1.06 m, but the gain is not consistent across isolated outages or estimator architectures. These results show that predictive-model evaluation in robotics should specify both prediction horizon and measurement-update schedule. For models used in feedback, offline rollouts are most informative when their sensing and correction pattern reflects closed-loop operation. Code is available at https://github.com/rdharini2001/Robot_World_Model

</details>


### [50] [Toward Robust LiDAR Semantic Segmentation for Real-World Deployment: Evaluation under Coarse Labels, Adverse Conditions, and Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02830)
*Samir Abou Haidar,Alexandre Chariot,Mehdi Darouich,Cyril Joly,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结构化的评估协议，从粗粒度标签语义、八种LiDAR退化条件下的鲁棒性、以及跨数据集的域泛化三个维度系统评估LiDAR语义分割模型的部署就绪度，并在嵌入式Jetson AGX Orin平台上测量推理速度，揭示基准性能与实际部署能力之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR语义分割评估协议仅关注干净、单域和细粒度标签设定，未能全面评估真实世界系统所需的安全关键标签语义、退化传感条件和跨域变化，缺乏统一的评估标准来评判模型的实际部署就绪度。

Method: 提出一个三维度的结构化评估协议：(i) 与自动驾驶安全优先级对齐的粗标签评估；(ii) 在八类模拟真实世界大气、几何和传感退化条件下的鲁棒性测试；(iii) 无适应的跨数据集域泛化评估。同时测量在嵌入式Jetson AGX Orin平台上的推理速度以反映部署约束。

Result: 研究发现细粒度基准排名并不总能反映安全相关性能；所有方法在退化条件下均出现显著性能下降，且鲁棒性特征具有架构依赖性；当前域泛化能力不足以支撑可靠部署。这些结果暴露了基准性能与部署就绪度之间的具体差距。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个更贴近实际应用的LiDAR语义分割评估参考协议，证明仅依赖标准基准评估不足以判断模型的部署就绪度，需要结合安全语义、退化鲁棒性和域泛化等多维度综合治理。

Abstract: LiDAR-based semantic segmentation is a core perception module for autonomous vehicles and mobile robots. Despite the strong performance of recent state-of-the-art methods on standard benchmarks, existing evaluation protocols remain focused on clean, single-domain settings and fine-grained label taxonomies, leaving deployment readiness largely unassessed. Real-world systems must handle safety-critical label semantics, degraded sensing conditions, and cross-domain variability, yet no unified protocol currently addresses all three aspects together. In this paper, we propose a structured evaluation protocol that assesses the deployment readiness of LiDAR semantic segmentation models along three complementary dimensions: (i) coarse-label evaluation aligned with autonomous driving safety priorities, revealing how label granularity affects different methods; (ii) robustness under eight types of LiDAR corruptions designed to emulate real-world atmospheric, geometric, and sensor degradations; and (iii) domain generalization across datasets without adaptation. The evaluation includes inference speed measured on an embedded Jetson AGX Orin platform, directly reflecting deployment constraints. Our results show that fine-grained benchmark rankings do not always reflect safety-relevant performance, that all methods experience substantial degradation under corruptions with architecture-dependent robustness characteristics, and that current domain generalization remains insufficient for reliable deployment. These findings expose concrete gaps between benchmark performance and deployment readiness, and provide a reference protocol for more practically grounded evaluation of LiDAR semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02861)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种用于自主机器人决策的可审计性架构，通过四层结构化决策流程（语义感知、信念推理、动作合成、执行验证）确保每个自主动作都能通过文档化的因果链追溯到传感器证据。在仓库机器人导航的500个模拟决策周期中，TRACE实现了98.6%的证据可追溯性、99.0%的时间连续性和98.1%的决策可重构性，并满足EU AI Act对高风险系统透明度的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的自主机器人面临根本性的可审计性挑战：当事故发生时，调查人员无法重建系统做出特定决策的原因。现有深度学习系统的黑箱特性使得决策过程无法事后追溯，需要一种能够确保决策级透明度和因果链文档化的结构化决策框架。

Method: TRACE框架将决策过程组织为四个可审计层：1）语义感知层，实现基于证据的实体识别；2）信念推理层，利用因果图进行概率状态估计；3）动作合成层，实现带约束的规划及反事实文档化；4）执行验证层，负责合规性监控。该框架是模型无关的，但设计为可集成基于学习的感知模块（CNN、transformer），同时保持决策级可审计性。实验采用三个客观指标：证据可追溯性（传感器到决策的链接）、决策可重构性（事后分析能力）和时间连续性（审计轨迹完整性）。

Result: 在仓库机器人导航实验中的500个模拟决策周期中，TRACE实现了98.6%的证据可追溯性、99.0%的时间连续性和98.1%的决策可重构性。对照分析显示，LIME等事后方法仅能提供特征归因，缺乏决策级重建所需的工件结构。

Conclusion: TRACE框架解决了自主系统决策级可审计性的核心难题，满足EU AI Act对高风险系统透明度的监管要求，为安全关键型自主系统的可解释AI做出了贡献，确立了传统可解释性方法（如特征归因）与完整的、可审计的因果决策框架之间的重要区分。

Abstract: Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. The framework organizes decision-making into four auditable layers: Semantic Perception for evidence-grounded entity recognition, Belief Reasoning for probabilistic state estimation with causal graphs, Action Synthesis for constraint-aware planning with counterfactual documentation, and Execution Verification for compliance monitoring. TRACE is model-agnostic yet designed to integrate learning-based perception modules (CNNs, transformers) while preserving decision-level auditability. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. Post-hoc methods like LIME provide feature attributions but lack the artifact structure needed for decision-level reconstruction. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验首次搜索了D_s*+ → p n̄ 的重子衰变过程，在4.128至4.226 GeV质心能量下、对应7.33 fb^-1积分亮度的e+e-对撞数据中未发现显著信号，并在90%置信水平上设定了分支比上限为1.3×10^-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次对D_s*+介子的重子衰变模式进行搜索，这是此前尚未被研究过的物理过程，可为理解D_s*+介子的衰变机制和重子产生提供重要信息。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的e+e-对撞数据，实验覆盖4.128至4.226 GeV的质心能量范围，总积分亮度为7.33 fb^-1，对D_s*+ → p n̄衰变过程进行搜索分析。

Result: 未观察到显著信号，在90%置信水平下设定该衰变过程的分支比上限为1.3×10^-4。

Conclusion: D_s*+ → p n̄ 衰变在现有统计数据内未被观测到，其分支比被限定在1.3×10^-4以下（90%置信水平），为该稀有重子衰变过程提供了首次实验约束。

Abstract: The first search for the baryonic decay $ D_{s}^{*+} \to \ p \bar{n} $ is performed using $e^+e^-$ collision data taken at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV, collected by the BESIII experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$. No significant signal is observed, and an upper limit on the branching fraction is set to be $1.3\times 10^{-4}$ at the $90\%$ confidence level.

</details>


### [53] [Observation of $ψ(3686)\to p K^- K_S^0 \bar Ξ^0+c.c.$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02051)

Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约27.12亿ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到ψ(3686)→pK⁻K_S⁰Ξ̄⁰+c.c.衰变，统计显著性为11.5σ，并测得该衰变的分支比为(2.84±0.40±0.25)×10⁻⁶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在扩展稀有多奇异数ψ(3686)衰变的实验信息，为相关衰变模式提供实验参考。此类稀有衰变有助于深入理解粲偶素衰变机制及其中的奇异夸克动力学。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的(2.712±0.014)×10⁹个ψ(3686)事例样本，对该稀有衰变道进行搜索与测量。通过衰变运动学重建和信号与背景分离技术，确定信号事例数并计算统计显著性。

Result: 首次观测到ψ(3686)→pK⁻K_S⁰Ξ̄⁰+c.c.衰变，统计显著性为11.5σ。测得分支比为(2.84±0.40±0.25)×10⁻⁶，其中第一项不确定度为统计误差，第二项为系统误差。

Conclusion: 该实验测量首次确立了这一稀有多奇异数衰变道的存在及其分支比，填补了该领域的实验空白，并为未来相关衰变模式的研究提供了重要实验参照。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2.712 \pm 0.014) \times 10^{9}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the decay of $ψ(3686)\to p K^- K_S^0 \bar Ξ^0+c.c.$ is observed for the first time with a statistical significance of $11.5σ$. The branching fraction of this decay is measured to be $(2.84\pm 0.40\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-6}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This measurement extends the experimental information on rare multi-strange $ψ(3686)$ decays and provides an experimental reference for future studies of related decay modes.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb探测器在2015–2018年收集的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量13 TeV，积分亮度6 fb⁻¹），通过B_s⁰介子衰变到D_s⁻π⁺态的干涉效应，检验CPT和洛伦兹不变性的基本对称性。在标准模型扩展框架下，对洛伦兹破缺参数Δa_μ的灵敏度达到10⁻¹⁴ GeV量级；在不假设洛伦兹不变量破坏时，测得CPT破缺参数z的实部为0.018±0.016±0.003，虚部为0.003±0.004±0.002，这是对B_s⁰系统中CPT破坏的最精确约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本对称性（如CPT和洛伦兹不变性）的检验是探测超越标准量子场论框架的新物理的灵敏探针。任何对这些对称性的违反都可能暗示存在更深层次的物理规律。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在2015–2018年收集的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量13 TeV，积分亮度6 fb⁻¹），研究B_s⁰介子衰变到D_s⁻π⁺态的干涉效应，并在标准模型扩展（SME）框架下对结果进行解释。

Result: 对洛伦兹破缺参数Δa_μ的灵敏度达到O(10⁻¹⁴) GeV水平；测得CPT破缺参数Re(z)=0.018±0.016±0.003和Im(z)=0.003±0.004±0.002（第一项为统计不确定度，第二项为系统不确定度），结果为目前对B_s⁰系统中CPT破坏的最精确约束。

Conclusion: 该研究未发现CPT或洛伦兹对称性破坏的显著证据，但提供了目前对B_s⁰系统中CPT破坏的最精确测量，为超越标准模型的新物理探索设定了更严格的实验边界。

Abstract: Tests of fundamental symmetries, such as $C\!PT$ and Lorentz invariance, provide sensitive probes of physics beyond the standard quantum-field-theory framework. Possible violations of these symmetries are investigated through interference effects in decays of $B_s^0$ mesons to $D_s^-π^{+}$ states. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector in 2015--2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of $13~\mathrm{TeV}$, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The results are interpreted within the Standard Model Extension framework, yielding sensitivities to the Lorentz-violating parameter $Δa_μ$ at the ${\cal O}(10^{-14})\,\mathrm{GeV}$ level. Without assuming Lorentz-invariance violation, the $C\!PT$-violating parameter $z$ in the $B_s^0$ system is measured to be $\mathcal{R}e(z) = 0.018 \pm 0.016 \pm 0.003$ and $\mathcal{I}m(z) = 0.003 \pm 0.004 \pm 0.002$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. These results represent the most precise constraints on $C\!PT$ violation in the $B_s^0$ system.

</details>

Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验利用约100亿J/ψ衰变产生的Λ和反Λ超子作为有效束流，与束流管材料发生非弹性散射，首次测量了Λ+p→Σ⁰+p和反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p的截面，并首次观测到反超子非弹性散射（重子末态）证据，显著性为3.1σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超子-核子及反超子-核子相互作用对于理解强相互作用的基本性质具有重要意义，但长期以来缺乏实验数据，尤其是反超子散射数据几乎空白。作者希望通过利用BESIII探测器的高统计量J/ψ样本，以独特的"超子束流"方案直接测量这些稀有散射过程。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的(10087±44)×10⁶个J/ψ事件，通过J/ψ→Λ反Λ衰变产生超子作为有效束流，束流管材料（铍和油中的氢）作为靶。分析两类过程：(1) 从超子与⁹Be靶的散射（Λ+⁹Be→Σ⁰+p+⁸Li）在超子动量1.074 GeV/c下测量截面，并通过表面主导散射假设间接提取质子靶截面；(2) 利用束流管油中氢成分直接测量Λ+p→Σ⁰+p和反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p截面。最终将两种方法结果合并。

Result: 在超子动量1.074 GeV/c下，超子-铍散射截面为(10.1±1.4±0.7) mb（Λ）和(1.7±0.6±0.4) mb（反Λ）。直接质子靶测量为(3.2±1.1±0.5) mb（Λ+p→Σ⁰+p）和(1.5±0.5±0.1) mb（反Λ+p→反Σ⁰+p），与间接提取一致。合并截面分别为(4.7±0.7) mb和(1.1±0.3) mb。反Λ散射信号显著性3.1σ，为反超子非弹性散射（重子末态）的首个证据。Λp与反Λp非弹性散射截面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供了反超子-质子非弹性散射的实验证据，揭示了Λp与反Λp散射截面之间的显著不对称性（比例约4:1），为超子-核子和反超子-核子相互作用的理论研究提供了新的关键实验约束，也展示了BESIII实验作为一种新型"超子散射设施"的研究能力。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(10087\pm44)\times10^{6}$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we investigate the inelastic scattering processes $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, exploiting hyperons from $J/ψ\toΛ\overlineΛ$ decays as an effective beam and the beam-pipe materials as targets. The processes $Λ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\toΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ and $\overlineΛ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ are measured at a hyperon momentum of $1.074~\mathrm{GeV}/c$, with cross sections of $(10.1\pm1.4_{\rm stat}\pm0.7_{\rm syst})$ mb and $(1.7\pm0.6_{\rm stat}\pm0.4_{\rm syst})$ mb, respectively. Under the assumption of surface-dominated hyperon-nucleus scattering, these measurements are used to extract the corresponding proton-target cross sections. Independently, direct measurements using the hydrogen component of the beam-pipe oil yield $(3.2\pm1.1_{\rm stat}\pm0.5_{\rm syst})$ mb for $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $(1.5\pm0.5_{\rm stat}\pm0.1_{\rm syst})$ mb for $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, consistent with the indirect determinations. The combined cross sections are $(4.7\pm0.7)$ mb and $(1.1\pm0.3)$ mb, respectively. The $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$ signal constitutes the first evidence for anti-hyperon inelastic scattering with baryonic final states, with a significance of $3.1σ$. The pronounced difference between the $Λp$ and $\overlineΛp$ inelastic scattering cross sections provides new experimental constraints on hyperon-nucleon and anti-hyperon-nucleon interactions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用LZ实验2.84吨·年的曝光数据，将核反冲能量窗口扩展至约270 keV（高于名义自旋无关搜索窗口），并采用剖面似然比检验进行统计推断，同时构建详细的背景模型并考虑多种稀有背景拓扑结构。

Result: 观测到1个能量为248±23(stat)±23(sys) keV的核反冲事件，该事件位于已知背景预期较低的区域。剖面似然比检验显示与背景唯假设存在张力：计入look-elsewhere效应后全局显著性为2.6σ，在测试模型中出现最大局部显著性为3.4σ。

Conclusion: 该观测事件与背景唯假设存在中等程度的偏差（2.6σ全局），但尚未达到发现暗物质的显著性阈值。这一结果值得关注，为未来更高曝光量下验证该信号是否来自暗物质相互作用提供了依据。

Abstract: We report on a search for dark matter particles interacting with xenon nuclei in an exposure of 2.84 tonne-years with the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment. An extended nuclear recoil energy window up to approximately 270 keV enables searches for effective field theory and inelastic models of dark matter where high-energy recoils account for a larger fraction of the predicted recoil spectrum compared to the nominal spin independent interaction. We observe one event with characteristics consistent with a nuclear recoil of $248\pm23\,\mathrm{(stat)}\pm23\,\mathrm{(sys)}$ keV, in a region where the known background expectation is low. A profile likelihood ratio test finds tension with the background-only hypothesis at a global significance of 2.6$σ$ when accounting for look-elsewhere effects, with a maximum local significance of 3.4$σ$ across the models tested. We describe the analysis and event of interest, the background model used in the statistical inference, and detail several of the rare background topologies considered.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波腔轴子探测器（haloscope）受标准量子极限（SQL）的约束，即相位保持的线性放大对于一个窄带信号至少引入一个量子的总系统噪声。由于轴子信号本质上是非单色的宽带信号，研究者希望探索能否利用这一特性突破传统SQL的限制，提高轴子探测灵敏度。

Method: 操作约瑟夫森参量放大器（JPA），将其配置为使腔共振频率精确位于半泵浦频率处。在这种配置下，轴子信号对称地占据信号带与闲频带两个频段。通过对零差读出的正交分量（quadrature）分析，将镜像的频谱分量进行非相干叠加，从而使测量到的信号功率翻倍，而真空噪声保持恒定。

Result: 该操作实现了每频率仓0.5个量子的有效噪声极限，经过最优匹配滤波后等效于1/√2个量子的总有效极限，即相比于传统标准量子极限减半。

Conclusion: 该研究证明对于非单色轴子信号，通过在JPA中利用信号与闲频带的对称分布，可以有效将标准量子极限减半，为提升微波腔轴子暗物质探测灵敏度提供了新的理论框架与实验路径。

Abstract: The search for wave-like dark matter using microwave cavity haloscopes is constrained by the Standard Quantum Limit, which dictates that phase-preserving linear amplification results in a minimum of one quantum of total system noise for a narrow-band signal. We demonstrate that this limit is effectively halved for a non-monochromatic axion signal coupled to a high-$Q$ cavity. By operating a Josephson Parametric Amplifier such that the cavity resonance is centered exactly at the half-pump frequency, the axion signal symmetrically populates both the signal and idler bands. Through quadrature analysis of the homodyne readout, we show that the incoherent sum of these mirrored spectral components doubles the measured signal power while the vacuum noise remains constant. This operation yields an effective noise limit of 0.5 quanta per frequency bin, translating to an overall effective limit of $1/\sqrt{2}$ quanta after optimal matched filtering.

</details>

### [58] [Extended Body Propagators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01829)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明Ansoldi等人的延展体传播子对应一个具有特定概率密度函数$f(A)=C\,A\,(α^{2}+A^{2})^{-D/2}\mathbf{1}_{A>0}$的随机变量的特征函数，并给出了精确的指数、尺度等参数。作者认为该观测和构建的面积测度是新颖的，并进一步论证该面积测度恰为三角形面积的分布律（其中两条边由一维Polyakov积分伴生手稿中测度抽取），最后给出了将管状世界面测度约化为该三角形测度的论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三种表面上迥异的延展体传播子（由Ansoldi等人、Erbin等人和Stanford等人提出）各自产生于完全不同的场景，但都可看作Polyakov路径积分的一种实现。作者希望厘清它们之间的联系，特别是揭示Ansoldi传播子的概率论本质，并将其面积测度推广到二维单纯复形上的路径空间测度。

Method: 作者证明Ansoldi传播子等价于上述概率密度函数所对应随机变量的特征函数；通过Ansoldi/几何比较固定指数为环境维度D（别无其他参数），并对比Erbin等人的on-shell两点振幅和JT引力与其D=4情形一致。作者进一步证明该面积测度精确地等于一个三角形面积的分布律，其中两条边来自伴生手稿中一维Polyakov积分的测度；最后给出一个部分严格的论证，将两条任意边界曲线间的管状世界面测度约化为该三角形测度。

Result: 确定了Ansoldi传播子的概率测度身份（尺度α=√(b/2)的幂律分布的特征函数），确认了其D=4情形与Erbin等人的on-shell两点振幅及JT引力的一致性；建立了该面积测度与一维Polyakov积分面积测度的精确关系（三角形面积定律），并实现了管状世界面测度的降维约化。作者经文献综述后认为该观测与面积测度构建是新颖的。

Conclusion: 该面积测度将成为早前非延展体路径空间测度向二维单纯复形扩展的自然候选；文中给出的三角形面积解释与管状世界面约化论证共同为该研究方向提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: Three seemingly distinct extended-body propagators devised by Ansoldi et al., Erbin et al., and Stanford et al.\ respectively arise in completely different settings, though each can be considered a realization of the Polyakov path integral. This paper proves that the Ansoldi propagator corresponds to the characteristic function of the random variable with the probability density function $f(A)=C\,A\,(α^{2}+A^{2})^{-D/2}\mathbf{1}_{A>0}$ in ambient dimension $D$, of scale $α=\sqrt{b/2}$. The exponent is fixed by the dimension and by nothing else in the Ansoldi/geometric comparison; the on-shell two-point amplitude of Erbin et al. and JT gravity agree with its $D=4$ member in the senses made precise below. After an extensive literature review, the author believes that this observation and constructed area measure is novel. The area measure will, however, emerge as the natural candidate for the extension of the earlier non-extended body path space measures to two dimensional simplicial complexes. We further show that the area measure of this paper is exactly the law of the area of a triangle, two of whose sides are drawn from the measure obtained in the companion manuscripts for the one-dimensional Polyakov integral. We close with an argument, rigorous in part, which reduces the measure of tubular worldsheets between two arbitrary boundary curves to that of this triangle.

</details>

Result: 成功获得了Biot-Savart定律中卷积核的傅里叶变换的闭式解析解，并在此基础上给出了傅里叶变换后的电流密度与傅里叶变换后的磁场数据之间的显式函数关系，为二维磁反演问题提供了一条完整的频域求解路径。

Conclusion: 本文为该数学方法提供了清晰、完整的统一阐述，系统澄清了卷积定理方法在二维磁反演问题中的应用细节，使后续研究者能够更直接地引用和实现该方法。

Abstract: A collection and clarification is given of the key mathematical details of the method of applying the convolution theorem as part of the solution to the two-dimensional magnetic inverse problem: reconstructing current density from magnetic field data. Particular emphasis is given to obtaining a closed-form solution for the Fourier transform of the convolution kernel that features in the Biot-Savart law. Having done this, the Fourier-transformed current density is obtained, via the convolution theorem, as a function of Fourier-transformed magnetic field data and other variables.

</details>


### [60] [Fractional oscillators with fractional damping in the presence of external forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01883)
*Fernando Olivar-Romero,Oscar Rosas-Ortiz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带有显式分数阶阻尼项的分数阶振子对外部力的响应。对应的三参数Caputo分数阶微分方程具有精确解，其中两个分数参数与内部耗散机制相关，第三个参数表征阻尼项。研究表明外部驱动力决定了系统的长期行为：恒定力下系统达到固定位置，正弦力下系统持续振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分数阶阻尼振子在外部驱动力下的动力学响应机制，特别是明确分数阶阻尼项时系统如何演化，以及外部力如何影响系统长期行为。

Method: 采用三参数Caputo分数阶微分方程建模，通过Laplace变换获得精确解。外部力的Laplace变换定义了解的轮廓，具体分析了无力、恒定力、正弦力和阶跃力四种情形。

Result: 恒定驱动力下系统经过瞬态后达到固定位置；正弦驱动力下系统表现出持续振荡行为。外部驱动力决定了分数阶阻尼振子的长期行为。

Conclusion: 外部驱动力从根本上决定了分数阶阻尼振子的长期动力学特征，且该模型与牛顿-斯科特-布莱尔粘弹性材料的动力学定律相关联。

Abstract: We investigate how a fractional oscillator reacts to external forces when the dynamic law includes a damping term that is explicitly fractional. The corresponding three-parametric fractional differential equation (in the Caputo sense) admits exact solution. Two of the fractional parameters are associated with the intrinsic dissipation mechanism that produces continuous dissipation of energy. The third fractional parameter characterizes the damping term against which the oscillator is driven by the external force. When external time-dependent forces are included, the fractional dynamic law is associated with the Newton-Scott-Blair model of viscoelastic materials. The Laplace transform of the external force defines the profile of the solution; specific examples include the absence of external forces as well as constant, sinusoidal and stepped external forces. With a constant driving force, the system reaches a fixed position after a transient period. With a sinusoidal driving force, the system exhibits persistent oscillatory behavior. These results suggest that the external driving force determines the long-term behavior of the fractionally damped oscillator.

</details>


### [61] [On the Convergence of Metadynamics with Gaussian Hills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01934)
*Andrey Badanin,Olga Rogacheva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于一维集体变量的元动力学（metadynamics）增强采样方法，将其演化问题约化为复制子型微分方程并分析长期行为。结果表明：周期性集体变量下元动力学可以收敛，但定义在有限区间上的集体变量只能以弱意义（有限原子测度）形式存在平稳解，导致缺乏清晰准稳态，元动力学方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元动力学是分子建模中广泛使用的增强采样方法，通常使用高斯核函数。作者希望基于已有的收敛性结果，深入探讨一维集体变量下使用高斯核的元动力学方法的局限性，从数学理论层面理解其长期行为的根本限制。

Method: 将元动力学的演化问题约化为复制子型微分方程（replicator-type differential equation），并通过分析该方程在周期性和有限区间两类不同域上的长时间行为（长期收敛性、平稳解的存在性与弱收敛性）来揭示元动力学的理论特性。

Result: （1）对于周期性集体变量，证明了元动力学的收敛性；（2）对于定义在有限区间上的集体变量，平稳解仅以弱意义存在（作为有限原子测度），微分方程的解弱收敛于该测度；（3）由于缺乏清晰的准稳态，元动力学在该情形下无效；（4）只有在区间外部自由能保持近常数且范围足够大（相对于σ）时，才能在"INTERVAL"框架下捕捉到准稳态的瞬态解。

Conclusion: 周期性集体变量下的元动力学理论上是收敛的，但有限区间情形下元动力学缺乏明确的准稳态，因此实际应用中效果不佳。要使元动力学在有限区间场景中有效，需要满足区间外部自由能近似平坦且区域足够宽（与高斯宽度σ可比）的条件。

Abstract: Metadynamics is a class of enhanced-sampling methods that is widely used in molecular modeling. Here, we consider metadynamics with a one-dimensional collective variable and explore the limitations of using Gaussian hills in light of existing convergence results. We reduce the corresponding evolution problem to a replicator-type differential equation and analyze its long-time behavior. For a periodic collective variable, we prove the convergence of metadynamics. However, the situation is fundamentally different when the collective variable is defined on a finite interval. In this case, the stationary solution only exists in the weak sense as a finite atomic measure. The solution to the differential equation weakly converges to it. Consequently, metadynamics is ineffective due to the absence of a clear quasi-stationary state. A quasi-stationary, transient solution can only be captured in the "INTERVAL" framework if the free energy outside the finite interval remains nearly constant across sufficiently large regions compared to $σ$.

</details>


### [62] [Superintegrability of stratified symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01953)
*Zhuo Chen,Kai Jiang,Nicolai Reshetikhin,Husileng Xiao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文定义了分层辛空间上哈密顿系统的超可积性概念，并聚焦于自旋Calogero-Moser-Sutherland（sCMS）系统——其相空间是通过紧李群余切丛的哈密顿约化得到的分层辛空间。论文证明了SU(3)情形的sCMS系统是超可积的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将超可积性的概念推广到分层辛空间这一更一般的几何框架中，并验证自旋Calogero-Moser-Sutherland系统在这一框架下的超可积性质，从而深化对这类经典可积系统的理解。

The associated spectral gap is 2 mu + O(1). Second, using the discrete Agmon comparison method and the Paley-Wiener theorem, we establish exponential localization of the ground-state wavefunction with a logarithmic upper bound on the decay rate alpha(mu) at most ln(3 mu) + O(1/mu), reflecting the bounded nature of the lattice dispersion; the sharp asymptotic rate is conjectured. Third, at quasimomentum pi, the parity symmetry is preserved, but the odd quadratic form becomes positive definite with a unique eigenvalue of order 1/mu that never reaches the Birman-Schwinger threshold, so the odd subspace yields only a virtual level. The even subspace supports exactly one bound state with energy -2 mu + 6 + 8/mu + O(1/mu^2), and the spectral gap to the essential spectrum is mu - 2 - 8/mu + O(1/mu^2). The reduction from two bound states at zero to one at pi preserves the total spectral flow and is a lattice-specific phenomenon. Main category: math-ph

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于亚椭圆Itô扩散的Kolmogorov生成元的Ruelle-Pollicott谱理论。将不变统计量对RP谱块的灵敏度分解为谱分母（spectral denominator）与残差（residue）的乘积，前者连接谱块与观测及扰动方向。对亚稳态系统，通过一个"被杀问题"（killed problem）得到两个互补的谱对象：Doob Q过程用于刻画阱内恢复，逃逸时钟与committor加权目的概率用于阱间迁移。同时引入索引N(f)，对可逆动力学满足N(f)≤1，因此N(f)>1可作为残差驱动放大的判定标准。

Result: 证明了RP谱隙闭合本身不足以构成预警信号，而残差增长可以在谱隙完全不变的情况下产生经典预警特征。在随机非正态系统中，RP谱严格冻结但经典指标比真正谱隙闭合时更令人警惕，RP分解将此归因于残差增长。在一维鞍结分岔（fold）中，Doob过程相关量以(εc-ε)^{1/2}和(εc-ε)^{3/2}标度，可区分分岔诱导与噪声诱导的临界转变。Doob漂移还与最优Girsanov采样相关联。

Conclusion: 早期预警信号是三元组的属性：RP谱块、观测项和扰动方向三者的共同作用，而非单纯依赖于谱隙变化。RP理论框架提供了区分分岔诱导与噪声诱导转变的能力，并为非正态、亚稳态系统提供了严格的预警理论基础。

Abstract: Tipping points---abrupt, potentially irreversible reorganizations of a system's statistical state---are commonly anticipated through critical slowing down: recovery slows, autocorrelation and variance rise, and spectra redden. This paradigm is powerful near simple equilibrium bifurcations but is not a general theory for stochastic, multistable, or metastable systems. We develop such a theory from the Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) spectrum of Kolmogorov generators of hypoelliptic Itô diffusions. Resolving the sensitivity of invariant statistics on RP spectral blocks shows that each contribution factorizes into a spectral denominator and a residue coupling the block to both the observable and perturbation direction. Small denominators permit large responses; residues produce them. Thus a closing RP gap is not sufficient for an early warning, while growing residues can generate the classical signature with no gap closure. An early-warning signal is therefore a property of a triple: RP block, observable, and perturbation direction. We demonstrate this on a stochastic non-normal system whose RP spectrum is exactly frozen, yet classical indicators become more alarming than during genuine gap closure. The RP decomposition attributes this to residue growth and yields an index $\mathcal{N}(f)$ satisfying $\mathcal{N}(f)\leq 1$ for reversible dynamics; hence $\mathcal{N}(f)>1$ certifies residue-driven amplification. For metastable systems, one killed problem yields two complementary spectral objects: the Doob $Q$-process isolates in-well recovery, while escape clocks and committor-weighted destination probabilities govern interwell transitions. In a one-dimensional fold, these scale as $(ε_c-ε)^{1/2}$ and $(ε_c-ε)^{3/2}$, separating bifurcation-induced from noise-induced tipping. Doob drift also connects to optimal Girsanov sampling.

</details>


### [67] [Asymptotic long-range order for the XY-model on random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02618)
*Margherita Disertori,Max Mihailescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机几何图上的经典XY模型（旋转模型），在温度趋于零（β→∞）且速率取决于n和ε的条件下，证明了该模型在随机几何图上展示出长程有序，即两点函数存在非零下界。结果在随机环境意义上是淬火的（quenched），即随着n→∞，长程有序以趋于1的概率成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机几何图通过采样独立点并按距离连接边来捕获底层域Ω的几何结构，是连接随机图与连续介质的重要模型。研究其上的经典自旋系统（如XY模型）的性质，特别是是否存在长程有序，有助于理解几何随机环境对统计力学相变行为的影响。

Method: 证明分为两个关键步骤：（1）证明在n→∞且ε→0足够慢时，随机几何图环境具有高概率的足够正则性；（2）利用凸性论证（convexity argument）和Brascamp–Lieb不等式，在β→∞且速率与n、ε相关的条件下推导两点函数的下界，从而建立长程有序。结果采用淬火意义下的概率陈述。

Result: 在适当的参数收敛速率下，XY模型在随机几何图G_{n,ε}上满足长程有序：两点函数存在一个与零分离的下界。该结果对随机环境是淬火的，即在n→∞时以概率趋近于1成立。

Conclusion: 文章证明了随机几何图上的XY模型在低温极限下存在长程有序，且这种性质在随机环境上以高概率整体成立。使用的Brascamp–Lieb不等式与凸性方法为处理此类非规则随机图上的经典自旋系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We study the classical $XY$-model on random geometric graphs $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$, which are obtained by sampling $n \in \mathbb{N}$ independent points in a finite domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, $d \geq 2$, and connecting two points by and edge if their distance is of order $\varepsilon > 0$. We refer to $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ as the random environment. Letting $\varepsilon \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$ at a sufficiently slow rate, these graphs capture the geometry of $Ω$.
  Denoting the inverse temperature by $β$, we show that in the limit $β\to \infty$ at a rate depending on $n$ and $\varepsilon$, the $XY$-model on $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ exhibits long range order in the sense that we prove a lower bound away from zero on the two-point function. Our result is quenched in the random environment: long-range order holds with large probability, converging to one as $n \to \infty$. To prove the statement, we show that with high probability the environment is sufficiently regular to apply a convexity argument and the Brascamp--Lieb inequality.

</details>

### [68] [New strategies for some pursuit-evasion differential games with Gronwall-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01922)
*Jamilu Adamu,Mehdi Salimi,Farouq Abba Wabi,Jewaidu Rilwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列空间l₂中的非合作追逃微分对策，其控制函数受Gronwall型约束（对控制资源施加依赖于历史的上界）。针对追击问题构造了新策略并给出追击完成的充分条件；针对躲避问题给出了逃避者保证不被捕获的 admissible 策略及成功躲避的充分条件。该方法将经典几何和积分约束下的微分对策结果推广到更广泛的Gronwall型约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微分对策通常假设控制函数受几何约束（如范数有界）或积分约束，而这些约束无法刻画控制资源消耗具有历史依赖性（记忆效应）的实际场景。本文旨在将追逃微分对策理论拓展到Gronwall型约束（历史依赖有界）的更一般框架下，从而覆盖更广泛的实际系统。

Method: (1) 将追逃博弈建模为l₂序列空间中的一阶微分方程，控制函数受Gronwall型约束；(2) 对追击问题：构造新的追击策略，推导保证追击完成的充分条件；(3) 对躲避问题：构建逃避者的admissible策略以保证避免捕获，并导出成功躲避的充分条件。

Result: 分别针对追击与躲避问题建立了充分的成功条件，构造了相应的追击策略和逃避策略，并证明所提方法将几何与积分约束下的微分对策结果推广至Gronwall型限制的更广系统类。

Conclusion: 论文成功将经典微分对策在几何/积分约束下取得的成果推广至Gronwall型历史依赖约束框架，为l₂空间中受资源历史限制的追逃博弈提供了系统性的可解性分析。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of non-cooperative pursuit-evasion differential games in the sequence space $l_{2}.$ The motions of the pursuer and the evader are described by certain first-order differential equations while their control functions are subject to Gronwall-type constraints which impose history-dependent bounds on their control resources. Two fundamental problems are investigated. For the pursuit problem, new strategy for the pursuer is constructed and sufficient conditions guaranteeing the completion of pursuit are established. For evasion problem, an admissible strategy of the evader ensuring avoidance of capture is obtained, and corresponding sufficient conditions for successful evasion are derived. The proposed approached extends existing results on differential games with classical geometric and integral constraints to a broader class of systems governed by Gronwall-type restrictions.

</details>


### [69] [A Hereditary Property of Cutting Plane Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02038)
*Gérard Cornuéjols,Vrishabh Patil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了切割平面程序在凸集闭包下的"遗传性质"（hereditary property），即对K的每个面F有F' = K' ∩ F。作者给出了该性质成立的两个充分条件：第一个适用于可由一族无格凸集的交集割表达的闭包程序（涵盖split、lift-and-project、Lovász–Schrijver、Sherali–Adams和Lasserre闭包）；第二个适用于割为Gomory角多面体有效不等式的程序（涵盖全基Dantzig割闭包）。同时证明了四个经典程序中遗传性质不成立，包括Gomory分数割闭包、混合整数Chvátal闭包、+割闭包以及可行基Dantzig割闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传性质是切割平面闭包研究中的基础性概念，它支撑着有限秩与闭包多面体性的归纳证明，也是抽象切割平面框架中的可容许性要求。然而，该性质尚未在各类经典切割平面程序中得到系统研究。厘清哪些程序具备遗传性质及其成立条件，对于理解切割平面方法的内在结构和理论边界具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先定义切割平面程序闭包的遗传性质，然后给出了两个充分条件定理。第一个充分条件针对可由一族无格凸集的交集割表达的闭包，要求存在单个无格凸集族同时在K及其每个面上实现闭包，从而证明split、lift-and-project、Lovász–Schrijver、Sherali–Adams和Lasserre闭包在一般凸集上（包括非暴露面）具有遗传性质。第二个充分条件针对割为Gomory角多面体有效不等式的程序，得到全基Dantzig割闭包的遗传性质。此外，通过构造反例证明四种经典程序的遗传性质不成立。

Result: 证明了两个遗传性质的充分条件，确认了六类闭包程序（split、lift-and-project、Lovász–Schrijver、Sherali–Adams、Lasserre以及全基Dantzig割）满足遗传性质；同时证明了四类程序（Gomory分数割闭包、混合整数Chvátal闭包、+割闭包、可行基Dantzig割闭包）不满足该性质。

Conclusion: 该研究首次对切割平面程序的遗传性质进行了系统分类刻画，建立了充分条件理论框架，清晰划分了满足与不满足该性质的程序类别，为后续切割平面闭包的秩理论和多面体性研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Let $K'$ denote the closure of a convex set $K$ under a given cutting-plane procedure. The procedure satisfies the \emph{hereditary property} if $F' = K' \cap F$ for every face $F$ of $K$. The property underlies inductive proofs of finite-rank and the polyhedrality of closures, and it is an admissibility requirement in abstract frameworks for cutting-plane procedures. Yet, it has not been studied systematically across well-known cutting-plane procedures. We establish two sufficient conditions for the property. The first applies to procedures that can be expressed as closures under intersection cuts from a family of lattice-free convex sets, when a single family realizes the closure of $K$ and of each of its faces. It yields the hereditary property for the split, lift-and-project, Lovász--Schrijver, Sherali--Adams, and Lasserre closures, applied over general convex sets and for faces that need not be exposed. Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic optimal control problem under inside information. The control process depends on an $\mathcal{F}_{T_0}$-measurable random variable $Y$, representing the static inside information, and is adapted to the enlarged filtration generated by the underlying Brownian motion and the random variable $Y$. Accordingly, the traditional stochastic integral fails to be well-defined in this non-adapted setting; we adopt forward integrals to formulate the stochastic integral terms in the system. By means of the Donsker delta function, the original controlled system is transformed into a $y$-parameterized system. We further establish the existence of solutions to forward \textit{stochastic differential equations} (SDEs), and prove the uniqueness of solutions via flow transformation techniques. Under a Gaussian assumption on $Y$, we derive both necessary and sufficient optimality conditions for the aforementioned control problem. Subsequently, we formulate the \textit{linear-quadratic} (LQ) optimal control problem under inside information. Through the $y$-parameterized transformation, the original problem is converted into an LQ control problem with random coefficients. A numerical example for the LQ case is provided at the end to validate our theoretical findings.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文填补了有向图最小循环基领域多个关键的理论空白：既解决了前向循环基的存在性与结构问题，又在计算复杂度层面给出了精确的刻画（一边是多项式可解，另一边是APX-hard）；同时通过引入opt-in图族，将整数性保证的研究推进到minor-closed的框架中，为该方向的后续研究提供了新的工具与视角。

Abstract: The cycle space of a directed graph is generated by a cycle basis, where, in general, cycles are allowed to have both forward and backward arcs. In a forward cycle, all arcs must follow the given direction. Several open questions remain regarding the complexity of the minimum cycle basis problem, in particular the minimum-weight integral cycle basis problem, and the minimum-weight weakly and strictly fundamental forward cycle basis problems. In this paper, we address these open questions. First, we study the existence, structure, and computational complexity of minimum-weight forward cycle bases. We give a complete structural characterization of digraphs that admit weakly fundamental (and hence integral) forward cycle bases. We further provide a characterization when a strongly connected digraph admits a forward fundamental cycle basis, proving that such a basis exists if and only if the set of directed cycles has cardinality equal to the cycle rank; in this case, the basis is unique. Lastly, we show that while minimum-weight forward fundamental cycle bases can be found in polynomial time whenever they exist, the minimum-weight forward weakly fundamental cycle basis problem is APX-hard via an L-reduction from the minimum-weight weakly fundamental cycle basis problem on digraphs with metric weights. Second, we introduce opt-in graphs, i.e., the family of graphs for which minimum cycle bases are integral for any weight function. We show that this family is minor-closed and hence, by the Robertson-Seymour theorem, is characterized by a finite set of forbidden minors, so that the opt-in recognition problem is solvable in polynomial time. Lastly, we present an algorithm to check whether a graph is opt-in, and if not, to identify which of its minors belong to the set of forbidden minors. Applying this algorithm, we show that the complete graph $K_n$ is opt-in if and only if $n \leq 7$.

</details>

Abstract: In this lecture note, we present a concise and self-contained derivation of the discrete-time Kalman filter equations that requires only a basic understanding of least squares estimation. Abstract: This paper shows that the optimal gain of the risk-sensitive linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem is invariant under input randomization, i.e., when the controller deliberately injects noise into the nominal control input. This appears counterintuitive at first glance because certainty equivalence does not hold for risk-sensitive LQR and input randomization inflates the effective process noise. Nonetheless, the gain is preserved because the input noise enters not only the system dynamics but also the cost functional, and its total effect on the gain eventually vanishes. Consequently, the optimal gain and its associated Riccati recursion need not be recomputed, and the increment in the optimal cost can be readily evaluated in closed form. This result facilitates the use of risk-sensitive LQR in applications that employ input randomization for privacy or exploration, such as watermarking for replay attack detection, differential privacy, and path integral control.

</details>

Result: 证明了在FR表示的凸问题类别中，特征筛选与样本筛选满足等变性质：dualization∘feature screening = sample screening∘dualization。这一结果将此前非正式的原始-对偶对应关系提升为严格的数学定理，统一了两种筛选范式。

Abstract: Feature and sample screening reduce the cost of machine learning by eliminating irrelevant features and noninformative samples, respectively. Although recognized as primal-dual counterparts, their relationship remains informal and model-dependent. Viewing screening and duality as transformations of objective functions, we introduce Fenchel-Rockafellar (FR) representations, a class of convex problems encompassing the Lasso and SVM that is closed under both transformations. We then prove that feature and sample screening form an equivariant pair: dualization followed by feature screening is equal to sample screening followed by dualization.

</details>

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel second-order dynamical system driven by a forward-backward splitting operator for solving the structured inclusion $0\in(A+B)(x)$ in a real Hilbert space, where $A$ is a maximal $ρ$-comonotone operator and $B$ is a $ν$-cocoercive operator. Temporal discretization further leads to a class of double inertial Halpern forward-backward splitting algorithms that encompasses the classical forward-backward splitting method and its inertial variants as special cases. Numerical experiments on split feasibility, sparse signal recovery, and image deblurring illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

Abstract: We study the problem of learning from data a parametric Nash equilibrium (NE) problem that is monotone (or strongly monotone) for all parameter values. In the presence of local and shared convex constraints, monotonicity enables efficient computation of generalized Nash equilibria of the learned game. We consider two learning scenarios: (i) direct learning of the NE problem from samples of the agents' costs, and (ii) inverse learning of surrogate agents' costs from samples of their best responses. We propose two methods to solve these tasks. The first is a penalty-based approach that promotes monotonicity of the learned game during training. The second, based on a representation theorem we introduce for a broad class of monotone games, parameterizes the agents' costs so that monotonicity of the NE problem is guaranteed by construction, for all parameter values, regardless of the training data used. We illustrate the applicability of the proposed methods on several numerical examples. A Python library and the examples reported in the paper are available at https://github.com/bemporad/learn_monotone_games.

Abstract: We study insensitizing control problems for a class of coupled linear stochastic parabolic systems. By duality, the analysis is reduced to an observability inequality for the corresponding adjoint system. The main analytical contribution is the derivation of new global Carleman estimates for coupled stochastic parabolic systems with zeroth- and second-order coupling terms, under suitable geometric assumptions on the control and observation regions. These estimates yield the required observability inequalities and, consequently, the existence of insensitizing controls. Furthermore, depending on the value of a weighting parameter $β\in[0,1]$, which determines the relative contributions of the two state components to the sentinel functional, we consider two cases. If $β\in\{0,1\}$, the sentinel functional depends on only one state component, and a single localized control acting in the drift of the first equation is sufficient. In contrast, if $β\in(0,1)$, both state components contribute to the sentinel functional, and two localized controls acting in the drift terms of the two equations are sufficient. Moreover, the control strategy in this paper involves two additional controls acting throughout the diffusion terms.

Abstract: We consider stochastic multi-objective optimization over a nonempty closed convex set, where every objective is an expectation and only sample-gradient information is available. The consistency of SAA gradients then transfers vanishing SAA residuals to Pareto stationarity for the population problem, while an additional concentration argument gives a finite-sample residual bound on compact sets. Abstract: A basic question in adaptive control is how rapidly a discrete-time nonlinear plant with unknown parameters may grow while remaining stabilizable. When the nonlinear growth exponent is known and only a scalar coefficient is unknown, existing theory identifies 4 as the exact critical exponent for stabilizability. We determine how this critical exponent changes when the growth exponent is also unknown. For any positive disturbance bound, one feedback law stabilizes all plants in some neighborhood of a nominal parameter pair if and only if the nominal exponent is below $3\sqrt{3}/2$. At equality, every compact parameter set whose exponents do not exceed this value remains stabilizable. If the exponent belongs to a known finite set, the critical exponent remains 4 for every nondegenerate compact coefficient interval. Hence finite exponent sets and arbitrarily short exponent intervals can have different critical exponents. Uncertainty in the growth rate therefore changes the range of growth that feedback can stabilize, not merely the size of the uncertainty set. The critical exponent remains $3\sqrt{3}/2$ under a known positive state-dependent multiplier bounded above and away from zero. For a system whose coefficient and exponent are known functions of an unknown parameter, a compact parameter family is stabilizable when its exponents do not exceed this value. At an interior parameter point where the Jacobian of these functions has rank two and the exponent is at least this value, no compact neighborhood is stabilizable.

</details>

Abstract: We study how far gradient descent (GD) can be accelerated by predetermined stepsizes in smooth convex optimization. Together with the non-anytime $O(n^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})$ rate achieved by silver schedules, our anytime lower bound establishes a strict separation between the achievable convergence exponents in the two settings.

Abstract: We study Gaussian-restricted barycenters for quadratic two-sided Kullback--Leibler unbalanced optimal transport with independent marginal penalties and no coupling entropy. Exact profiling of the barycenter mass reduces the problem to a smooth Gaussian shape functional with endogenous Gibbs weights. The diagonal second variation induces a parallel-sum tensor coupling the Bures--Wasserstein and Fisher--Rao metrics; its finite-mass extension admits a radial cone representation. Under common penalty scaling, global minimizers converge to a Gaussian Wasserstein barycenter with effective weights; for sufficiently large penalties, the minimizer is unique and admits a first-order analytic expansion. In the small-penalty regime, the distance of every global minimizer to the compact maximizer set of a weighted Chernoff affinity functional vanishes with respect to the mean--covariance parameter distance. Numerical experiments illustrate MM descent, local contraction, and the two penalty limits.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Electric toroidal dipoles (ETDs) characterize ferroaxial order, yet their bulk definition in periodic crystals has remained elusive because conventional multipole operators involve the ill-defined position operator. The ETD becomes finite exclusively in the ferroaxial phase and is strongly enhanced near a small band gap, accompanied by a sizable longitudinal spin current. Abstract: In this combined experimental and theoretical study, we investigate the role of disorder in governing the magnetic ground state of the quaternary Heusler alloy FeRuMnGe. In the FeRuMnZ (Z = Ga, Si) series, chemical substitution modifies atomic ordering and electronic structure, resulting in distinct magnetic ground states. Motivated by this, we extend the series to FeRuMnGe. X-ray diffraction reveals B2-type antisite disorder, where Fe--Ru and Mn--Ge intermix. Theoretical calculations show that such disorder modifies exchange interactions, leading to competing ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic couplings, and drives the system from half-metallic to metallic. Magnetic measurements reveal competing interactions, giving rise to a cluster-glass state coexisting with long-range magnetic order. The absence of a thermodynamic signature at $T_f$, together with ac susceptibility and relaxation measurements, supports the presence of short-range magnetic interactions and a glassy magnetic state. The compound exhibits an enhanced magnetic response below $\sim 161$ K and a maximum magnetization of $\sim 1.64~μ_B$/f.u. at 5 T. Overall, this work establishes a direct correlation between antisite disorder, competing exchange interactions, and glassy magnetism in quaternary Heusler alloys. Combined experimental results and theoretical calculations reveal that disorder drives the system from an AFM-dominated state in FeRuMnSi to an FM-dominated state in FeRuMnGe, providing deeper insight into the role of the extent of disorder in governing the magnetic properties of QHAs.

Method: (1) 在规范时空变分形式下，引入Riesz提升残差作为额外变量，构造混合系统；(2) 证明均匀inf-sup条件在允许时间片分解的棱柱剖分下成立，并证明在一般剖分下该条件不成立；(3) 推导关于精确Riesz提升与其Galerkin逼近之间误差的后验条件，在该条件下原始变量可拟最优；(4) 为两个变量推导后验误差估计器；(5) 设计双重自适应循环，交替进行测试空间与试空间富集；(6) 在1+1维和2+1维进行数值实验验证。

Result: 理论结果表明均匀inf-sup条件对一般剖分不成立，但通过所提出的后验条件仍可恢复拟最优性。数值实验在1+1维和2+1维展示了双重自适应循环的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般时空剖分下的最小残差时空有限元方法提供了理论保证和自适应算法框架，克服了传统均匀inf-sup条件的限制，使得方法适用于更广泛的时空网格场景。

Abstract: We study minimal residual space-time finite element discretizations of linear parabolic initial value problems in canonical space-time variational form. To deal with the arising dual norm, we introduce the Riesz lift of the residual as an additional variable. Quasi-optimality of the primal variable of the mixed system follows from a uniform inf-sup condition. This condition is known to be satisfied for finite element spaces w.r.t. prismatic partitions of the space-time cylinder that allow for a decomposition into time-slabs. We prove that this condition cannot be expected to hold otherwise. To recover stability for general partitions and the data at hand, we derive an a posteriori condition on the error between the exact Riesz lift of the residual and its Galerkin approximation -- being the secondary variable of our system -- under which the primal variable is quasi-optimal. We derive a posteriori error estimators for both variables, and use them in a double-adaptive loop that alternates test-space with trial-space enrichment. We illustrate our findings with numerical experiments in $1+1$ and $2+1$ dimensions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出两种在时间依赖的偏微分方程数值求解中强制施加界限约束的方法：一是基于任意时间步进方案的通用投影方法，二是基于隐式单步龙格-库塔方法和隐式多步方法修正公式的整体（monolithic）技术。通过求解约束优化问题，确保离散时间层的近似解满足界限约束，并在空间和时间上获得（形式上的）高阶精度。数值实验覆盖线性热方程、平流方程和非线性Allen-Cahn方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元方法是求解偏微分方程的精确且高效的工具，通过将变分问题限制在有限维逼近空间来实现。然而，有限元方法通常不能保证原问题固有的界限约束（如物理量的非负性或有界性）得到满足，这在许多实际应用中至关重要。

Method: 本文提出两种强制界限约束的方法：(1) 通用投影法——对任意抽象时间步进方案进行系统性修正，将近似解投影回可行约束集；(2) 整体（monolithic）方法——以修正形式的隐式单级龙格-库塔方法和一般隐式多步方法为典型示例，通过求解约束最优化问题，确保离散时间层的近似解满足界限约束，从而获得空间和时间上的形式高阶精度。

Result: 针对线性热方程、线性平流方程以及非线性Allen-Cahn方程给出了数值算例，验证了所提出方法在强制界限约束保持方面的有效性以及高阶时空精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的两类方法能够系统性地在时间相关偏微分方程的有限元求解中强制实施界限约束，同时保持高阶精度，从而弥补了传统有限元方法在约束保持方面的不足。

Abstract: Finite elements provide accurate and efficient methods for the numerical solution of partial differential equations by means of restricting variational problems to finite-dimensional approximating spaces. However, they do not, in general, guarantee enforcement of bounds constraints inherent in the original problem. We propose two approaches to enforcing bounds constraints for time-dependent problems. First, we propose general projective methods which result from a systematic modification of any abstract time-stepping scheme. Second, we present a monolithic technique for which we take a modified formulation of implicit single-stage Runge-Kutta methods and general implicit multistep methods as prototypical examples. By solving a constrained optimization problem, we are able to ensure that the bounds constraints are enforced by the approximate solution at the discrete time levels, obtaining (formally) high order methods both in space and time. Numerical examples for the linear heat and advection equations and nonlinear Allen-Cahn equation are given.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文调和了受Fokker-Planck方程约束的优化问题中两种不同视角下的伴随方程表述：宏观欧拉视角给出后向Kolmogorov伴随方程，而微观拉格朗日视角给出沿随机轨迹的路径伴随方程。作者在连续设定下建立了这两种表述的对应关系，并指出离散化后虽然二者的梯度不再精确一致，但都一致地逼近连续梯度，并给出了显式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Fokker-Planck约束优化中欧拉与拉格朗日两种伴随表述的表观不一致性问题，为理论分析和数值方案选择提供了统一的支撑与量化保证。

Abstract: The Fokker-Planck equation admits both a macroscopic Eulerian description through probability densities and a microscopic Lagrangian description through stochastic trajectories. Consequently, optimization problems constrained by the Fokker-Planck equation can be formulated from either perspective. Surprisingly, the corresponding adjoint equations appear to be fundamentally different: the macroscopic adjoint is governed by the backward Kolmogorov equation, whereas the microscopic adjoint evolves pathwise along stochastic trajectories. Explicit convergence rates are established for both discretization strategies.

Abstract: We introduce a compactly supported multinode Shepard operator for the interpolation of scattered data on the torus embedded in $\mathbb{R}^3$. The torus is treated as an algebraic surface defined by a quartic polynomial, and Gröbner bases are used to construct reduced polynomial spaces on the surface by removing the redundancy induced by the defining equation. This yields local Vandermonde systems adapted to the toroidal geometry. We discuss the metric structure of the torus, show the local equivalence between the periodic parameter distance and the Euclidean distance inherited from $\mathbb{R}^3$, and use this equivalence to motivate the compact support construction. We establish a uniform error estimate in terms of the maximal support radius and the local Lebesgue constants. Under uniform locality and stability assumptions, the method converges with order $d+1$ with respect to the fill distance. Numerical experiments on analytical test functions confirm polynomial reproduction and exhibit an error decay consistent with the theoretical analysis. The approach is also tested on Computational Fluid Dynamics data mapped onto the torus, including the interpolation of the velocity components, the reconstruction of the velocity magnitude from the interpolated components, and the reconstruction of a tangent velocity field through an orthonormal lifting of the interpolated components.

Abstract: We introduce a stream-function formulation for the Surface Stokes-Helfrich model and study an Arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) Surface Finite Element (SFEM) discretization in space and a semi-implicit discretization in time of these equations. This now classical method is a finite element type method that performs a Galerkin approximation of the oscillatory problem on a specific, problem dependent, basis set. The basis functions are precomputed in an offline stage. Inspired by homogenization theory and using some filtering ideas, we show how to define these basis functions in order to obtain an efficient method. The comprehensive set of numerical experiments that we present, in periodic and non-periodic cases, for the scalar-valued version of the problem (which is then self-adjoint) and for the vector-valued version of the problem (which is then in general non self-adjoint), demonstrates the performance of the approach.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1970年代以来，对任意非退化平面三角剖分上C^1三次样条空间S_3^1(T)的维数确定问题一直悬而未决。Schumaker提出的下界包含针对奇异内部四星形的局部修正σ，并猜测该下界总是可达的。验证或反驳这一猜想是理解样条空间维数几何依赖性的关键。

Method: 构造一个固定的18三角形复合形的一参数非退化实现族，其中仅中心顶点v_6(t)=(t,0)在容许区间I=(-3/4,24/55)内移动。通过平滑余因子（smoothing cofactor）计算和互补的Bernstein-Bézier计算两种方法分析该族在不同参数值下的维数变化，并与Schumaker下界进行比较。

Conclusion: 构造的反例推翻了Schumaker下界总是可达的猜想。奇异四星修正并不能捕获所有几何依赖的维数贡献；当所有局部块均满秩但全局仍出现额外维数增加时，说明存在真正全局性的相容性条件必须被纳入维数公式的考虑范围。

Abstract: Determining the dimension of the $C^1$ cubic spline space $S_3^1(\mathcal{T})$ on an arbitrary nondegenerate planar triangulation has remained unresolved since the 1970s. Schumaker's lower bound includes a local correction $σ$ for singular interior four-stars, and it was conjectured that this bound is always attained. We disprove this conjecture by constructing a one-parameter family of nondegenerate realizations of a fixed 18-triangle complex, with only the central vertex moving as $v_6(t)=(t,0)$ on the admissible interval $I=(-3/4,24/55)$. The family exhibits three distinct cases. For $t\in I\setminus\{1/5,3/83\}$, the lower bound is attained and $\dim S_3^1(\mathcal{T}(t))=33$. At $t=3/83$, the central four-star is singular, $σ=1$, and the resulting dimension 34 is exactly accounted for by the classical local correction. At $t=1/5$, however, all interior vertices are nonsingular and $σ=0$, yet $\dim S_3^1(\mathcal{T}(1/5))=34>P_{\mathcal{T}(1/5)}(1,3)=33$. The smoothing-cofactor calculation shows that the dependence at $t=3/83$ is confined to the central vertex block, whereas the dependence at $t=1/5$ couples all seven interior vertex cycles even though every individual block has full row rank. A complementary Bernstein--Bézier calculation gives the same dimension profile. Thus the singular-four-star correction does not capture every geometry-dependent contribution to $\dim S_3^1(\mathcal{T})$; genuinely global compatibility must also be taken into account.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the Smagorinsky model for the Navier-Stokes equations within a virtual element framework. We additionally prove that divergence-free virtual discretizations provide improved convergence orders, with weaker regularity assumptions than in the finite element literature. We conclude the paper with numerical results that corroborate the theory.

Abstract: The Boltzmann equation plays an important role in modeling mesoscopic behavior in a wide range of scientific and engineering applications. However, its numerical solution is computationally expensive due to the high dimensionality of the model and the nonlinear nonlocal collision operator, especially for steady-state problems that require iterative solvers. This cost becomes prohibitive for inverse problems, where the induced optimization problem requires repeated forward solves.
  In this work, we propose a reduced-order model (ROM) for the parametric Boltzmann equation to address this computational challenge. The ROM constructs a low-dimensional approximation space for the parameter-induced solution manifold through a residual-based greedy strategy, and the reduced solution is then obtained via residual minimization over the reduced space, subject to mass conservation. The overall efficiency of the ROM is achieved by exploiting the quadratic structure of the collision operator and a precomputed separable approximation of the collision kernel. The resulting ROM is further applied to a thermally-driven inverse problem for reconstructing collision parameters from the observed macroscopic temperature data. This is accomplished either by directly replacing the PDE constraint with the ROM, leading to a bilevel optimization formulation, or by reformulating the task as a single-level optimization problem through the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions. Numerical experiments in both collision-dominated and transport-dominated cases are performed to demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed ROM and its effectiveness in inverse problems. In particular, the resulting inverse problem is computationally much more tractable, achieving speedups of several orders of magnitude over that based on the full-order model while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [98] [Computational study of Proper Orthogonal Decomposition methods for parametric approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02583)
*Bosco García-Archilla,Alicia García-Mascaraque,Julia Novo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究演化参数化时间依赖偏微分方程的本征正交分解降阶模型（POD-ROMs）的数值实现。通过比较L^2(Ω)和H^1_0(Ω)投影构建相关矩阵，观察到两者性能相似；同时对比多种非线性项计算方法的效率，发现DEIM方法最为高效，可将计算时间减少约一半。此外，使用二维Brusselator模型分析多参数情形下的新方法和标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于单参数POD-ROM模型，文献中通常使用L^2(Ω)投影构建相关矩阵，尽管H^1_0(Ω)投影能提供最优逐点误差估计，需从实践角度比较两者差异。同时，降阶方程中非线性项的计算成本高且随问题复杂度增加而增大，需要寻找高效的数值求解策略。对于多参数模型，需要用更复杂的系统补充和验证新提出的方法。

Method: 论文对比了在L^2(Ω)和H^1_0(Ω)上投影构建相关矩阵的POD-ROM性能差异；系统比较了多种计算降阶方程中非线性项的方法，包括完整FEM张量构造和DEIM方法；并利用二维Brusselator模型对多参数情形的新标准方法进行补充分析。

Result: 实践表明L^2(Ω)与H^1_0(Ω)投影方案性能相似；DEIM方法在保持相同精度的前提下，相比完整FEM张量构造可将计算时间减少约一半，是最为高效的方案；Brusselator模型的数值结果进一步支持了新方法的误差分析。

Conclusion: 本文从计算实现角度系统评估了POD-ROM的关键环节，确认了DEIM在大幅降低非线性项计算成本的同时保持精度，并为多参数POD-ROM新方法提供了更复杂系统下的数值验证。

Abstract: This paper studies the computational implementation of proper orthogonal decomposition reduced-order models (POD-ROMs) for evolutionary parametric time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs). For a one-parameter model, many papers in the literature build the correlation matrix by projecting onto $L^2(Ω)$ even though optimal pointwise error estimates are proved when projecting onto $H^1_0(Ω)$. We compare both scenarios and observe the similar performance in practice. Additionally, to get the POD approximation it is necessary to compute the nonlinear term in the reduced equations. This requires a high computational cost that increases when the problem gets more complex. Numerical results in this paper show different approaches to address this issue. Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (DEIM) is the most efficient approach. It reduces computational time by approximately half compared to computing the whole FEM formulation by a tensor construction while maintaining the same accuracy. For a multiparameter model, we analyze the new and standard method proposed in \cite{newmethod} using a two-dimensional Brusselator model to complement the results and support the error analysis with a more complex system.

</details>


### [99] [Positivity loss in bandlimited spectral reproduction on spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02695)
*Hao-Ning Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了N带限谱算子精确重建到L阶所有模态时，正性损失的最小可能值。证明在球面S^d上，当1≤L<N时，均匀算子范数超出1的最小阶为(L/(N+1))^2，并通过Fejér峰值检验和浓度估计给出下界，通过修正Jackson算子得到匹配上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球面多项式逼近中，带限谱算子同时实现精确重建和正性保持两个理想性质时存在本质冲突。量化这种正性损失的最小可能值，对于理解逼近算子结构的固有局限至关重要。

Method: 下界采用Fejér峰值检验结合带限核的浓度估计；上界通过对正Jackson算子叠加光滑滤波器进行修正来构造；并在圆、滤波超插值等三种场景中检验结果的锐度。

Result: 当1≤L<N时，最小正性损失（均匀算子范数超出1的最小阶）为sharp order (L/(N+1))^2。在圆上取N=sL-1时，确定广义投影常数超出1的sharp order，并指出延迟de la Vallée-Poussin均值的s^-1过剩与最优s^-2阶之间的差距。同时给出滤波超插值算子范数距正性阈值1的定量下界。

Conclusion: 正性损失的下界为(L/(N+1))^2阶，该结果在多种逼近场景中成立，并揭示出滤波超插值的算子范数虽然一致有界，但其与正性阈值1的分离具有特定下界，且存在算子级别的极大值原理障碍。

Abstract: How small can the positivity loss be for an $N$-bandlimited spectral operator that exactly reproduces all modes up to degree $L$? For spherical polynomial approximation on $\mathbb S^d$, we prove that the smallest possible excess of the uniform operator norm above 1, equivalently the least positivity loss, is of sharp order $\left({L}/{(N+1)}\right)^2$ when $1\le L< N$. The lower bound follows from a Fejér peak test and a concentration estimate for bandlimited kernels, while a matching upper bound is obtained by correcting a positive Jackson operator with a smooth filter. We illustrate the result in three settings. On the circle, taking $N=sL-1$, this determines the sharp order of the generalized-projection constant above 1 and identifies the gap between the $s^{-1}$ excess of delayed de la Vallée--Poussin means and the optimal $s^{-2}$ order. For filtered hyperinterpolation, whose operator norm has long been known to be uniformly bounded, we give a quantitative lower bound on its separation from the positivity threshold 1. Finally, we identify an operator-level obstruction to maximum principles.

</details>


### [100] [A stable rank-adaptive step-and-truncate finite volume method for Vlasov transport on domains with piecewise linear boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02721)
*André Uschmajew,Andreas Zeiser*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究基于低秩近似的线性Vlasov输运方程数值求解方法，结合有限体积离散与秩自适应步进截断方案，证明其在均匀入流条件下继承L2稳定性与CFL限制，并给出低秩近似误差的显式界。数值实验覆盖1d1v与2d2v场景，后者处理约5.8×10^10相空间单元而数值秩不超过12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维相空间中的Vlasov输运方程数值求解面临维数灾难，传统全网格离散的计算与存储开销巨大。低秩逼近技术是缓解该问题的有效途径，但现有动态低秩近似（DLRA）方法存在切空间投影带来的建模误差与稳定性保障不足等问题，亟需设计稳定性可证、误差可控的低秩方案。

Method: 将线性Vlasov输运方程在有界空间域上以有限体积法离散，所得矩阵ODE采用秩自适应的步进-截断（step-and-truncate）格式求解。空间与速度网格可为非结构化，通过适当的数值通量保持分离的空间-速度表示。对秩截断误差给出了与截断容差相关的显式上界。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法在均匀入流条件下继承了完整有限体积前向Euler格式的L2稳定性和CFL限制条件。低秩近似误差可显式地以截断容差界定。数值实验在1d1v和2d2v场景验证了理论预测，2d2v算例中方法处理了非结构化空间网格和非零入流，全张量积离散约5.8×10^10个相空间单元，而数值秩最大仅为12。

Conclusion: 所提出的秩自适应低秩有限体积方法为线性Vlasov输运方程提供了一种高效且理论上可保证稳定性的数值方案，相比DLRA避免了切空间投影的建模误差，能在极高维相空间网格上以极低的数值秩获得可靠解。

Abstract: We consider the numerical solution of the linear Vlasov transport equation on bounded spatial domains with inflow boundary conditions based on low-rank approximation. We combine a finite volume discretization with a rank-adaptive step-and-truncate scheme for the resulting matrix ODE. The spatial and velocity meshes may be unstructured, while suitable numerical fluxes retain a separated space-velocity representation. For homogeneous inflow, we show that the low-rank scheme inherits the $L_2$ stability and CFL restriction of the underlying full finite volume forward Euler method. In addition, the low-rank approximation error is bounded explicitly in terms of the truncation tolerances, avoiding the modeling error associated with tangent-space projections in dynamical low-rank approximation. Numerical experiments in 1d1v and 2d2v confirm the predicted error behavior. In 2d2v, the method handles an unstructured spatial mesh with nonzero inflow and a full tensor-product discretization of approximately $5.8\cdot10^{10}$ phase-space cells while the numerical rank is at most twelve.

</details>


### [101] [Neural operators approximate strongly continuous convex monotone semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02727)
*Jonas Blessing,Philipp Schmocker,Alessandro Sgarabottolo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出利用神经算子学习Chernoff型单步算子来近似强连续凸单调半群，并分别引入Chernoff-神经算子和包络-神经算子两类架构，通过通用逼近定理和定量逼近率保证了逼近效果，最后在非线性PDE、随机最优控制和模型不确定性下的随机过程等多个数值示例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强连续凸单调半群广泛出现在非线性偏微分方程、随机最优控制和随机过程等应用中，但直接求解这些半群通常计算成本高昂且缺乏通用方法。利用Chernoff型单步算子可将半群演化分解为迭代单步过程，若能高效学习这些单步算子，则可实现对半群的通用近似。

Method: 本文提出两类神经算子架构：(1) Chernoff-神经算子，用于通用逼近Chernoff单步算子，并通过加权Hölder空间之间的稳定性估计将单步逼近误差在迭代过程中传播，从而获得对应半群的通用逼近结果；(2) 包络-神经算子，专门针对包络半群设计，能够推导出定量的逼近率。

Result: 通过通用逼近定理证明了Chernoff-神经算子可以任意精度逼近Chernoff单步算子，且误差可沿迭代传播实现半群的通用逼近；包络-神经算子进一步提供了定量逼近率。多个数值示例（包括非线性PDE、随机最优控制和模型不确定性下的随机过程）验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用深度学习方法高效近似凸单调半群提供了理论基础和实用框架，兼顾了通用性与定量保证，并在多个具有挑战性的应用场景中展示了良好性能。

Abstract: We approximate strongly continuous convex monotone semigroups by learning their Chernoff-type one-step operators with neural operators. First, we introduce the general class of so-called Chernoff-neural operators and show in a universal approximation theorem that they can approximate the Chernoff one-step operators arbitrarily well. By using stability estimates between weighted Hölder spaces, the one-step approximation error can be propagated through the iterations which yields universal approximation of the corresponding semigroup. Second, we introduce the more specialized class of envelope-neural operators for envelope semigroups which allows us to derive quantitative approximation rates. Finally, we illustrate the effectiveness of these neural operators in several numerical examples arising from non-linear partial differential equations, stochastic optimal control and stochastic processes under model uncertainty.

</details>


### [102] [Continuous data assimilation in steady Navier-Stokes equations with unknown viscosity: robust and efficient solvers and fast parameter recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02862)
*L. Rebholz,J. Reyes,J. Whitehead*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续数据同化（CDA）的方法，从部分不可压缩流观测数据中恢复稳态Navier-Stokes方程中的未知粘度参数，并配套设计了高效的参数恢复算法和CDA-NSE非线性求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方程发现方法（如SINDy）从数据中识别控制参数和模型的研究日益受到关注，但多数方法基于数据驱动视角。本文采用分析与数值PDE方法互补的路径，旨在从部分流场观测数据中恢复未知粘度，满足实际应用中无法获得完整数据的场景需求。

Method: 提出一个简单高效的参数恢复算法（具有二次收敛性），并设计了一个CDA-Picard + CDA-Newton非线性求解器（同样具有二次收敛性），用于稳态CDA-NSE方程的参数恢复。

Result: 理论分析证明了稳态CDA-NSE的适定性，参数恢复算法的二次收敛性以及CDA-Picard + CDA-Newton非线性求解器的二次收敛性。数值实验表明该方法即使在初始猜测很差的情况下也能快速有效地恢复参数。

Conclusion: 所提出的CDA方法结合参数恢复算法与非线性求解器，为从部分数据中恢复NSE未知粘度提供了一种高效、理论基础扎实的解决方案，在初始猜测不佳时仍表现良好。

Abstract: Recent advances in equation discovery methods such as SINDy have highlighted the growing interest in identifying governing parameters and models directly from data. In this work, we take a complementary approach grounded in analysis and numerical PDE methods: we recover an unknown viscosity in steady Navier-Stokes equations (NSE) from partial incompressible flow observations using continuous data assimilation (CDA). We propose a simple and efficient parameter recovery algorithm and also a nonlinear solver for CDA-NSE. Together, this creates a highly efficient technique for recovering an unknown viscosity from partial solution data. Our analysis establishes the well-posedness of steady CDA-NSE, quadratic convergence of the parameter recovery algorithm, and quadratic convergence of a CDA-Picard + CDA-Newton nonlinear solver. Numerical experiments illustrate that the methods are very effective in restoring parameters quickly, even with poor initial guesses.

</details>
