### [103] [A lower bound for the Call-Silverman height in cyclotomic extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01747)
*Alessio Cangini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对数域K上的二次后临界有限多项式，建立了游荡代数整数在K的分圆扩张中的Call-Silverman高度的下界，并在非整数情形下给出了部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算术动力学中，Call-Silverman高度是研究动力系统算术性质的重要工具。对游荡点的Call-Silverman高度建立有效的下界，是理解其算术性质的关键，尤其是在分圆扩张这一特殊背景下，此前缺乏系统性的下界结果。

Method: 该证明综合运用了位势理论（potential theory）、代数数论（algebraic number theory）以及等分布技术（equidistribution techniques）三类工具。

Result: 对二次后临界有限多项式，在分圆扩张中的游荡代数整数建立了Call-Silverman高度的下界；对非整数情形给出了部分（partial）结果。

Conclusion: 通过结合位势理论与代数数论，成功为特定动力系统下的游荡代数整数高度给出了定量下界，并指出对非整数情形仅有部分结果，暗示了该问题的进一步研究空间。

Abstract: For quadratic postcritically finite polynomials defined over a number field $K$, we establish lower bounds for the Call-Silverman height of wandering algebraic integers lying in cyclotomic extensions of $K$ and provide partial results for the nonintegral case. Our proof combines potential theory and algebraic number theory with equidistribution techniques.

</details>


### [104] [On the cuspidal cohomology of Iwahori congruence subgroups of $\mathrm{SL}(3, \mathbb{Z})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01776)
*Zachary Porat*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究SL(3,Z)的Iwahori同余子群I(3,p)在p处尖点同调。通过推广Ash-Grayson-Green的关键结果，在p≤227的范围内发现三个具有非零尖点类的水平，并在其中两个水平上计算了Hecke算子作用，首次获得了在Iwahori水平上出现的、p处为Steinberg型、平凡上同调权重的非本质自对偶自守表示的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Iwahori同余子群的自守形式，特别是希望通过计算Iwahori水平下的尖点同调，寻找非本质自对偶（non-essentially-self-dual）的自守表示，这类表示在数论和Langlands纲领中具有重要价值。由于此前缺乏此类例子，需要计算同调来发现和验证它们的存在。

Method: 推广Ash、Grayson和Green的经典结果以适应Iwahori同余子群I(3,p)（模p上三角矩阵构成的SL(3,Z)子群）。利用这些推广的理论工具，在p≤227的素数范围内系统地计算I(3,p)的尖点同调，筛选具有非零尖点类的水平，然后在这些水平上计算Hecke算子的作用。

Result: 在p≤227范围内找到了三个具有非零尖点同调类的水平，并在其中两个水平上成功计算了Hecke算子的作用。这些发现首次给出了非本质自对偶、平凡上同调权重且在p处为Steinberg型的自守表示在Iwahori水平出现的具体例子。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了Iwahori水平下非本质自对偶自守表示实例的空白，为理解Locally Symmetric Spaces上的同调与自守形式之间的关系提供了新的计算证据，也为进一步研究Steinberg型自守表示在p进和模表示理论中的性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate automorphic forms for congruence subgroups of $\mathrm{SL}(3, \mathbb{Z})$ that are Iwahori at $p$. In particular, we study the cuspidal cohomology of Iwahori congruence subgroups $\mathcal{I}(3, p)$, which are comprised of matrices in $\mathrm{SL}(3, \mathbb{Z})$ that are upper triangular modulo $p$. In order to work with $\mathcal{I}(3, p)$, we generalize key results from Ash, Grayson, and Green [J.\ Number Theory 19 (1984), pp.\ 412-436]. For levels $\mathcal{I}(3, p)$ with $p \leq 227$, we found three levels with nonzero cuspidal classes and were able to compute the action of Hecke operators at two of these levels. These are the first examples of non-essentially-self-dual automorphic representations of trivial cohomological weight that are Steinberg at $p$ appearing at Iwahori level.

</details>


### [105] [Ranks and integer points on elliptic curves induced by Fibonacci triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01789)
*Andrej Dujella*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究由斐波那契Fibonacci和卢卡斯Lucas数列构造的椭圆曲线E_k: y²=(F_{2k}x+1)(F_{2k+2}x+1)(F_{2k+4}x+1)，该曲线源于正则Diophantine三元组{F_{2k},F_{2k+2},F_{2k+4}}。对奇数k，文中给出新的有理点Q_k，证明其与标准点P_k=(0,1)独立，从而rank E_k(Q)≥2；当秩恰为2时，所有整数点恰好对应于Hoggatt-Bergum扩展问题的两个已知解。通过对两条圆锥曲线L²-5F²=±4做参数化并应用单射特化准则，证明对应的单参数椭圆族在奇数情形generic rank为2，偶数情形为1。最后给出计算数据并提出秩分布猜想：秩1、2、3的概率分别为1/4、1/2、1/4，秩≥4的密度为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是深入理解由斐波那契Fibonacci与卢卡斯Lucas数列生成的椭圆曲线族的算术性质，特别是其Mordell-Weil秩。这些曲线源于正则Diophantine三元组，在数论中自然出现，且与经典的Hoggatt-Bergum扩展问题紧密相关。通过寻找新的独立有理点并确定generic rank，可更全面地刻画此类特殊椭圆曲线族的秩行为与整数点结构。

Method: 本文采用多种代数与解析数论方法：(1) 对奇数k显式构造新的有理点Q_k，利用椭圆曲线上点的线性无关性判定其与已知点P_k的独立性；(2) 当秩为2时，利用rank限制来分析E_k上的所有整数点，将其与Hoggatt-Bergum扩展问题已知解建立对应；(3) 对圆锥曲线L²-5F²=±4进行参数化，构造单参数椭圆族，并利用单射特化准则（injective specialization criterion）证明generic rank；(4) 通过计算数据分析并启发式地提出秩分布猜想。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对每个奇数k≥3，新有理点Q_k与P_k独立，故rank E_k(Q)≥2；(2) 若奇数k≥3且rank恰为2，则E_k上所有整数点恰好来自Hoggatt-Bergum扩展问题的两个已知解；(3) 参数化两个圆锥曲线后，证明对应的单参数椭圆族generic rank在奇数情形为2、偶数情形为1；(4) 计算数据支持启发式秩分布猜想：P(rank=1)=1/4，P(rank=2)=1/2，P(rank=3)=1/4，rank≥4的概率为零。

Conclusion: 本文系统深化了对Fibonacci/Lucas型椭圆曲线族的理解，给出了奇数情形下秩至少为2的构造性证明，并通过generic rank的精确确定和rank分类下的整数点刻画，将椭圆曲线算术与Diophantine三元组紧密联系起来。同时，计算数据和启发式分析为秩分布的进一步研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Let $F_n$ and $L_n$ denote the Fibonacci and Lucas numbers, respectively, and consider \[
  E_k:\qquad y^2=(F_{2k}x+1)(F_{2k+2}x+1)(F_{2k+4}x+1). \] These elliptic curves arise naturally from the regular Diophantine triples \[
  \{F_{2k},F_{2k+2},F_{2k+4}\}. \] For odd $k$, we exhibit the rational point \[
  Q_k=\left(
  -\frac{F_{k-1}}{L_kF_{k+1}F_{k+2}},
  \frac{F_{2k+1}}{L_kF_{k+1}F_{k+2}}
  \right). \] For every odd $k\geq 3$, this point is independent of the standard point $P_k=(0,1)$; in particular, $\operatorname{rank}E_k(\mathbb{Q})\geq 2$. Moreover, if $k\geq 3$ is odd and $\operatorname{rank}E_k(\mathbb{Q})=2$, then all integer points on $E_k$ are exactly the points arising from the two known solutions of the Hoggatt-Bergum extension problem. By parametrizing the two conics $L^2-5F^2=\pm4$ and applying an injective specialization criterion, we also show that the corresponding one-parameter elliptic families have generic ranks $2$ in the odd case and $1$ in the even case. Finally, we discuss computational data and propose the heuristic rank distribution $1/4,1/2,1/4$ for ranks $1,2,3$, respectively, with density zero for rank at least $4$.

</details>


### [106] [On everywhere locally soluble varieties in families over toric bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01825)
*Vladi Mitankin,Leo Schäfer,Damaris Schindler,Florian Wilsch*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在环面基上某些簇族中局部可溶纤维的密度问题，并发展了一种新的双曲线方法来处理具有形如 B^a(log B)^{-Δ} 增长行为的计数函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部可溶纤维在簇族中的密度是一个数论中的经典问题，与Hasse原理偏差有关。已有的方法在处理多项式基或简单基时有效，但面对环面基以及更复杂的计数函数增长形式时，需要发展新的工具。

Method: 开发了一种新的双曲线方法（超曲面方法的变体），专门适用于环面基上的计数问题。该方法能够处理计数函数的增长行为为 B^a(log B)^{-Δ} 这类更一般的形式，从而可以定量地确定局部可溶纤维的密度。

Result: 确定了在环面基上的某些簇族中局部可溶纤维的密度，并建立了相应计数函数的确切渐近估计。

Conclusion: 新的双曲线方法成功推广到环面基情境，使得对具有对数修正项的计数函数的密度分析成为可能，为该类几何与数论交叉问题提供了统一的处理框架。

Abstract: We determine the density of locally soluble fibres in certain families of varieties over a toric base. For this we develop a new form of the hyperbola method for toric bases which allows for counting functions with a growth behaviour of the form $B^a(\log B)^{-Δ}$.

</details>


### [107] [When Fibonacci and Lucas Meet Smith: A Computational Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01955)
*Ronie P. Dario,João L. Gonçalves,Moniky P. N. Oliveira*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了斐波那契数列和卢卡斯数列中哪些项是Smith数（复合整数，其十进制各位数字之和等于其质因数（计重数）的各位数字之和）。利用已有的完整因子分解数据，作者在两条数列中进行了计算搜索，获得了新的Smith数项：卢卡斯数列中发现了10个指数小于1000的Smith项（后续又添加了4个），斐波那契数列中发现了7个新的Smith项。特别有趣的是，指数827同时出现在两条数列中，即F_827和L_827都是Smith数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smith数是一个经典的数字理论概念，但此前没有人系统地研究过斐波那契数列和卢卡斯数列中的Smith项。该问题的陈述很简单，但对于较大的指数n，检验一项是否为Smith数需要对其进行完整因子分解，这很快变成了一个计算数论的挑战。作者希望利用现今可用的完整因子分解数据，在这两条经典数列中寻找新的Smith数。

Method: 作者采用计算方法：利用已有的完整因子分解数据，分别对斐波那契数列F_n和卢卡斯数列L_n进行计算搜索。对于偶指数的斐波那契项，利用恒等式F_{2n}=F_nL_n，将斐波那契和卢卡斯两者的因子分解数据结合，以恢复检验Smith数所需的完整因子分解。

Result: 在卢卡斯数列中，第一作者发现了10个指数小于1000的Smith项（索引3, 95, 105, 114, 183, 437, 609, 682, 827, 902），已发布为OEIS A395686；随后Sean A. Irvine又添加了4个指数（1090, 1153, 1215, 1378）。在斐波那契数列中，在已有OEIS A382922的基础上，搜索发现了7个新的Smith数：F_1440, F_1554, F_1596, F_1863, F_2256, F_2277, F_2559。值得注意的是，指数827同时出现在两条数列搜索结果中。

Conclusion: 本文通过计算搜索首次系统性地考察了斐波那契与卢卡斯数列中的Smith数，发现了两条数列中的多个新Smith项，并证明了F_827和L_827同时为Smith数这一有趣的巧合现象。研究展示了利用恒等式F_2n=F_nL_n结合两条数列的因子分解数据来验证Smith性质的有效性。

Abstract: The Fibonacci and Lucas sequences are old friends in recreational number theory. Here we ask a simple question: which of their terms $F_n$ and $L_n$ are Smith numbers? A Smith number is a composite integer whose decimal digit sum equals the sum of the decimal digits of its prime factors, counted with multiplicity. The question is easy to state, but for large $n$ it quickly becomes a factorization problem. Using available complete factorization data, we carried out computational searches in both sequences and obtained new Smith terms in each of them. For the Lucas sequence, the first author's search found ten Smith terms with indices below 1000: 3, 95, 105, 114, 183, 437, 609, 682, 827, 902. These results were published in the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences as OEIS A395686. Four further indices, 1090, 1153, 1215, 1378, were subsequently added to the sequence by Sean A. Irvine. For the Fibonacci sequence, starting from the cases already recorded in OEIS A382922, our search produced seven further Smith numbers: $F_{1440}$, $F_{1554}$, $F_{1596}$, $F_{1863}$, $F_{2256}$, $F_{2277}$, $F_{2559}$. For the even-index cases, the identity $F_{2n}=F_nL_n$ allows available Fibonacci and Lucas factorization data to be combined to recover the complete factorizations needed for the Smith test. Surprisingly, the two searches also meet at index 827: both $F_{827}$ and $L_{827}$ are Smith numbers.

</details>


### [108] [Jacquet-Zagier treatment of the beyond endoscopy trace formula for $\mathrm{GL}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01990)
*Pranjal Pandurang Warade*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了GL₂/Q上超越端点的迹公式，该公式与对偶群GL₂(C)的任意对称幂表示σ_k相关。通过将尖点核与相应的Eisenstein级数E(g,s)积分，在s=1处取留数实现迹公式，用连续变形的迹公式替代Arthur截断操作，并在Hitchin-Steinberg基的迹变量上进行Poisson求和，证明了椭圆部分主导项的亚纯延拓性质及极点结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越端点的迹公式是数论中Langlands纲领的重要工具，旨在将L-函数纳入迹公式框架。此前这类公式的研究仅在少数情形（如自同构谱的标准L-函数）有所进展。本文旨在首次系统地研究GL₂上相应于一般对称幂表示σ_k的超越端点迹公式，从而为更深层的自守L-函数性质提供新方法。

Method: (1) 在每个有限位点通过基本函数构建包含L-函数L(s_B,π,σ_k)的adelic函数；(2) 将尖点核函数与对应的Eisenstein级数E(g,s)积分，在s=1处取留数来实现迹公式，从而以连续变形的迹公式取代Arthur的截断操作；(3) 在Hitchin-Steinberg基的迹变量上实施Poisson求和；(4) 证明椭圆部分的主导项在Re(s_B)≥0上具有亚纯延拓，且在s_B=1处有k阶极点。

Result: 成功建立了GL₂/Q上任意对称幂表示σ_k的超越端点迹公式，证明其椭圆部分的主导项可亚纯延拓至Re(s_B)≥0，并在s_B=1处具有k阶极点。该结果推广了此前已知的特例（如标准表示情形），为任意k的对称幂L-函数提供了新的解析工具。

Conclusion: 本文首次为GL₂上任意对称幂表示建立了超越端点的迹公式，通过留数方法繞开Arthur截断，并刻画了椭圆部分主导项的奇点结构。这一框架为进一步研究对称幂L-函数的零点分布、亚纯延拓等深层性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: We begin the study of the beyond endoscopic trace formula for $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ attached to any symmetric power representation $σ_k$ of the dual group $\mathrm{G}L_2(\mathbb{C})$. For an adelic function that incorporates the $L$-functions $L(s_B,π,σ_k)$ through the basic functions at all the finite places, we integrate the cuspidal kernel against a corresponding Eisenstein series $E(g,s)$ and realize the trace formula as a residue at $s=1$, replacing Arthur's truncation operation by a continuously deformed trace formula. We argue Poisson summation on the trace variable of the Hitchin-Steinberg base and show that the dominant term of the elliptic part admits meromorphic continuation to $\mathfrak{R}(s_B) \geq 0$ with a pole of order $k$ at $s_B =1$.

</details>


### [109] [Summing the reciprocal of the polynomial appearing in Fermat's Last Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02112)
*Ariel Edery*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式倒数在整数格点上的三重和S(m,N)=Σ1/(x^N+y^N-z^N)。由于费马大定理保证x^N+y^N≠z^N，该和有定义。作者将S(m,N)分解为显式主导项D(m,N)=(2m-1)H_m^(N)（近似为2ζ(N)的线性函数）和余项R(m,N)。数值与解析分析表明：余项在N≥4时收敛，而R(m,3)可能对数发散（源于费马near misses造成的跳跃），从而整体和呈直线形式（斜率2ζ(N)），仅N=3在m=9附近有微小偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的出发点是费马大定理保证了f=x^N+y^N-z^N（N≥3，正整数组）永不为零，从而其倒数1/f无奇点。这启发作者研究这个有界倒数项的三重有限和S(m,N)，探索其整体结构与渐近性质，并揭示其与费马大定理及数论深层次结构（如near misses）之间的微妙联系。

Method: 方法分为两部分：(1) 将S(m,N)分解为主导项D(m,N)=(2m-1)H_m^(N)，其中H_m^(N)快速收敛到Riemann zeta函数ζ(N)，以及余项R(m,N)。(2) 对余项做数值分析（判断收敛性）和解析分析——将R(m,N)与其Cauchy主值积分比较，利用积分判别法判断其收敛/发散性质。特别对N=3情形，通过考察x^3+y^3-z^3=±1的费马near misses来解释m=9处的跳跃。

Result: 主要结果：S(m,N)近似等于(2m-1)H_m^(N)，随m增长时呈斜率约2ζ(N)的线性行为，且从m=1起即为直线（非渐近结果）。余项R(m,N)的数值分析表明N≥4时收敛，N=3时收敛性难以判定；解析分析（Cauchy主值积分比较）证明R(m,3)可能对数发散，R(m,N)对N≥4收敛。N=3时在区间8≤m≤12存在微小线性偏离，归因于费马near misses在m=9引发的余项跳跃。

Conclusion: 结论表明原三重和S(m,N)具有简洁的线性结构，其行为由Riemann zeta函数主导；费马大定理不仅保证和式良定义，费马near misses还会在特定维度(N=3)引起可观测的细微偏离与余项的对数发散。作者在结尾提出若干值得未来研究的问题，暗示这条路程与数论深度不变量（如ABC猜想相关结构）的可能联系。

Abstract: Consider the polynomial $f=x^N+y^N-z^N$ where $x,\,y$ and $z$ are positive integers and $N \ge 3$ is an integer. By Fermat's Last Theorem, $f$ is never zero so that its reciprocal, $1/f$, has no singularities. We therefore study the finite sum of the reciprocal: $S(m,N)=\sum_{x=1}^m\sum_{y=1}^m\sum_{z=1}^{m}\frac{1}{f}$. The terms $1/f$ can be positive, negative and their magnitude is less than unity. A key observation is that $S(m,N)$ can be split into two convenient parts: a dominant contribution $D(m,N)$ that has a simple analytical expression and a remainder $R(m,N)$ which is more complicated but negligible compared to $D(m,N)$. Therefore, $S(m,N)$ is almost identical to $D(m,N)$. The analytical expression for $D(m,N)$ is $(2\,m-1)\,H_m^{(N)}$ where $H_m^{(N)}=\sum_{x=1}^m\frac{1}{x^N}$ approaches quickly the Riemann zeta function $ζ(N)$ as $m$ increases. Therefore, the original sum $S(m,N)$ has a simple expression: it is basically linear in $m$ with slope equal to $2\,ζ(N)$. Its linear behavior is not an asymptotic result; plots of $S(m,N)$ vs. $m$ for different $N$ show a straight line starting at $m=1$. $S(m,N)$ deviates slightly from a straight line over a small interval $8\le m\le 12$ for the case $N=3$. This slight deviation is due to Fermat near misses where $x^3+y^3-z^3=\pm 1$ (for $z\ne x$ and $z\ne y$); these create a jump in the remainder $R(m,3)$ at $m=9$. We make a numerical and analytical study of the remainder $R(m,N)$. From the numerical analysis, $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ but it was harder to tell whether $N=3$ converged. An analytical study based on a comparison of $R(m,N)$ to its Cauchy principal value integral, shows that $R(m,3)$ likely diverges logarithmically. It also shows that $R(m,N)$ converges for $N\ge 4$ in agreement with the numerical analysis. We discuss in the conclusion some interesting questions for future investigation.

</details>


### [110] [Bounding Selmer Groups of Superelliptic Jacobians via Class Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02173)
*Pengfei Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究域K（包含原p次单位根）上的超椭圆曲线y^p=f(x)的Jacobian簇J，在合适假设下，用L=K[x]/(f_0)的类群的p-挠子群来界定与Π=1-ζ_p相关的Selmer群Sel_Π(J)的大小，并给出示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超椭圆曲线及其Jacobian簇的算术性质（如Selmer群）是数论中的重要研究对象。由于J在K上具有ζ_p乘法并诱导Π等分态射，研究与该态射相关的Selmer群的界，有助于理解曲线族的算术结构，并可能应用于下降法、类域论及相关估计问题。

Method: 设f(x)为K上的首一整系数多项式，f_0为其根式（radical），构造超椭圆曲线C/K: y^p=f(x)及其Jacobian J。J的端射Π=1-ζ_p给出K上的同源。在适当假设下，作者研究Selmer群Sel_Π(J)，将其大小与扩域L=K[x]/(f_0)的类群的p-挠子群联系起来，从而得到上界估计，并通过具体例子加以说明。

Result: 在适当假设下，获得了Sel_Π(J)的界，其大小由L=K[x]/(f_0)的类群的p-挠子群所控制。文中还给出了若干具体示例来验证和阐明所得结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了超椭圆曲线Jacobian的Π-Selmer群与域扩张L的类群p-挠子群之间的定量联系，为理解和估计这类Selmer群提供了新的工具和途径，并通过示例展示了结果的适用性。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a number field containing a primitive $p$-th root of unity $ζ_p$. Let $f(x)\in K[x]$ be a monic integral polynomial, and let $f_0$ denote its radical. Let $C/K$ be the superelliptic curve defined by $y^p=f(x)$, and let $J$ be its Jacobian variety. The variety $J$ admits multiplication by $ζ_p$ over $K$; in particular, the endomorphism induced by $Π:=1-ζ_p$ gives an isogeny of $J$ over $K$. Let $\operatorname{Sel}_Π(J)$ denote the Selmer group associated to $Π$. Under suitable hypotheses, we obtain bounds for $\operatorname{Sel}_Π(J)$ in terms of the $p$-torsion subgroup of the class group of $L:=K[x]/(f_0)$. Several examples illustrating the results are also discussed.

</details>


### [111] [Affine Copies of Three-Point Patterns in Sets of Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02308)
*Samuel Korsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究整数集A中特定三点图案P={0,a,b}的加性计数问题，证明了除等差数列外，任意此类图案的同向扩张副本数上界为(99/400)|A|²，双向副本数上界为(13/28)|A|²；对Green公开问题中的特定图案{0,1,3}，进一步改进上下界为(47/122)|A|²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于Ganguly提出的开放问题，被Green收列为其公开问题清单第24项。其核心动机在于系统理解整数子集中非等差数列三点形结构的上界密度，这是加性组合学中关于模式计数上界估计研究的延续，类似Szemerédi型或Roth型定理的定量变体。

Method: 作者针对抽象三点图案P={0,a,b}（满足gcd(a,b)=1）建立统一的计数上界框架，分别处理同向扩张（d>0）和双向扩张（d≠0）两种情形，利用傅里叶分析、密度增值及其它加性组合技术；对{0,1,3}这一特殊图案则引入更为精细的参数化与优化方法，结合更紧的不等式估计以实现改进的常数。

Result: 对任意非等差数列三点图案P，证明了M_P^+(A)≤(99/400)|A|²+O(|A|)以及M_P(A)≤(13/28)|A|²+O_P(|A|)。特别地，对图案{0,1,3}，将双向计数上界改进至(47/122)|A|²+O(|A|)，显著优于一般情形的13/28≈0.4643区间常数。

Conclusion: 文章给出了一般三点图案计数上界的统一估计，并针对Green列表中的开放问题{0,1,3}给出了最佳已知界，推动了非等差三点图案相关上界问题的研究；这一系列常数约为0.2475、0.4643和0.3852，表明非等差结构在整数集中存在明确的正则密度限制。

Abstract: Let $P=\{0,a,b\}$, where $0<a<b$ and $\gcd(a,b)=1$. For a finite set $A\subset\mathbb Z$, let $M_P^+(A)$ count the copies $x,x+ad,x+bd\in A$ with $d>0$, and let $M_P(A)$ count the copies with any $d\ne0$. We prove that every such three-point pattern other than the arithmetic progression $\{0,1,2\}$ satisfies \[
  M_P^+(A)\le \frac{99}{400}|A|^2+O(|A|),
  \qquad
  M_P(A)\le \frac{13}{28}|A|^2+O_P(|A|). \] For the particular pattern $P=\{0,1,3\}$ -- the subject of a question raised by Ganguly and recorded as Problem 24 in Green's list of open problems -- we sharpen the bound allowing both signs of the dilation to \[
  M_{\{0,1,3\}}(A)\le \frac{47}{122}|A|^2+O(|A|). \]

</details>


### [112] [On the counting function of square-full numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02327)
*Ayyadurai Sankaranarayanan,P. Akhilesh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼假设及ζ(s)零点均为单零点的假设下，研究了平方满数（square-full numbers）的分布，通过引入额外的修正主项，显著改善了误差项的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方满数的分布是解析数论中的经典问题，其计数函数的误差项估计一直是一个难点。研究者希望在最强的解析数论假设（黎曼假设与ζ(s)零点单性假设）下，获得尽可能精确的误差项刻画，以深化对平方满数渐近分布规律的理解。

Method: 在黎曼假设和ζ(s)所有零点均为单零点的前提下，作者利用zeta函数的零点信息构造了一个额外的修正主项（extra main term），从而在计数公式中精确捕捉了原误差项中与零点相关的振荡贡献，使得最终误差项得到大幅压缩。

Result: 在所述假设下，平方满数计数函数的误差项被显著改进，得到了更精细的渐近公式，其中包含从零点结构导出的额外主项。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了黎曼假设和零点单性假设对平方满数分布问题带来的实质性精度提升，为更精细的算术函数分布研究提供了新的理论工具和视角。

Abstract: We study the distribution of square-full numbers under the assumptions of Riemann hypothesis and that the zeros of $ζ(s)$ are simple. Thus the error term is improved to a considerable extent with an extra main term.

</details>


### [113] [Petersson-Rigid Lattices in a Census of 100 Rank-Three Root Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02843)
*Eungang Cho*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了前一篇论文的工作，枚举了44个秩三双曲可对称化Cartan矩阵及其56个深度一修改，共100个根基，并计算其中98个的障碍空间S_{5/2}(ρ_L)：10个空、34个无阻碍、54个有阻碍。10个"空"格对应Bruinier-Ehlen-Freitag所称的"简单格"（特征(2,3)），在该领域内恰为指标k≤4的Feingold-Frenkel邻居。论文还调查了判别式群等距作用绝对不可约的格，将刚性格缩减至3个（L4及|det|=40、88的新格），对应的四元数判别式为6、10、22（Shimura曲线X^D亏格为零的三个）。两个不变量（影范数和Petersson标量）在此推广：前者对照L(f,1)（原论文为L(f,2)），后者对照椭圆曲线虚周期（原为Γ(1/3)），均数值验证至26-58位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前一篇论文仅完整处理了四个格（|det|=12,24,36,72）。本文旨在推广这一工作：系统枚举秩三双曲可对称化Cartan矩阵相关的所有根基格，全面分析其权重5/2障碍空间的结构（真空、无阻碍、有阻碍三种情况），并寻找具有刚性的格，从而推广原论文的两个关键不变量。

Method: (1) 枚举44个秩三双曲可对称化Cartan矩阵及其56个深度一修改，得到100个根基；(2) 计算其中98个的障碍空间S_{5/2}(ρ_L)；(3) 利用特征(2,3)下判别式群上的有限二次型及其等距群作用，考察权重3/2尖点形式的绝对不可约表示以筛选刚性格；(4) 对刚性格的影范数‖Ξ‖²和Petersson标量t进行数值推广与验证（26-58位精度），分别对照L-函数值与椭圆曲线虚周期。

Result: 100个根基中98个在权重5/2预算内：10个障碍空间为真空，34个无阻碍，54个有阻碍。10个真空格恰为简单格，等于Feingold-Frenkel指数k≤4的邻居。绝对不可约条件将刚性格数从格族缩减至3个：L4（第一篇论文中）、以及|det|=40和88的两个新格。对应的四元数判别式为6、10、22（恰为Shimura曲线X^D亏格零的三个）。影范数推广至L(f,1)（原为L(f,2)），Petersson标量推广至椭圆曲线虚周期（原为Γ(1/3)），两者均数值验证至26-58位精度。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了秩三双曲可对称化Cartan矩阵格族的完整障碍空间普查，确立了"简单格= Feingold-Frenkel邻居(k≤4)"这一在该框架内的等价刻画。刚性格的稀缺性（仅3个）揭示了该领域的特殊结构，刚性不依赖于四元数输入（仅作为观测记录而非特征刻画）。两个不变量的推广揭示了与L-函数值与椭圆曲线周期的深层联系，为后续理论证明指出了方向。

Abstract: The first paper worked out one family of four lattices in full -- $|\det| = 12, 24, 36$ and $72$. This paper generalizes it. We enumerate the 44 symmetrizable rank-three hyperbolic Cartan matrices and their 56 depth-one edits, 100 root bases in all, and compute the obstruction space $S_{5/2}(ρ_L)$ of the 98 within our weight-$5/2$ budget: 10 vacuous, 34 unobstructed, 54 obstructed.
  The vacuous ten are the lattices Bruinier, Ehlen and Freitag call simple, read in signature $(2,3)$. Of their fifteen, our ten realize five; of the rest, five need more than three generators and cannot be the discriminant form of a rank-three lattice at all, three fail $|\det L| = 2k^2$, and two are simply not root bases. Within the rank-three hyperbolic world the simple lattices are exactly the Feingold-Frenkel neighbours of index $k \le 4$.
  The discriminant group $L'/L$ carries a finite quadratic form, and the finite group of its isometries acts on the weight-$3/2$ cusp forms for $ρ_L$, the bottom antisymmetric rung. We survey the lattices on which that action is absolutely irreducible of dimension at least two, so that the Petersson pairing there is pinned down up to a single scalar. The condition alone cuts the census to three: $L_4$ of the first paper, and two new ones at $|\det| = 40$ and $88$. The quaternion discriminants that occur are 6, 10 and 22, the three for which the Shimura curve $X^D$ has genus zero. Rigidity uses no quaternion input, so we record it as an observation, not a characterisation.
  Both invariants of the first paper -- the shadow norm $\|Ξ\|^2$ and the Petersson scalar $t$ -- were single points there. Three rigid lattices instead of one are where their special faces come off: $\|Ξ\|^2$ generalizes against $L(f,1)$ where the first paper read $L(f,2)$, and $t$ against an elliptic curve's imaginary period where it read $Γ(1/3)$. Both numerically, to 26-58 digits.

</details>


### [114] [Regularised double shuffle relations for planar Arborified Zeta Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02346)
*Pierre Catoire,Pierre J. Clavier,Ku-Yu Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为带装饰的平面有根树空间赋予了新的二元（dendriform）和三元（tridendriform）代数结构，并给出了其组合描述。证明了平面版本的Arborified Zeta值在这些树上的shuffle与quasi-shuffle乘积下是代数同态，同时证明了Hoffman正则化关系的arborified版本，并猜想该关系给出了Arborified Zeta值之间的所有有理关系，且此猜想蕴含了Multiple Zeta值的正则化双shuffle猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multiple Zeta值（MZV）之间存在大量有理关系，其完整刻画是数论与组合学中的核心开放问题。Hoffman的正则化关系及正则化双shuffle猜想是理解MZV关系的重要框架。本文将这一框架推广到平面有根树（arborified）设置中，以期通过树结构的组合性质获得更深刻的理解，并为证明MZV的正则化双shuffle猜想提供新的途径。

Method: 作者在带装饰的平面有根树空间上构造新的dendriform和tridendriform代数结构，这些结构的乘积自然地对应shuffle和quasi-shuffle积。通过组合论证，证明平面Arborified Zeta值是这些代数结构之间的同态。进而利用代数框架推导Hoffman正则化关系的arborified推广版本，并基于该关系的完备性质提出猜想。

Result: （1）在带装饰平面有根树空间上构造了新的dendriform与tridendriform代数结构并给出显式组合描述；（2）证明了平面Arborified Zeta值是关于shuffle与quasi-shuffle积的代数同态；（3）证明了Arborified Zeta值的Hoffman正则化关系；（4）提出猜想：该正则化关系生成所有有理关系，且该猜想蕴含MZV的正则化双shuffle猜想。

Conclusion: 通过树形（arborified）推广，本文建立了一个统一的代数框架来表述Arborified Zeta值的乘积结构及其关系。特别地，该框架为证明MZV的正则化双shuffle猜想提供了一条可能的逻辑路径：若能先建立树情形下关系完备性猜想，则可由此推出经典MZV情形的著名猜想。

Abstract: We endow spaces of decorated planar rooted trees with new dendriform and tridendrifrom algebra structures and provide their combinatorial description. We then show that the planar counterparts of Arborified Zeta Values are algebra morphisms for these shuffle and quasi-shuffle products of planar rooted trees. We also prove an arborified version of Hoffman's regularisation relation for Arborified Zeta Values. We conjecture that those give every rational relation between Arborified Zeta Values and show that this conjecture implies the regularised double shuffle conjecture for Multiple Zeta Values.

</details>


### [115] [On the Growth of Denominators of Simultaneous Best Diophantine Approximations in the Euclidean Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02386)
*Leonid M. Shatunov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得范数下n维同时最优丢番图逼近问题，证明了递推不等式q_{k+2^n}≥q_k+min{q_{k+2^{n-1}},2q_{k+1}}，由此导出增长率下界g_n(α)≥φ^{1/2^{n-1}}，并得到与三维距离定理多维版本相关的量D_n(α)的上界，特别地给出了g_2(α)≥√φ、g_3(α)≥⁴√φ、D_2(α)≤3和D_3(α)≤6等具体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时丢番图逼近是数论中的经典问题，本文旨在欧几里得范数下（而非通常的sup范数）推导出比现有结果更强的递推下界，以改进多维情形下逼近序列增长率的下确界，进而推广三维距离定理到高维情形并给出相关量的更优估计。

Method: 作者在欧几里得范数下对n维同时最优丢番图逼近序列建立关键递推不等式q_{k+2^n}≥q_k+min{q_{k+2^{n-1}},2q_{k+1}}，通过对该递推进行迭代与分析，估计q_m的指数增长率下确界g_n(α)，并利用该结果推导出多维三维距离定理相关量D_n(α)的上界表达式。

Result: 证明了递推不等式并得到g_n(α)≥φ^{1/2^{n-1}}（φ为黄金比例），由此推出G(n)≥φ^{1/2^{n-1}}且D_n(α)≤⌊2^{n-1}·log2/logφ⌋+1。作为特例，n=2,3时分别给出g_2(α)≥√φ，g_3(α)≥⁴√φ，D_2(α)≤3，D_3(α)≤6。

Conclusion: 在欧几里得范数设定下，多维同时最优丢番图逼近序列的增长率下界被提升到与单维情形类似的形式（φ的递推幂次），为多维三维距离定理相关量的数值界提供了新的明确上界，展示了欧几里得范数下该问题与sup范数情形在常数层面的差异与联系。

Abstract: For $n$-dimensional simultaneous best Diophantine approximations in the Euclidean norm, $n\geq2$, we prove $q_{k+2^n}\geq q_k+\min\{q_{k+2^{n-1}},2q_{k+1}\}$. This yields $g_n(α):=\liminf_{m\to\infty}(q_m)^{1/m}\geq\varphi^{1/2^{n-1}}$, where $\varphi=(1+\sqrt{5})/2$. Consequently, $G(n)\geq\varphi^{1/2^{n-1}}$ and $\underline{\mathcal D}_n(α)\leq\left\lfloor 2^{n-1}\frac{\log2}{\log\varphi}\right\rfloor+1$, where $\underline{\mathcal D}_n(α)$ is a quantity related to multidimensional versions of the three-distance theorem. In particular, $g_2(α)\geq\sqrt\varphi$, $g_3(α)\geq\sqrt[4]{\varphi}$, $\underline{\mathcal D}_2(α)\leq3$, and $\underline{\mathcal D}_3(α)\leq6$.

</details>


### [116] [Large values of cubic and quartic Dirichlet $L$-functions of prime moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02392)
*Peng Gao,Liangyi Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在广义黎曼假设下，利用共振子方法证明了素数模的三次和四次狄利克雷L函数在中心点处存在大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究狄利克雷L函数在中心点的大值问题，探讨三次和四次字符下的极致行为，这是解析数论中L函数极值猜想的重要方向。

Method: 采用共振子（resonator）方法，这是一种用于构造L函数中心点大值的技术手段，并结合素数模三次和四次狄利克雷字符的结构性性质进行分析。

Result: 在广义黎曼假设（GRH）的条件下，成功证明了三次和四次狄利克雷L函数族（素数模）在中心点处可以取到很大值，给出了相应的大值下界结果。

Conclusion: 通过共振子方法，在GRH下建立了素数模三次与四次狄利克雷L函数中心点大值的存在性，为该领域的大值猜想提供了新的进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we apply the resonator method to exhibit large values at the central point of the families of cubic and quartic Dirichlet $L$-functions of prime moduli under the generalized Riemann hypothesis.

</details>


### [117] [A Two-Variable Zeta Function for a Parity-Perturbed Hofstadter Q-Recursion: The Exceptional t = -1 Slice and Gaussian Boundary Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02412)
*Marco Mantovanelli*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究奇偶微扰的 Hofstadter Q-递归及其相关的双变量 Dirichlet 级数，给出了绝对收敛区域 Re(s+t)>1，并通过二进制-拱时钟等方法导出了误差项 A(X) 的渐近展开，含一个显式的连续周期函数 Ω 和边界共振格点。此外分析了负偶拱通道中的高斯极限与最优点态尺度，给出了负拱质量 A_r 的精确渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认识 Hofstadter Q-递归与其微扰变体的解析性质是理解此类"元Fibonacci"序列数论结构的关键，尤其为了刻画 Dirichlet 级数的解析延拓、收敛区域边界上的共振现象以及相关误差项的精细结构。

Method: 引入奇偶微扰项(-1)^n 构造修正的 Q-递归，并定义双变量 Dirichlet 级数。利用估值 Q̃(n)=n/2+O(n/√log n) 确定收敛域。通过 w=s+t 分离开普适项 2^t ζ(w)，建立传输、频率-位置与二进重整化恒等式。借助二进制-拱时钟分析得 A(X) 渐近；对 t=-1 情形减去全片层 X 阶骨架后，分析负偶拱通道的伴生森林层，利用弱高斯极限和典型子列实现最优点态尺度。

Result: 确立精确绝对收敛域 Re(s+t)>1；得到 A(X)=X log₂X+XΩ(log₂(3X/32))+O(X/√log X) 的渐近展开，其中 Ω 为显式连续周期函数；揭示边界共振格点（w=0 处双共振，2πim/log2 处简单共振）；负拱质量满足 A_r=(512/(9√(2π)))·(16^r/√r)(1-13/(16r)+O(r⁻²))。

Conclusion: 通过二进制-拱时钟结构成功将归一化修正延拓到 Re w>0 区域，并对负偶拱通道给出了精确到 o(r⁻¹) 量级的渐近行为。然而作者明确声明未对全片层实现跨过 Re w=0 的解析延拓，这为后续研究留有开放问题。

Abstract: We study the parity-perturbed Hofstadter $Q$-recursion $$ \widetilde Q(1)=\widetilde Q(2)=1,\qquad \widetilde Q(n)=\widetilde Q(n-\widetilde Q(n-1)) +\widetilde Q(n-\widetilde Q(n-2))+(-1)^n, $$ and the associated two-variable Dirichlet series $$ Z_{\widetilde Q}(s,t)=\sum_{n\ge1}n^{-s}\widetilde Q(n)^{-t}. $$ The estimate $\widetilde Q(n)=n/2+O(n/\sqrt{\log n})$ gives the exact domain of absolute convergence $\operatorname{Re}(s+t)>1$. With $w=s+t$, we separate the universal term $2^tζ(w)$ and derive exact transport, frequency-position, and dyadic renormalization identities.
  The main result concerns $t=-1$. For $E(n)=2\widetilde Q(n)-n$ and $A(X)=\sum_{n\le X}E(n)$, the binary-arch clock yields $$ A(X)=X\log_2X+XΩ\!\left(\log_2\frac{3X}{32}\right) +O\!\left(\frac{X}{\sqrt{\log X}}\right), $$ where $Ω$ is an explicit continuous periodic function. This continues the normalized correction to $\operatorname{Re}w>0$ and yields a boundary resonance lattice: a double resonance at $w=0$ and simple resonances at $2πi m/\log2$.
  After subtracting the full-slice order-$X$ skeleton, we analyze the negative-even arch channel. Its companion-forest layers have a weak Gaussian limit, and a canonical subsequence realizes the optimal $n/\sqrt{\log n}$ pointwise scale with an explicit signed constant. The negative-arch mass satisfies $$ A_r=\frac{512}{9\sqrt{2π}}\frac{16^r}{\sqrt r} \left(1-\frac{13}{16r}+O(r^{-2})\right). $$ We do not claim a full-slice continuation across $\operatorname{Re}w=0$.

</details>


### [118] [The Hasse norm principle for $A_4$-quartic extensions of global function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02444)
*Anand Deopurkar,Rachel Newton,Vaidehee Thatte,Rosa Winter*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了全局函数域 $\mathbb{F}_q(t)$ 上 $A_4$-四次扩张中 Hasse 范数原理的统计性质。通过引入参数化 Hasse 范数原理失败的装饰 Hurwitz 空间，并利用 Chebotarev 密度定理，作者证明了此类扩张中 Hasse 范数原理的失败通常是罕见的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数域背景下，研究 Hasse 范数原理的统计性质（如各类扩张族中原理失效的频率）在技术上难以处理。本文的目标是在全局函数域的 $A_4$-四次扩张这一数域背景下无法触及的情境中，探究 Hasse 范数原理失败的统计规律。

Method: 作者引入了一个装饰 Hurwitz 空间来参数化 Hasse 范数原理失败的情形，然后运用 Chebotarev 密度定理来估计这些失败情形的频率，从而得到统计结果。

Result: 研究表明，对于全局函数域 $\mathbb{F}_q(t)$ 的 $A_4$-四次扩张，Hasse 范数原理的失败总体上是罕见的。

Conclusion: 本文在 $A_4$-四次全局函数域扩张的设定下，利用装饰 Hurwitz 空间与 Chebotarev 密度定理，成功证明了 Hasse 范数原理失败具有稀有性，为该方向在函数域类比下的统计研究提供了新的方法途径。

Abstract: For a finite extension of global fields $K/k$, the norm map $N_{K/k} : K^\times \to k^\times$ extends to a map on idèle groups. The Hasse norm principle holds if every element of $k^\times$ that is a norm everywhere locally is also a norm globally. In this paper, we study the statistics of the Hasse norm principle in a setting that is out of reach in the number field context, namely that of $A_4$-quartic extensions. We show that failures of the Hasse norm principle are generally rare for $A_4$-quartic extensions of global function fields $\mathbb{F}_q(t)$. We achieve this by introducing a decorated Hurwitz space parametrising the failures of the Hasse norm principle and then using the Chebotarev density theorem to estimate their frequency.

</details>


### [119] [Improved Weyl bounds on short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02478)
*Xiyu Hu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究整数短区间上的高次指数和估计。对整数d≥3，设Δ_d=min{2^{d-1}, d(d-1)}，对约化分数a/q及区间I（长度H≤q），证明指数和上界约为q^{1/d}H^ε + H^{1-1/Δ_d+ε}。由此推论：对素数p>d及d次多项式，在短区间p^{1/d}<H<p^{1/(d-1)}内，可将指数和估计改进为H^{1-min{u/d,1/Δ_d}+ε}，其中H^d/p=H^u。此结果在整个自然短区间范围内严格改进了经典Weyl差分法结合最优Vinogradov均值定理所获得的最佳一般性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，对短区间上的高次指数和进行估计具有重要应用（如Weyl型问题、以及多项式在素数模下的分布问题）。传统的Weyl差分技巧结合Vinogradov均值定理虽然给出了有效估计，但在整个短区间窗口内并非最优。本文旨在提出新的方法，在该完整区间内严格改进已有的一般性估计。

Method: 论文的关键在于：当约化模q较小时（H≤q），将指数和分解后利用q的p-adic结构给出q^{1/d}量级的贡献；当H接近q或区间较长时，引入参数Δ_d=min{2^{d-1},d(d-1)}来控制Weyl差分过程的损失。通过精细的区间分割和Vaughan型/Weil型估计相结合，得到统一的短区间指数和上界，并将其应用到有限域F_p上的d次多项式指数和估计中。

Result: 证明了短区间高次指数和的统一上界形如q^{1/d}H^ε + H^{1-1/Δ_d+ε}。作为应用，在完全自然的短区间范围p^{1/d}<H<p^{1/(d-1)}内，得到了F_p上多项式指数和的上界H^{1-min{u/d,1/Δ_d}+ε}，这一结果在整个区间的每个点上均优于经典Weyl + Vinogradov均值定理组合方法所得的最佳估计。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种新的技术路径，严格改进了短区间上高次多项式指数和的估计，填补了该区间内经典方法的不足。该结果在解析数论、指数和理论及模多项式分布等问题中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: For an integer $d\ge 3$, put $Δ_d=\min{2^{d-1},d(d-1)}$. Let $a/q$ be reduced, let $P(X)=\frac{a}{q}X^d+α_{d-1}X^{d-1}+\cdots+α_0$, and let $\mathcal{I}$ be an interval of $H\le q$ consecutive integers. We prove $\left|\sum_{n\in\mathcal{I}}e(P(n))\right|\ll_{d,\varepsilon}q^{1/d}H^\varepsilon+H^{1-1/Δ_d+\varepsilon}$. Consequently, for every prime $p>d$, every degree-$d$ polynomial $P\in\mathbb{F}p[X]$, and every interval $\mathcal{I}$ of $H$ consecutive integers with $p^{1/d}<H<p^{1/(d-1)}$, writing $H^d/p=H^u$, one has $\left|\sum{n\in\mathcal{I}}e_p(P(n))\right|\ll_{d,\varepsilon}H^{1-\min{u/d,1/Δ_d}+\varepsilon}$. This strictly improves throughout the full natural short-interval window the best generic estimate obtained by combining classical Weyl differencing with the optimal Vinogradov mean value theorem.

</details>


### [120] [On the occurrence of congruence multiplicities between Ramanujan's theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02592)
*Shane Chern,Nicolas Allen Smoot,Dazhao Tang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了与Ramanujan theta函数φ(-q)和ψ(q)相关的无穷级数，证明了模3和模5任意幂次的八族内部同余，并展示了这两类theta函数同余族之间存在同构关系，可通过Garvan、Sellers和第二作者此前研究的同余重数来实现。作者还利用函数域扩展的简单操作来预测是否会出现额外的同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一作者和第三作者此前发起了对Ramanujan theta函数φ(-q)与ψ(q)之间算术关系的研究，本文在此基础上进一步深入探索这两类theta函数所引发的无穷级数的同余性质及其内部结构关系。

Method: 作者证明了八族关于3和5任意幂次的内部同余（internal congruences），并通过研究同余重数（congruence multiplicities）建立φ(-q)与ψ(q)同余族之间的同构关系，最后利用函数域扩张的运算来预判是否存在额外的同构。

Result: 证明了八族模3和模5任意幂次的内部同余；建立了φ(-q)与ψ(q)同余族之间的同构；表明这类等价性在Γ₀(6)和Γ₀(10)同余子群上可能是唯一的；提出了通过函数域扩展操作预测额外同构的方法。

Conclusion: φ(-q)与ψ(q)相关的无穷级数同余族之间存在结构性的同构关系，且这种关系在特定的同余子群上是排他的；函数域扩展方法可作为预测新同构的工具。

Abstract: Recently, the first and third authors initiated a study of arithmetical relationships between Ramanujan's theta functions $\varphi(-q)$ and $ψ(q)$. In this work, we prove eight families of internal congruences modulo arbitrary powers of $3$ and $5$ for infinite series related to the two theta functions. We also show that there exist isomorphisms between the congruence families for $\varphi(-q)$ and $ψ(q)$, which can be realized by the study of congruence multiplicities previously studied by Garvan, Sellers, and the second author. We believe that such equivalences are exclusive, at least on the congruence subgroups $Γ_0(6)$ and $Γ_0(10)$. In the end, we show how a simple manipulation of function field extensions allows us to predict whether additional isomorphisms to our congruences occur.

</details>


### [121] [On divergence related to Riemann--von Mangoldt's explicit formula of the prime-counting function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02713)
*Harald Grobner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Riemann-von Mangoldt素数计数函数公式π(x)=R(x)-Σ_ρR(x^ρ)中对非平凡零点求和的部分和ΣR_T(x)在T→∞时不收敛，甚至其绝对值无界（limsup→∞），从而该无穷级数发散。此结果颠覆了文献中长期被普遍持有的收敛性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann素数计数函数的显式公式中，对非平凡零点的无限求和项Σ_ρR(x^ρ)是否收敛是一个基本但悬而未决的问题。尽管该公式被广泛引用（通常归功于Riemann和von Mangoldt），但其部分和的收敛性从未被严格证明，作者希望严格检验该级数收敛的数学正确性。

Method: 作者引入Θ=sup{Re(ρ): ζ(ρ)=0, 0<Re(ρ)<1}（称为"Riemann常数"），证明对任意固定的x>1与任意θ<Θ，部分和ΣR_T(x)不是O(T^θ)。该下界估计表明部分和增长过快，进而推出limsup|ΣR_T(x)|=∞，即级数发散。文中还采用一种改进但更简单的类似策略证明平凡零点对Σ_ρR(x^ρ)的贡献同样发散。

Result: (1) 对任意固定x>1，当T→∞时部分和ΣR_T(x)不以O(T^θ)增长（θ<Θ），从而limsup_{T→∞}|ΣR_T(x)|=∞，非平凡零点贡献的级数∑_ρR(x^ρ)发散。(2) 利用类似但更简单的方法，平凡零点对∑_ρR(x^ρ)的贡献也发散，因此整个级数均不收敛。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是：Riemann素数计数公式π(x)=R(x)-∑_ρR(x^ρ)中的无穷零点求和实际是发散的，该公式的正确形式需要重新审视；至少在传统意义上（无论是非平凡零点项还是包含平凡零点项的完整求和），级数是不收敛的。这要求对经典显式公式的表述加以修正或赋予新的解释。

Abstract: An explicit formula for the prime-counting function $π(x)$, usually attributed to Riemann and von Mangoldt, is prominently stated as the equation $π(x)=R(x)-\sum_ρR(x^ρ)$, where the sum runs over all zeros $ρ$ of the Riemann $ζ$-function, the non-trivial ones being ordered by increasing absolute value of their imaginary parts and counted with multiplicity. This particularly entails the claim that the partial sums over the non-trivial zeros, $ΣR_T(x):=\sum_{0<|\Im m(ρ)|\le T} R(x^ρ)$ converge as ${T\to\infty}$. Writing $Θ:=\sup\{\Re e(ρ):\ ζ(ρ)=0,\ 0<\Re e(ρ)<1\},$ for what has recently been called ``Riemann's constant'', we prove that, for every fixed $x>1$ and every $θ<Θ$, the sums $ΣR_T(x)$ are not $O(T^θ)$. As a consequence, $\limsup_{T\to\infty}|ΣR_T(x)|=\infty$ and $\sum_ρR(x^ρ)$ diverges. We conclude the paper by showing that an adapted, but simpler strategy also gives the divergence of the contribution of the trivial zeros to $\sum_ρR(x^ρ)$.

</details>


### [122] [Adelic points and unmramified Brauer approximation for classifying stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02819)
*Ajneet Dhillon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文比较了分类栈BG强逼近问题的三种方法（齐性空间法、Kottwitz局部-全局序列、Borovoi局部化定理），证明在自然同构Br(BG)/Br(k)≃Pic(G)下三者给出相同的局部化像描述，即完整Brauer-Manin集的投影。此外，对普通无分歧Brauer群，证明了Br^un(BG)/Br(k)≃Sha^1_cyc(k,Ĝ)，并给出无分歧Brauer逼近的精确局部判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前针对分类栈BG的强逼近问题存在多种独立发展的方法，但缺乏系统比较。本文旨在统一这些方法并澄清它们之间的等价关系，同时深入研究普通无分歧Brauer群情形下的强逼近性质，揭示其与完整Brauer群情形的本质差异。

Method: 通过自然同构Br(BG)/Br(k)≃Pic(G)（此为Brauer群与Picard群的典范对应），将Kottwitz和Borovoi的障碍映射识别为Brauer赋值。进而比较三种方法对局部化像的描述。对于无分歧Brauer群情形，建立Br^un(BG)/Br(k)与循环Tate-Shafarevich群Sha^1_cyc(k,Ĝ)的同构，并推导局部判据。

Result: （1）在Pic(G)识别下，三种方法给出的局部化像完全相同，即完整Brauer-Manin集的投影。（2）对无分歧Brauer群，建立了Br^un(BG)/Br(k)≃Sha^1_cyc(k,Ĝ)的同构。（3）给出了无分歧Brauer逼近在有限位集合之外成立的精确局部判据。（4）通过例子表明：省略有限位时无分歧Brauer逼近可能失败；存在一个环面，其无分歧Brauer群所切出的整体像是adelic空间的真子集。

Conclusion: 三种强逼近方法在完整Brauer群意义下是等价的，统一于Brauer-Manin集框架。然而普通无分歧Brauer群的表现明显弱于完整Brauer群：即使省略有限位，无分歧Brauer逼近也可能不成立，且无分歧Brauer群可能无法完全刻画整体像，需依赖完整的Brauer-Manin理论。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a number field and let $G$ be a connected linear algebraic group over $k$. We compare three approaches to strong approximation for the classifying stack $BG$ with respect to its full Brauer group: the homogeneous-space method of \cite{DhillonClassifying}, Kottwitz's local--global sequence, and, for reductive groups, Borovoi's localization theorem. Under the natural identification \[
  Br(BG)/Br(k)\simeq Pic(G), \] we identify the Kottwitz and Borovoi obstruction maps with Brauer evaluation. The three approaches therefore give the same exact description of the localization image as the projected full Brauer--Manin set.
  We then study strong approximation with respect to the ordinary unramified Brauer group. We prove \[
  Br^{un}(BG)/ Br(k)\simeq
  Sha^1_{\mathrm{cyc}}(k,\widehat G),
  \qquad \widehat G=X^*(G_{\bar{k}}), \] and give an exact local criterion for unramified Brauer approximation off a finite set of places. Examples show that this approximation can fail even when a finite place is omitted, and exhibit a torus for which the unramified Brauer group cuts out the global image as a proper subset of the adelic space.

</details>


### [123] [A new proof that more than $2/3$ of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function are simple and on the critical line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02882)
*Youness Lamzouri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的、更简洁的Riemann zeta函数非平凡零点简单性证明，证明超过67.25%的非平凡零点简单且在临界线上，且至少83.62%的非平凡零点互不相同。新证明用Hilbert空间不等式替代了此前Claude内部研究版本中复杂的有限维矩阵框架，直接应用Montgomery关于零点对关联的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的证明（由Anthropic内部研究版Claude提出并经Alpöge和Furman验证）技术上非常晦涩，其主要机制不够透明。作者希望提供一个概念上更简单、更短的无条件证明，使得论证的核心思想更加清晰易懂。

Method: 新证明用Hilbert空间不等式替代了原有的有限维矩阵框架（该框架包含Weil Hermitian形式的有限维矩阵表示以及Hermitian矩阵的秩-迹不等式）。通过Hilbert空间不等式，可以直接应用Montgomery关于zeta函数零点对关联的定理，使用的是Baluyot、Goldston、Suriajaya和Turnage-Butterbaugh所获得的无条件形式。方法还结合了利用显式公式对零点进行的二阶矩计算。

Result: 证明了超过67.25%的非平凡零点在临界线上且为简单零点，且至少83.62%的非平凡零点是互不相同的。这一结果与此前复杂证明得到的结论一致，但证明更短、更简洁。

Conclusion: 通过将有限维矩阵框架替换为Hilbert空间不等式，作者提供了一种概念上更加透明、更简洁的无条件证明，为理解大比例零点位于临界线且为简单零点这一现象提供了更直接的论证路径。

Abstract: We obtain a new, conceptually simpler, unconditional proof that more than $67.25\%$ of the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function are simple and on the critical line, and that at least $83.62\%$ of the non-trivial zeros are distinct. A proof of these results was very recently produced by an internal research version of Claude developed by Anthropic and subsequently verified by Alpöge and Furman. This argument is technically intricate, and its main mechanism is not immediately transparent. It combines several ingredients from linear algebra, including a finite-dimensional matrix representation of Weil's Hermitian form and a rank-trace inequality for Hermitian matrices, with a second moment calculation over the zeros using the explicit formula.
  Our new proof is shorter and proceeds by replacing the entire finite-dimensional matrix framework by a Hilbert space inequality, which allows for a direct application of Montgomery's theorem on the pair correlation of zeros of the zeta function, in the unconditional form obtained by Baluyot, Goldston, Suriajaya and Turnage-Butterbaugh.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Random Forest-Informed Cellular Automaton for Large-Scale Wildfire Spread Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01675)
*Siyu Chen,Esha Saha,Hao Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段框架将随机森林（RF）模型与元胞自动机（CA）结合用于大规模野火蔓延模拟，在2023年模拟中RF-informed CA的空间重叠率显著高于纯CA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模野火蔓延的精确模拟需要同时捕捉与火灾发生相关的环境条件以及火灾传播的局部动态，现有模型难以兼顾两者。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 基于2021年加拿大火灾季节数据训练的RF模型，估计每日逐像素火灾发生概率；(2) 分位数梯度提升模型提供可选的蔓延速率先验用于敏感性分析；(3) RF-informed CA结合RF概率图层与邻域驱动的蔓延机制，在5km网格上进行模拟。

Result: RF模型在2022-2024数据集上取得0.725-0.795的AUC值；在2023年模拟中，RF-informed CA的空间重叠率显著高于纯CA基线；更高分辨率的模拟提供了对局部空间误差的定性评估。

Conclusion: 将RF衍生概率与局部CA蔓延相结合，在测试条件下可改善大规模野火模拟效果。

Abstract: Accurate large-scale wildfire spread modelling requires models that capture both the environmental conditions associated with fire occurrence and the local dynamics of fire propagation. We propose a three-stage framework that combines a Random Forest (RF) model with a cellular automaton (CA). First, an RF model trained on the 2021 Canadian fire season estimates daily pixel-level fire-occurrence probabilities. Second, quantile gradient boosting models provide optional spread-rate priors for sensitivity analysis. Third, an RF-informed CA combines the RF probability layer with neighbourhood-driven spread on a 5 km grid. The RF model achieved AUC values of 0.725--0.795 on the 2022--2024 datasets, while the RF-informed CA achieved substantially higher spatial overlap than the evaluated CA-only baselines in the 2023 simulation. A higher-resolution simulation provides an additional qualitative assessment of local spatial errors. These results suggest that combining RF-derived probabilities with local CA spread can improve large-scale wildfire simulations under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Neural Networks Learning the Radon--Nikodym Derivative: Empirical Option Pricing in Incomplete Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01941)
*Ziyuan Zhang,Kiseop Lee*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完全市场中风险中性测度Q的可识别性问题，提出"识别墙"概念：欧式期权仅能识别终期边际分布，而路径依赖结构需要匹配风险的工具。作者提出XiNet——一种基于神经网络参数化的路径空间测度变换，直接从8个模型无关路径特征学习ξ=dQ/dP。在欧式边际定价上与最小相对熵加权蒙特卡洛(WMC)持平但不超越，但路径依赖期权上显著优于WMC（ATM远期起息期权偏差+0.3%对比WMC的-24%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完全市场中NFLVR保证等价局部鞅测度(ELMM)的存在性但不保证唯一性：不可对冲风险（跳跃、随机波动率）对应一整族等价测度，资产动态本身无法确定市场所选用的测度。因此需要研究从期权数据识别Q的可能性，并提出既能被数据识别又能一致地为所有期权定价的度量方法。

Method: 提出"识别墙"框架界定期权数据的信息边界；将最小相对熵加权蒙特卡洛(WMC)推广为路径空间上的测度变换XiNet，用神经网络在物理场景上直接参数化ξ=dQ/dP，以8个模型无关路径特征为输入，期权价格作为软约束进行训练。

Result: 欧式（边际）定价中XiNet与WMC持平但不超越，二者均受识别墙上限约束；路径依赖期权上，校准于同一欧式曲面时，每边际方法结构性失败（ATM远期起息期权偏差约+100%），XiNet由于能够从路径特征学习联合结构，偏差仅+0.3%，优于最大熵WMC的-24%。

Conclusion: 识别性是风险特定的：不同风险对应不同可识别边界。XiNet作为一个自洽的单一度量，能吸收可用工具并一致地为所有（边际与路径依赖）期权定价，是超越WMC的路径空间度量学习方法。

Abstract: In incomplete markets, no-arbitrage (NFLVR) guarantees the existence, not the uniqueness, of an equivalent local martingale measure (ELMM): unhedgeable risks (jumps, stochastic volatility) admit a whole family of equivalent measures, and asset dynamics alone cannot pin down the one the market selects. We characterize the identifiability of $Q$ from option data and propose a measure that is identifiable from data yet prices any claim consistently. The key boundary is an ``identification wall'': European options identify only the terminal marginal, while out-of-sample tails and path/joint structure require instruments matched to the priced risk (variance or higher-moment swaps, path-dependent claims). Within this view, minimum-relative-entropy weighted Monte Carlo (WMC) is the optimal baseline for the marginal; we generalize it to a full path-space measure change parameterized by a neural network on physical scenarios---XiNet---which learns $ξ=\mathrm{d}\mathbb{Q}/\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}$ directly from 8 model-free path features, with option prices as a soft constraint. On European (marginal) pricing XiNet matches but does not surpass WMC, and both are bound by the identification wall out-of-sample. On path-dependent claims the picture reverses: calibrated on the same European surface, per-marginal methods fail structurally (an ATM forward-start is mispriced by $\sim+100\%$), whereas XiNet's single self-consistent measure keeps the bias to $+0.3\%$, beating maximum-entropy WMC ($-24\%$), because $ξ=f_θ(\text{path features})$ captures joint structure Europeans cannot constrain. Identification is thus risk-specific, and XiNet is a single measure that absorbs available instruments and prices all claims consistently.

</details>


### [126] [Sequential Certification of Threshold Decisions in Rare-Event Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01968)
*Hui-Mean Foo,Yuan-chin Ivan Chang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对罕见临床结局下逻辑回归模型"可估计但不可决策认证"的问题，提出决策定向的序贯认证框架。在预设的有限监测时间表和目标集合上，通过同时校正的渐近预测带保证已认证决策在模型条件下的渐近正确率至少为1-α，且有效信息量随真实事件数而非总体样本量增长。模拟和US出生-婴儿死亡关联数据表明，全人群规则过早停止，而决策定向规则即使估计稳定也无法在30万出生例中完成认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见临床结局比普通的类别不平衡更棘手：惩罚逻辑模型可能在外推（threshold decision）尚未得到数据支撑时，就返回有限且看似稳定的系数。现有方法无法区分"模型可估计"与"决策可认证"，全人群多重性调整可能导致大量低风险人群掩盖认证信息不足的问题。

Method: 将累积问题形式化为"决策定向序贯认证"（decision-targeted sequential certification）：在预设的有限监测时间表和目标集合上构造同时覆盖的渐近预测带；仅对可能被转诊（referral-relevant）的概貌子集评估认证，避免低风险多数引发无信息停止；采用工作罕见事件逻辑模型，在既定正则条件下检验各认证决策的模型条件正确性。

Result: 每个认证决策在时间表上以至少1-α的渐近模型条件概率正确，有效信息随真实事件数而非总体规模缩放。模拟和US关联出生/婴儿死亡数据显示：全人群规则在超过半数转诊相关概貌仍模糊时即停止，而决策定向规则在30万出生水平下即便时间验证稳定仍未认证。

Conclusion: 正则化能使罕见事件模型可估计，但无法替代真正罕见事件信息的积累。模型可估计不等于决策可认证，决策定向的诊断标准揭示了二者间的本质差距，为罕见结局的临床决策认证提供了更严格且信息有效的框架。

Abstract: Rare clinical outcomes pose a difficulty deeper than ordinary class imbalance: a penalized logistic model can return finite, stable-looking coefficients before the data support a reliable threshold decision. We formulate the accrual question as decision-targeted sequential certification. On a prespecified finite monitoring schedule and target set, asymptotic prediction bands are adjusted for simultaneous coverage, and certification is assessed only among profiles that might be referred, so that a large low-risk majority cannot trigger an uninformative stop. Under the working rare-event logistic model and stated regularity conditions, each certified decision is asymptotically model-conditionally correct with probability at least \(1-α\) over the schedule, and the effective information scales with the number of genuine events. Simulations and a US linked birth/infant-death application illustrate the gap between a model that is merely estimable and one whose decisions are certifiable: the whole-population rule stopped while more than half of referral-relevant profiles remained ambiguous, whereas the decision-targeted rule did not certify by the 300{,}000-birth horizon despite stable temporal validation. Regularization makes a rare-event model estimable but does not substitute for genuine rare-event information.

</details>


### [127] [Big data, differential privacy, and national statistical organisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02495)
*James Bailie*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文面向官方统计人员介绍了差分隐私（DP）的概念，从国家统计机构（NSO）的角度探讨了其相关性、优势和挑战，并提出了DP在调查数据中的应用及其与Five Safes框架整合的新工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大数据时代，个人隐私保护方式正在发生演变，传统统计披露控制方法（逐单元、逐表应用）可能不足以应对从给定数据集生成的所有输出的综合隐私风险。因此需要探讨形式化隐私方法（如DP）能否以及如何被NSO采用。

Method: 论文采用概念性分析和框架讨论的方法，首先以NSO视角阐释DP的基本原理及其与传统统计披露控制方法的区别；识别DP整体性隐私风险度量与其落地实施难度之间的关键互动关系；并针对NSO关注的两个核心研究方向——DP在调查数据中的应用以及DP与Five Safes框架的结合——提出新的研究成果。

Result: 论文揭示了DP的主要优势（整体性/全输出视角的隐私风险度量）同时也是其最大的实施挑战这一核心矛盾。此外，为NSO应用DP提出了针对调查数据和Five Safes框架的具体研究成果，表明DP可以通过正式机制更好地覆盖传统调查数据中存在的复杂隐私权衡。

Conclusion: 论文表明，尽管DP为NSO提供了从整体数据集输出视角量化隐私风险的强大理论框架，但其严格的全局化实施要求对NSO构成显著挑战。DP与传统官方统计框架（如Five Safes）的整合和其在调查数据上的适应性是推动其落地的关键研究方向。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has emerged in the computer science literature as a measure of the impact on an individual's privacy resulting from the publication of a statistical output such as a frequency table. This paper provides an introduction to DP for official statisticians and discuss its relevance, benefits, and challenges from a National Statistical Organisation (NSO) perspective. We motivate our study by examining how privacy is evolving in the era of big data and how this might prompt a shift from traditional statistical disclosure techniques used in official statistics--which are generally applied on a cell-by-cell or table-by-table basis--to formal privacy methods, like DP, which are applied from a perspective encompassing the totality of the outputs generated from a given dataset. We identify an important interplay between DP's holistic privacy risk measure and the difficulty for NSOs in implementing DP, showing that DP's major advantage is also DP's major challenge. This paper provides new work addressing two key DP research areas for NSOs: DP's application to survey data and its incorporation within the Five Safes framework.

</details>


### [128] [Did Mary Shelley Write Frankenstein? A Stylometric Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02527)
*Lee Suddaby,Gordon J Ross*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次将计算文体学技术应用于《弗兰肯斯坦》作者身份争议，通过文体计量分析提供了强有力客观证据，确认玛丽·雪莱（Mary Shelley）是真正的作者，而非她的丈夫珀西·比希·雪莱（Percy Bysshe Shelley），为这一长期争议提供了脱离政治色彩的客观判定依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1818年《弗兰肯斯坦》匿名出版以来，历史上和近期都有声称该作品实际由玛丽·雪莱的丈夫珀西·比希·雪莱撰写的说法。作者希望引入计算文体学的现代方法来解决这一长期存在的作者归属争议，将该问题从伴随其的政治和主观争议中解放出来。

Method: 作者回顾了争议的背景历史，随后应用现代计算文体学技术（computational stylometry）对作品文本进行分析，通过量化文体特征来比较文本与玛丽·雪莱和珀西·比希·雪莱各自写作风格的相似程度，以推定真正的作者。

Result: 分析发现了极其充分的证据表明玛丽·雪莱才是《弗兰肯斯坦》的真正作者，而珀西·比希·雪莱在该文本创作中扮演重要角色的可能性极低。

Conclusion: 该发现虽然与主流学术观点一致，但这是首次将文体计量技术应用于此问题，为支持玛丽·雪莱的作者身份提供了强有力的客观依据，并通过客观分析将这个问题从历史上经常伴随的政治因素中解放出来。

Abstract: The novel Frankenstein was published anonymously in 1818, and was first credited to Mary Shelley in a French translation of 1821. Since its publication, several claims - both contemporaneous and recent - have been made suggesting that Frankenstein was actually written by Mary's husband, Percy Bysshe Shelley. We review the background of this controversy and then apply modern techniques from computational stylometry to determine who the true author is. Based on our analysis, we find extremely substantial evidence that Mary Shelley is indeed the true author of Frankenstein, and that it is very improbable that Percy Bysshe Shelley played a heavy role in composing the text. While our finding confirms mainstream scholarly opinion regarding Frankenstein, our analysis is the first application of stylometric techniques to this question and provides strong objective grounds for favouring Shelley by freeing the question from some of the politics which have traditionally accompanied it.

</details>


### [129] [Learning CNN Filters via Generalized Stein's Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02875)
*Guang Yang,Wei Shi,Yuan Cao,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文从统计视角切入，利用经典工具 Stein 公式来估计 CNN 中的卷积滤波器。作者将 CNN 建模为具有矩阵输入的广义指标模型，将卷积滤波器视为指标向量，并提出一种基于奇异值分解（SVD）的方法，借助广义一阶 Stein 公式准确学习卷积滤波器。理论分析表明该估计达到最优收敛速度，与已知链接函数的广义线性模型相当。大量模拟和真实数据实验显示该方法优于 Adam 等流行深度学习算法，且可扩展至非线性降维与表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN 中的卷积操作是特征提取的核心，但卷积滤波器的学习长期以来依赖以梯度下降为基础的深度学习优化方法（如 Adam），缺乏严格的统计理论支撑和可解释性。作者希望从统计学角度出发，利用经典 Stein 公式为卷积滤波器估计提供一种有理论保证且高效的新途径。

Method: 首先将 CNN 形式化为具有矩阵输入的广义指标模型，其中卷积滤波器被视为待估计的指标向量。基于广义一阶 Stein 公式，提出一种基于 SVD 的方法来精确学习卷积滤波器。该方法利用矩阵值输入的结构特性，通过奇异值分解提取滤波器方向信息。

Result: 理论分析证明所提出的估计量达到最优收敛速度，与链接函数已知的广义线性模型性能相当。模拟研究和真实数据实验均表明该方法的估计性能优于 Adam 等主流深度学习优化算法。此外，该方法还适用于非线性降维任务，展现出表示学习的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种从统计学视角理解并解决 CNN 卷积滤波器学习问题的新框架，兼具理论保障与实证优势。方法不仅限于滤波器估计，还拓展至非线性降维和表示学习，为深度学习与统计估计之间架起了桥梁，有望启发更多兼具可解释性与性能的模型设计。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have undoubtedly revolutionized image data analysis and the field of computer vision. As the cornerstone of CNNs, the convolution operation enables the networks to extract abstract features and uncover hidden relationships in the image data. This paper considers the problem of estimating convolution filters from a statistical perspective using a classical tool --- Stein's formula. We first formulate CNNs into a general index model with matrix-valued input, where convolution filters can be viewed as index vectors. Furthermore, we propose a novel singular value decomposition (SVD) based approach to accurately learn the convolution filters based on a generalized version of the first-order Stein's formula. Theoretical analysis suggests that our estimation achieves an optimal convergence rate, comparable to that of generalized linear models where the link function is known. Extensive simulation studies and real data analyses demonstrate that our approach outperforms popular deep learning algorithms, such as Adam. Notably, our method extends beyond filter estimation and can be applied to nonlinear dimension reduction, providing a viable pathway for representation learning.

</details>

### [130] [A greedy nearest-neighbor approach to quantify site revisitation: comparing two sympatric raven species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01858)
*Bar Ashkenazi,Miguel de Guinea,Michael Assaf,Ran Nathan*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种贪心最近邻聚类方法（含局部重分配和基于多边形的停留地构建），从异质采样频率的GPS追踪数据中生成带时间戳的到访序列，并将其应用于死海地区两种同域乌鸦（扇尾乌鸦和棕颈乌鸦）的九年追踪数据。基于访序序列，使用非马尔可夫个体移动模型（IMM）量化递归模式，揭示两种物种在站点探索（β）与偏好返回（α）上的显著差异，并通过熵分析进一步区分两种物种的空间利用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动生态学中的一个核心挑战是如何从原始追踪数据中描述具有生态意义的停留地（residence sites），因为采样频率具有异质性且站点边界不确定。现有方法难以稳健地从数据中提取站点访问序列，进而限制了对动物空间利用策略（尤其是记忆驱动的递归行为）的量化与比较。

Method: 1）提出一种贪心最近邻聚类方法，结合局部重分配和基于多边形的站点构建，生成每个被追踪个体带时间戳的站点访问序列；2）将这些访问序列应用于非马尔可夫个体移动模型（IMM），量化新站点探索β与偏好返回α之间的平衡；3）结合熵分析（站点熵与站点间转移的条件熵）进一步比较两种密切相关的同域乌鸦物种的空间利用特征。

Result: 推断的移动动力学与IMM预测一致，并揭示了明确的种间差异：棕颈乌鸦以更高的速率持续发现新站点（β较低），而扇尾乌鸦在前列排名站点中的访问集中度更高（更高的偏好返回）。熵分析进一步分离了两个物种：棕颈乌鸦表现出更高的站点熵以及更高的站点间转移条件熵。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个稳健的描述种间移动策略差异的框架，可用于从GPS数据中推断动物的记忆效应，并且揭示了两种密切相关的同域物种在空间利用策略上的显著分化。

Abstract: A central challenge in movement ecology is to describe ecologically meaningful residence sites from raw tracking data due to heterogeneous sampling frequency and uncertain site boundaries. Here, we develop a greedy nearest-neighbor clustering approach with local reassignment and polygon-based site construction that generates timestamped sequences of site visits for each tracked individual, and apply it to nine years of GPS data from two sympatric raven species in the Dead Sea region---the Fan-tailed raven (\emph{Corvus rhipidurus}) and the Brown-necked raven (\emph{C. ruficollis}). Using the resulting visitation sequences, we quantify recursion patterns using the non-Markovian individual mobility model (IMM), which captures the balance between novel-site discovery $β$ and preferential return $α$. The inferred dynamics are consistent with IMM predictions and reveal clear interspecific differences: Brown-necked ravens continue to discover new sites at a higher rate (lower $β$), whereas Fan-tailed ravens show a steeper concentration of visits among top-ranked sites. Entropy analyses further separate the species, with Brown-necked ravens exhibiting higher site entropy and higher conditional entropy of site-to-site transitions. Together, these results provide a robust framework for describing interspecific differences in movement strategies that can be used to infer memory from GPS data and suggest distinct space-use strategies in two closely related sympatric species.

</details>


### [131] [Mutation--selection balance on an infinite trait space: confinement, drift and equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02086)
*Phil. Pollett*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个在可数无穷性状空间上同时包含突变、选择和密度依赖调控的性状结构种群模型。利用具有可数多类型的密度依赖马尔可夫种群过程的结果，将底层连续时间马尔可夫链近似为无穷非线性微分方程系统，并分别对"有界死亡率"与"约束性死亡率"两种情形分析了系统的平衡态结构、特征值及唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生态与进化生物学中，理解突变、选择和密度制约如何共同决定种群的性状分布是核心问题。此前针对有限性状空间的分析无法充分捕捉无限性状空间中的生物学现象（如连续突变流导致的性状持续迁移）。本文旨在为无限性状空间上的确定性近似与平衡态存在性提供严格的数学框架。

Method: 首先将个体水平的过程建模为可数无穷类型空间上的连续时间密度依赖马尔可夫链，利用密度依赖种群过程的结果导出确定性的无穷维非线性常微分方程系统。随后建立解的存在性与正不变性。关键分析工具是将算子L=D^{-1}P（P为突变算子、D为死亡算子）与Kreĭn-Rutman紧正算子理论相结合，证明L具有代数简单的严格正主特征值，并据此推导非零平衡态存在的阈值条件。

Result: 研究表明有界死亡率与约束性死亡率之间存在根本性差别：当死亡率有界时，突变可不断将质量输送通过性状空间，稳态性状分布可能不存在；当死亡率随性状指标绝对值的增大而无界增长时，算子L=D^{-1}P为紧算子，具有代数简单的严格正主特征值。在约束性死亡率情形下平衡态唯一，且其性状分布由L的主特征向量决定。数值实验证实了理论结果，并展示了两种死亡率情形下截然不同的行为。

Conclusion: 本文给出了可数无穷性状空间上进化种群模型的严密确定性近似与平衡态分析框架，揭示了死亡率轮廓的有界性对系统长期行为的决定性影响，为理解无限性状空间中进化动态的数学结构提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We study a trait-structured population model incorporating mutation, selection and density-dependent regulation on a countably infinite trait space. The underlying stochastic process is a continuous-time Markov chain in which individuals reproduce at a trait-independent rate, offspring traits are determined by a mutation kernel on $\mathbb Z$, mortality depends on trait, and births are progressively suppressed as the population approaches a fixed population ceiling. Using results for density-dependent Markov population processes with countably many types, we derive a deterministic approximation in the form of an infinite system of nonlinear differential equations.
  We establish existence and positive invariance of solutions, and investigate the equilibrium structure of the deterministic system. A fundamental distinction emerges between bounded and confining mortality profiles. When mortality remains bounded, mutation may continually transport mass through the trait space and a stationary trait distribution need not exist. In contrast, when mortality increases without bound as the absolute value of the trait index becomes large, the operator $L=D^{-1}P$, where $P$ and $D$ govern mutation and mortality, is compact. By combining compactness with Kreĭn-Rutman theory for compact positive operators on Banach lattices, we show that $L$ has an algebraically simple principal eigenvalue with a strictly positive eigenvector, and we derive a threshold condition for the existence of a non-zero equilibrium. In this regime the equilibrium is unique, and its trait distribution is determined by the principal eigenvector of $L$. Numerical experiments support the theoretical results and illustrate the contrasting behaviours associated with bounded and confining mortality profiles.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond species area curves: a theoretical approach to the relationship between diversity and area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02365)
*Hwai-Ray Tung,Simon A Levin*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文使用空间中性模型研究不同多样性指数（如Simpson指数和Shannon指数）对应的有效物种数与面积之间的关系（即Hill数-面积曲线），通过理论和模拟证明：当面积足够大时，有效物种数与面积呈线性关系；而当面积较小时，该关系类似于幂律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物种-面积曲线只关注物种数量，忽略了每个物种的种群大小信息。为了更全面地理解生物多样性并指导保护工作，需要考察包含丰度信息的多样性指数（有效物种数）随面积的变化规律。

Method: 采用先前已用于研究物种-面积曲线的空间中性模型，结合理论推导和数值模拟两种手段，分析不同阶数的Hill数（如Simpson指数和Shannon指数对应的有效物种数）与面积之间的关系。

Result: 发现在面积足够大时，有效物种数与面积呈线性关系；在面积较小时，其关系近似呈幂律形式。这一结论跨越不同的多样性指数均成立。

Conclusion: 有效物种数-面积关系在不同面积尺度下表现出不同的标度行为（小面积下为幂律，大面积下为线性），这为将多样性指数纳入空间尺度分析提供了理论基础，丰富了物种-面积关系框架下的生物多样性理解。

Abstract: Species area curves, which describe the number of species present as a function of area, have long been used to understand biodiversity and inform conservation efforts. While understanding the number of species is important, it leaves out information about the population sizes of each species. In this work, we examine the relationship between the effective number of species from different diversity indices, like Simpson's index and the Shannon index, and area. These effective number of species are also referred to as Hill numbers. Using a spatial and neutral model that has previously been used to understand species area curves, we show through a combination of theory and simulations that the relationship between the effective number of species and area is linear when the area is sufficiently large and resembles a power law for smaller areas.

</details>


### [133] [An adaptive time-tree transition kernel for Bayesian phylogenetic inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02445)
*Marius Brusselmans,Guy Baele,Samuel L. Hong,Jiansi Gao,Marc A. Suchard,Andrew Rambaut,Luiz Max Carvalho*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为subTreeLeap（STL）的新型自适应树转移核，通过在系统发育树上沿patristic距离路径行走并根据可调半径参数进行修改，来提高贝叶斯系统发育推断中树拓扑空间探索的效率，从而加快MCMC收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯系统发育和系统动力学分析因复杂模型与大规模基因组数据的结合而非常耗时，即使使用高性能计算硬件，burn-in阶段仍可能长达数天甚至数周。树拓扑提议（tree topology proposal）探索树空间的效率是影响贝叶斯系统发育推断性能的关键瓶颈之一，现有标准核在该方面效率不足。

Method: 提出一种新颖的自适应树转移提议核STL，其核心思想是沿系统发育树上的patristic距离路径进行"跳跃"式修改，并通过一个可调的半径参数来控制跳跃范围。STL是一种通用提议，既可用于当代序列数据，也可用于时间校准数据（后者因其尊重时间先序约束而尤其适用）。作者通过将STL与标准树转移核在经验数据上的长时间"黄金运行"（golden runs）结果进行对比，评估其对后验树空间探索收敛性和统计混合的影响。

Result: 实验发现STL能够成功探索与标准核相同的后验树空间，但在多数情况下以更高效的方式进行——即在保持相同后验分布的前提下实现了更快的收敛和更好的混合性能。

Conclusion: STL作为树拓扑提议在贝叶斯系统发育推断中具有显著的性能优势，尤其适用于时间校准数据。论文还讨论了STL的局限性及未来可能的改进方向，有望进一步大幅提升贝叶斯系统发育推断的计算速度。

Abstract: Bayesian phylogenetic and phylodynamic analyses can be very time-consuming, owing to the combination of complex models that are used to estimate key parameters from increasingly large genomic data sets and their associated metadata. The use of high-performance computer hardware can -- to a certain extent -- alleviate the computational burden and markedly decrease the time to results. Still, even converging to the posterior can be a lengthy endeavour, with the burn-in aspect of such analyses potentially taking days or even weeks for large data sets. One of the key aspects that hampers performance in Bayesian phylogenetic inference is the efficiency with which tree topology proposals explore tree space. We here propose a novel adaptive tree transition kernel, which we call `subTreeLeap' (STL), which involves modifying the phylogeny by walking along patristic distance paths in the tree according to an adaptable radius parameter. STL is a general proposal, which can be used with contemporaneous or time-calibrated sequence data, being particularly suited to the latter due to respecting temporal precedence constraints. We carefully assess its impact on convergence and statistical mixing of the exploration of posterior tree space, by comparison to replicate ``golden runs'' obtained from lengthy analyses of empirical data under standard tree transition kernels. We find that STL successfully explores the same posterior tree space as standard kernels, but often does so in a more efficient manner. We discuss limitations as well as future potential improvements to STL that could substantially increase the speed at which Bayesian phylogenetic inferences are obtained.

</details>
